veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
2, ciṅkārāvēlar teru
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi -600 017
to.pē: 2433 9030

moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vitti ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. ati muḷikkac ceytavar paritimāl kaliñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟimaliyām maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkiḷi nili nāṭṭiya maliyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavi eḻutiyavi aṉittum meymmiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭit tam āyvu matukiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuriyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uriyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavi alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpiyāka avaṟṟi yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉi akara niral tokiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kāliyil, tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tamiḻ maṇṇiyum tamiḻ iṉattiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uriyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉi vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkarutti yāṉ urikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷi amittār.
tēvanēyar paṭippukaḷ aṉittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷit tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷiyum kaṭṭurikaḷiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉivar kikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya niliyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpati nilip paṭuttum vakiyil avarkkup paṭiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumiyarum toṇṭarumāvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!							iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!						    irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



patippuri
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatit tam paṉmoḻip pulamiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatit tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻarkaḷukkuk kiṭitta viraccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷi veḷiyiṭu vatil perumip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlatti vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

tamiḻ vaḷarcci
i. urinaṭi 
kiḷaviyākkam
1. coṟṟirivu muṟikaḷ
kiḷaviyiṉ iyalviḷakkam : mūvakikkiḷavi - ōreḻuttu, īreḻuttu, palaveḻuttu.
kiḷavi eṉpatu nālvaki ilakkaṇaviyaṟ colliṟkum potuvāṉa col. kiḷattal collutal. kiḷappatu kiḷavi.
māntaṉ nākarikam vaḷara vaḷarak karuttukkaḷ palkukiṉṟaṉa. aṭippaṭik karuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ kiḷikkumpōtu, muṉṉavaṟṟik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ palvēṟu vakiyil tiripaṭikiṉṟaṉa. allākkāl palaporuḷoru collum (Polysemy) pulacolloru vaṭivum (Homonymy) mikkup poruḷ mayakku ēṟpaṭum. atit taṭukkac col vaṭivi māṟṟa vēṇṭiyuḷatu. cuḷ eṉṉum aṭi poruntaṟ poruḷiṟ ceḷ eṉṟu tiriyāviṭiṉ cuṭutaliyum uṟittali yum kuṟikkuñ colloṭu ati mayakka nērum. ceḷ eṉṉum vaṭiviṉiṉṟē ceṇṭu, ceṇṭi, ceṭi, ceṇṇu, ceru, ceṟu, ceṟi, ceṟṟi, ceḻi, cēr mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa. nuḷ eṉpatu neruṅkaṟporuḷil naḷ eṉṟu tiriyāviṭiṉ, kiḷḷu talik kuṟikkum colloṭu ati mayakka nērum. mēlum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa karuttukkaṭku vevvēṟu collum col vaṭivum vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, vērccollum aṭiccollum palvēṟu tiripukoṇṭā loḻiya moḻivaḷarccikkiṭa milli.
(1) aṟuvakittiripu
e.ṭu.
valittal 	:	ūṅku - ūkku, kuṇṭu - kuṇṭamkuṭṭam; 
		nantu - natti, kuṟiñci - kuṟicci.
melittal	:	oppu - ompu, kuttu - kuntu. pōkku - pōṅku.
nīṭṭal 	: 	uṇ-ūṇ, kuṭa-kuṭā, nakarakam-nākarikam.

kuṟukkal 	: 	āṅku-aṅku, tēvu-teyvam, vaṇaṅku- vāṅku- vaṅku-
		vaṅki=vaḷintakatti,  neḷivaḷiyal.
tokuttal 	: 	turutti - tutti, peṭṭi - peṭi, 
			veymmi - vemmi, vēyntōṉ -
			vēntaṉ.
virittal 		: 	paravar - paratavar, māṭam-māṭakam.
(2) mukkuṟi

			= celuttutal.

kaṭikkuṟi 	: 	cāy-cā, nal-na.
(3) mummiki

			metu-mettu.
kaṭimiki 		: 	tirum-tirumpu, kaṭi-kaṭici.
tokuttalum virittalum pakukiḷavikkum, iṭik kuṟiyum iṭimiki yum pakākkiḷavikkum, uriyaveṉa vēṟupāṭaṟika. āyiṉum ivvēṟupāṭu mutaṟkālac coṉṉilikku ēṟkātu.

mōṉittiripu

uyirttiripukaḷellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, u-o, u-a, u-i eṉṉum mūṉṟum palateṉcoṟkaḷiṉ mūlaṅkāṇavum, teṉ collā vaṭacollā eṉṉum aiyattiṟkiṭamāṉa cila coṟkaḷit teṉcolleṉṟu tuṇiyavum, peruntuṇiyāyiruttalāl, mika mutaṉmi vāyntaṉavākum. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē, muṉṉit tiripu, aḷḷittiripu. piṉṉittiripu eṉa aḻikkalām. muṉṉittiripum mōṉittiripum oṉṟē.

palvaki īṟukaḷ
poruḷ māṟumpōtu collum māṟavēṇṭumeṉpatu, collākka aṭippaṭi neṟimuṟi, allākkāl, poruḷ atuvō ituvō eṉa mayaṅka nērum. maṇ eṉṉuñ col maṇalik kuṟittaṟku allīṟu peṟṟu maṇal eṉṟāṉamiyum, kampu eṉṉuñ col, marakkōlik kuṟiyātu kaṉiya (ulōka)k kōlik kuṟittaṟku ikara vīṟu peṟṟuk kampiyeṉ ṟāṉamiyum, kāṇka. pakkattiyum iṭuppiyum kuṟikkum maruṅku eṉṉum col, maruṅkal eṉa ullīṟu peṟṟē taṉippaṭa iṭuppik kuṟittatu.
mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya īṟukaḷ, cuṭṭukkaḷum cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷumākavēyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ, mutaṟkālattil peyareccamāka maṭṭumaṉṟip peyarākavum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. M = mJ (bg.), anta (pe.e.) ikkālattum atu pōtu eṉṟu, cuṭṭup peyar peyareccamāy vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
uyirolikaḷit taṉit tolikkum pōtu, kuṟiliṉum neṭilē iḷañciṟārkkum muntiyal māntarkkum eḷitām. kuṟili viṭṭolikkum pōtu mūccuppi muyaṟci mikku vēṇṭiyirut taliṉ, kīḻvakuppu māṇavar kuṟil neṭil vēṟupāṭiṉṟiyē uyireḻuttukkaḷi olittup pōtal kāṇka. itaṉāl uyireḻuttukkaḷ mutalil neṭilākavē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu uyttuṇarappaṭum.
a i u am mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu  (31)
eṉṟu kūṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār, caṟṟup piṉpu,
neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōrēḻut torumoḻi  (43)
kuṟṟeḻut tintum moḻiniṟi pilavē  (44)
eṉṟu ati orumaruṅku maṟuttiruppatum, viṉāveḻuttuk kaḷik kuṟikkumiṭattu.
ā ē o am mūṉṟum viṉāa  (32)
eṉa ekaratti viṭṭiruppatum, tolkāppiyam neṭukalum āyiṭi eṉṉum toṭarē payiṉṟu varuvatum, āṇṭu īṇṭu eṉṉum coṟkaṭkuk kuṟukiya vaṭiviṉmiyum, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭku iṉakkuṟil pintit tōṉṟi yuḷḷamiyum, itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷākum.
cuṭṭīṟukaḷ
ā-uṇā, nilā, irā, viḷā, kaḷā, nuṇā, kaṭā, miḻā.
ī-kurī (kurīi = kuruvi).
ū - koḷ - koḷum - koḷumū - koṇmū (mukil).
ciṟu paṟavi (kuruvi) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum kurī eṉṉum collip piṟkālattār kurīi eṉa aḷapeṭiyākki viṭṭaṉar.
koṇmū - kaṭalnīrik koḷḷum mukil. koḷḷutal = mukattal. koḷumutal = mukattal.
āvīṟu muṟiyē a, avu eṉat tiriyum,
e.ṭu. nilā - nilavu, irā-ira-iravu, kaḷā-kaḷa-kaḷavu. avu īṟu avam eṉṟum tiriyum.
e.ṭu. arā - ara - aravu - aravam, kaḷā - kaḷa - kaḷavu - kaḷavam.
mutaṟkālattil neṭilākavēyirunta muccuṭṭukkaḷum piṟkālattil kuṟilākavum māṟiṉa.
e.ṭu.	ā - a, ātu - atu, āṅku - aṅku.
		ī - i, ītu - itu, īṅku - iṅku.
		ū - u, (ūtu) - utu, ū - uṅku.
eḻuttukkaḷ taṉiniliyiṉṟip puṇarnilippaṭṭuc coṟ kaḷākum pōtu, neṭil vaṭiviṉuṅ kuṟilvaṭivē palukka (uccarikka) eḷitām.
īkāra ūkārac cuṭṭīṟukaḷ ikkālattu iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟil vaṭivukaḷē iṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.

	u - koḻu (kalappik kāṟu), karu (cūl), urumu, varaku.
cila akaravīṟu avu eṉṟu tiriyum.
e.ṭu.	kuḻa - kuḻavu, maḻa - maḻavu.
cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷ
ellā meykaḷuḷḷum iyalpāṉatum eḷitāṉatum makara mātaliṉ, mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu makaramey yīṟṟatē. atu piṉṉarp piṟa meyyīṟṟatākat tirintum, iṟutiyilum iṭiyilum vēṟeḻuttup peṟṟu virintum, ēṉiyīṟu kaḷōṭu kūṭiyum, palvēṟu taṉiyīṟukaḷiyum kūṭṭīṟukaḷiyum piṟappittuḷḷatu.
am ikkālattil taṉiyīṟu; mutaṟkālattil kūṭṭīṟu (a+m)
am - aṉ - al. e.ṭu. tiṟam - tiṟaṉ - tiṟal.
uyirum uyir meyyumākiya īṟukaḷellām coṟkaḷum coṟ citivukaḷumē yātaliṉ, attaki īṟu peṟṟa collellām mutaṟkālattil iru collākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
e.ṭu. uṇā (uṇ + ā) = uṇṇum atu, uṇṇum poruḷ.

aku, acu, aṭu, atu, apu, avu, aṟu, akku, accu, aṭṭu, attu, appu, aṟṟu, aṇ, am, ar, al, aḻ, aḷ, aṉ eṉṉum īṟukaḷum, ivaṟṟiṟkotta ikaravukara mutal vaṭivukaḷumē, iṉṟu muṟiyē ku, cu, ṭu, tu, pu, vu, ṟu, kku, ccu, ṭṭu, ttu, ppu, ṟṟu, ṇ, m, r, l, ḻ, ḷ, ṉ eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iviyellām uṇmiyiṟ cuṭṭaṭiyiṉavākum.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aṉ, aḷ, ar, atu, avi, a eṉpaṉa pālīṟukaḷalla; aṇ, aḻ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟu iyalpāṉa potuvīṟukaḷē.
cila iṭappeyarkaḷ cilaviṭattuc cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷ pōl tōṉṟum. avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
e.ṭu.	cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu : akam - kaḻakam eṉpatiṟpōl oru 
					  peyarīṟu (aku + am).
		iṭappeyar 	:	akam = maṉi, iṭam, kallakam = 
					kalviccāli (kaṟkumiṭam), kaṉ 
					maṉi, kalluḷḷa iṭam.
		cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu	:	iṭam-ōr īṟu. v.L.: kaṭṭiṭam =  maṉi
		iṭappeyar	:	iṭam-oru peyarccol.
				e.ṭu. iruppiṭam = irukkumiṭam.
				kaṭṭiṭam = kaṭṭukiṉṟa iṭam.
ōr īṟu pala vakiyil tōṉṟalām.
e.ṭu.	avu    :  irā-ira-iravu, cel-celavu.
	aṉ   :  am-aṉ, al-aṉ.
	antu :  am-antu, atu-antu, attu-antu.
pala coṟkaḷil lakaram takaramākat tirivatālum, mel eṉṉuñ col metu eṉṟu tirintiruppatālum atu eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu al eṉpataṉ tiripākavē koḷḷap paṭṭatu. aḵtu eṉpatu atu eṉat tokkateṉṟu koḷvataṟ kiṭamuṇṭēṉum, atu eṉat tokkateṉṟu koḷvataṟkiṭamuṇṭēṉum, atu eṉpataṉ vali iraṭṭiya vaṭivamāṉa attu eṉpatu paḵtu eṉpa tōṭoppa āytamiṭiyiṭṭa maṟuvaṭivu koṇṭateṉpatu poruttamām. metu metteṉṟāyatu pōl atu aḵteṉṟāyatu; patu patteṉṟāyatu. āytatti yaṭutta takaram, aḵṟiṇi, kaḵṟītu, paḵṟi, paḵṟuḷi eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōl ṟakaramākat tirivatē puṇarcciyilakkaṇa mātalāl aḵtu, paḵtu, eṉpavaṟṟiluḷḷa āytam iyalpāṉa iṭiyeḻuttaṉ ṟeṉṟum, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa iṭikkālat tiripeṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
collīṟukaḷ tiriyumpōtu, meli valiyākalām, vali meliyākalām.
e.ṭu. nīṅku-nīkku, pōkku-pōṅku.
palvakik kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ
accam, virivu, ōci, oḷi, vaṇṇam, acivu, ūṟu, mutaliya palvēṟu poruḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ, ōroḻuṅku paṭṭa īṟukaḷik koṇṭirā viṭiṉum, makkaḷ karuttit teḷivāyp pulappaṭuttaṟku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa 
vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.
accakkuṟippu - kapīr. tiṭukku, tuṇukku, verukku.
virivukkuṟippu - avakku, kupīl-kupīr, carēl-carēr, tiṭum, 
tiṭīr, paṭakku, pucukku, pocukku, vicukku, viruṭṭu, veṭukku.
ōcikkuṟippu - kaṇīr, kiṟiccu, kīccu, caḷār, taṭāl - taṭār, turuṭṭu, puḷiccu, paṭār, molōr.
oḷikkuṟippu - paḷiccu, paḷīr, miṉukku.
vaṇṇakkuṟippu - civīr, cevēr, veḷēl - veḷēr.
acivukkuṟippu - kiṇukku, kiṇukkaṭṭi, cavakku, totukkup putukku.
ūṟṟukkuṟippu - kaṭukku, cuṟukku, cuḷīr, parapara, potukku, noḷukku, metukku.
olikkuṟippillāta coṟkaḷellām iyaṟcoṟkaḷalla; īṟu peṟṟa tiricoṟkaḷē.
e.ṭu. miṉ-miṉukku, melu-melukku, paḷ-paḷiccu, veḷ-veḷēr.
valiyiraṭṭal
e.ṭu. paku - pakkam, pēcu - pēccu, neṭu - neṭṭi, karutu - karuttu, kuṟu - kuṟṟam, kuṟṟi, neṟu neṟṟi.
eḻuttiraṭṭal (Germination or Doubling of Consonants) eṉṉum collamiti, āriya moḻik kuṭumpak kiḷikaḷum airōppāviṉ vaṭamēlik kōṭiyiluḷḷatum, āṅkilatti uḷḷiṭṭatumāṉa, tiyūttāṉiyattiṟkē ciṟappāṉateṉṟu (i)riccārṭu māricu kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
taṉikkuṟiliyaṭutta mey uyirōṭu puṇarumpōtu iraṭṭuvatu, tamiḻukkum āṅkilattiṟkum potuvām.
e.ṭu. tamiḻ - kaṇ + i = kaṇṇi, koḷ + ai = koḷḷi, il + am = illam, iṉ + um = iṉṉum.
E. thin + er = thinner, sit + ing = sitting.
palvakit tiri coṟkaḷ
viḷ
viḷ - viḷa, viḷattu, viḷampu.
viḷ - viṭu - viṭar, viṭi, viṭuti, viṭal.
viḷ - vir - viri, virivu, viral, virippu.
viḷ - vil - viyaṉ. (l)
viḷar - viḷā, viḷa, viḷavu, viḷavam, viḷatti
viḷ - viṇ - vīṇ - vīṇpu - vīmpu, vīṇi

pōli (Interchange of Letters)
oru collil, ōriṭattil, oru taṉiyeḻuttiṟku allatu iṇiyeḻuttiṟkup pakaramāka (patilāka) muṟiyē maṟṟoru taṉiyeḻuttu allatu iṇiyeḻuttu vantu, poruḷ māṟā tiruppatu pōliyām. pōliruppatu pōli. pōlutal - ottal. iṭam nōkki, mutaṟpōli, iṭip pōli, kaṭip pōli eṉap pōli mūvakippaṭum.
e.ṭu.	     mutaṟpōli 	: nāyiṟu - ñāyiṟu, maṭali - vaṭali;
		iṭippōli 	: kuṭavaṉ - kucavaṉ - kuyavaṉ;
			neyavu	: necavu, aracu - aricu, nīntu - nīñcu;
		kaṭippōli	: kaṭam - kaṭaṉ, kūtal - kūtar.
poruḷ māṟātirukkum pōliyāl, ceyyuḷil etuki yamippiṟkaṉṟi moḻivaḷarccikkiṭamilli. āyiṉ pōli muṟiyit taḻuvip poruḷ vēṟupaṭum coṟkaḷ moḻiyi mika vaḷampaṭuttum.
e.ṭu. : tarukku - cerukku, paku-vaku, mīṟu-vīṟu, katali-kacaḷi, kuḻal- kuṭal, paḻam-payam-payaṉ, vāyil-vācal, motti-mocci, iṭam-iṭaṉ, nāḷi-nāḻi, nāḷi-nāṭi.
ilakkaṇappōli (Metathesls)
oru taṉiccolliṉ eḻuttukkaḷum oru kūṭṭuc colliṉ uṟuppukkaḷ allatu uṟuppuccoṟkaḷum, muṉpiṉṉāka muṟi māṟi variṉ ilakkaṇap pōli eṉappaṭum.
e.ṭu. 	taṉiccol 	alari-araḷi,	koppuḷam -
		pokkuḷam,  	cati-taci,  	ñimiṟu-miñiṟu
		viciṟi-civiṟi.
kūṭṭuccol
ilvāy - vāyil, ilmuṉṟu - mūṉṟil - muṟṟam, katuvāli - kavutāri tāṉimuṉ - muṉṟāṉi.
mūvakic coṟkiṭakki
taṉiccol, kūṭṭuccol, toṭarccol eṉac coṟkiṭakki mūvakippaṭum. avi muṟiyē oru collum iru collum iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pala collumākum.
e.ṭu. nilam, taṇṇīr, ceṅkāl nāri, vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ.
kūṭṭuccol vakikaḷ
1. iraṭṭikkiḷavi	e.ṭu.	kalakala, viruviru
2. iraṭṭitta col	e.ṭu.	cekkac cevēr, ciṉṉañciṟu
3. aṭukkut toṭar	e.ṭu.	palapala, tirumpat tirumpa
4. mīmicic col	e.ṭu.	kaṉṟukkuṭṭi, ōṅkiyuyarnta
5. iru peyaroṭṭu	e.ṭu	kumarimali, makkaṭcelvam
6. mikavuccol	e.ṭu.	āṇmakaṉ, peṇ peṇṭāṭṭi
7. muraṇpaṭucol	e.ṭu.	peṇmakaḷ, peṭṭip pacaṉkaḷ
8. iṇimoḻi	e.ṭu.	kāykaṟi, vāḻvu tāḻvu
9. aṭipeṟṟa col	e.ṭu.	naṉcey, talikkaḻakam
10. kiḷaviyam
	(eccattoṭar)	e.ṭu.	āṭci māṟi, intiyoḻiyiṉ
11. toṭariyam 	e.ṭu.	tamiḻ vāḻum, tamiḻaṉ uyarvāṉ (muṟṟuttoṭar)
aṭukkuttoṭar oṟṟiṭi mikkum tirintum tokkum varum.
e.ṭu. palappala, paṟpala, meṉmēlum, nannāṉku, vevvēṟu, cila coṟkaḷ taṉiccoṟkum kūṭṭuccoṟkum potuvāyirukkum.
oru poruḷ tantu oru collāyum iruporuḷ tantu iru collāyum iruppatu potuccol.
aḷittēṉ = tantēṉ (orucol), vaṇṭuttēṉ (irucol).
ceyvōṉ = purivāṉ (orucol), ceyaṟki maḻi (irucol).
toṭarccol vakikaḷ
1. aṭukkukkiḷavi	e.ṭu.	kuṭukuṭukuṭu
2. mīmiciccol	e.ṭu.	mutupaḻantoṉ (moḻi)
3. aṭukkuttoṭar	e.ṭu.	tīttīttī
4. mikavuccol	e.ṭu.	ariñāṇkayiṟu, peṇ 
				piḷḷip piḷḷi
5. aṭipeṟṟacol	e.ṭu.	tūṅkeyileṟinta, toṭittōṭ
				cempiyaṉ, maturik 
				kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīrar
6. kiḷaviyam	e.ṭu.		tamiḻaṉ urimipeṟa vēṇṭiṉ
7. toṭariyam	e.ṭu.		intiya araciyaṟ caṭṭatti 
				māṟṟiyamittal vēṇṭum
poruṭkēṟṟa oliyamippu
potuvāka, mellōcic coṟkaḷ ceyaliṉ meṉmiyiyum  vallōcic coṟkaḷ ceyaliṉ vaṉmiyiyum, uṇarttum.
e.ṭu. kuttutal = catiyuḷḷa iṭattil muṭṭiyāl allatu kattiyāl tākkutal. kuṭṭutal = vaṉmiyāṉa maṇṭiyil muṭṭiyāl tākkutal.
muttutal = mukattōṭu allatu vēṟuṟuppoṭu mukam mellap poruntutal. muṭṭutal = maṇṭiyoṭu maṇṭi iṭittal.
arital = ciṟitāy naṟukkutal. aṟuttal = peritāy naṟukkutal. arivatu arivāḷ; aṟuppatu aṟuvāḷ.
kaṟittal = kaṉṟu iḷampulli metuvāyk kaṭittal.
kaṭittal = peritāṉa vilaṅku mutirnta payiri valitākak kaṭittal.
caṉṉam = melliyatu. tiṇṇam = tiraṇṭatu.
kuñcu = mellitāṉa iḷampaṟavi. kuccu = vallitāṉa ciṟukōl.
tiṇṭu = melliya pañcaṇi. tiṭṭu = vaṉṉilamāṉa ciṟumēṭu.
karuppu = iyalpāṉa karuniṟam. kaṟuppu = ciṉattāl allatu mutircciyāl nērum iruṇṭa niṟam.
marūu
cila taṉiccoṟkaḷum kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷum toṭarc coṟkaḷum vaḻakkāṟṟil maruvi, atāvatu ilakkaṇiyarāl oppuk koḷḷap peṟum vakiyil kuṟukiyum tirintum,  vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.
taṉiccol
elumpu - eṉpu, teṉku - teṅku, naṭuvaṇ - nāppaṇ, nerutal, nerunaṟṟu - nēṟṟu, pakuti - pāti, peyar - pēr, perumakaṉ - perumāṉ - pemmāṉ, pirāṉ; poḻutu - pōḻtu, pōtu, yāṉi - āṉi, viyar - vēr.
kūṭṭuccol
ariyuḻakku - āḻākku, koṇṭuvā - koṇṭā - koṇā - koṇar, cumiyaṭi - cumaṭu - cummāṭu, cōḻa nāṭu - cōṇāṭu, teṅkaṅkāy - tēṅkāy, pukaviṭu - pukaṭṭu - pōṭṭu - puḻikki - pūḻkki - pūṭki, pōkaviṭu - pōkaṭu - pōṭu, variyāṭu - varuṭi, māryāppu - mārāppu, mukakkūṭu - mukkāṭu.
toṭarccol
arumaruntaṉṉa - arumānta - arumanta.
karivalam vanta nallūr - karuvi.
pūṭki, pōṭu eṉpaṉapōṉṟa coṟkaḷ, mūla niliyil kūṭṭuc collāyiṉum, marūu niliyil oru coṟpōl  niṉṟu  orēporuḷ tarutalāl, taṉic collākavē koḷḷappaṭum.

tamiḻakattil piṟappōṭu toṭarpuṭiya āriyakkulap piriviṉi ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, poṉṉiṟa uyirikaḷ pārppāra eṉṟum, karuniṟa vuyirikaḷ paṟi eṉṟum, aṭi peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. pārppāra nākam, paṟi nākam.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpik kāṭṭum aṭimoḻi, apporuḷkaḷic ciṟappākak kiyāḷum vakuppāriṉ peyarākum.
e.ṭu. kuṟavaṉ pōkaṇi = periya pōkaṇi araca maruttuvam = aracarukkuc ceyyum vilakkūṇillā maruttuvam.
cila karuvippeyarkaḷiṉ aṭimoḻi, akkaruviyāṟ ceyyappaṭum allatu ceppañ ceyyappaṭum poruḷiṉ peyarākum.
e.ṭu. kōṇiyūci = periyavūci.

puṇarcci
irucoṟ puṇarvatu puṇarcci. moḻi potumakkaḷāl ākkap peṟṟatākaliṉ, ataṉ puṇarcciyum avaratu amippē, kavaṉit taṟiyappeṟātu ēṟkeṉavēyirunta coṟkaṭṭu neṟimuṟi kaḷiyē, puṇarcci neṟimoḻikaḷ (vitikaḷ) eṉa eṭuttuk kūṟiṉar ilakkaṇiyar, iyalpup puṇarcci, tiripuppuṇarcci eṉap puṇarcci iru vakippaṭum. oruvaki vēṟupāṭumiṉṟi iyalpā yiruppatu iyalpup puṇarcci, ētēṉumoru vakiyil tirivatu tiripup puṇarcci, tirital vēṟupaṭutal. atu tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal eṉa mūvakippaṭum. muṉṉillāta eḻuttō aciyō  tōṉṟuvatu tōṉṟal; ōreḻuttu maṟṟōreḻuttāka māṟuvatu tirital; muṉṉuḷḷa eḻuttō aciyō maṟivatu keṭutal.
puṇarcciyiṟ putitāyt tōṉṟum aci, iru coṟkaḷi allatu oru colliyum oru col uṟuppiyum cārntu niṉṟu iyippatāl (icippatāl), cāriyi eṉappaṭum.
iyalpuppuṇarcci
cāttaṉ + vantāṉ = cāttaṉ vantāṉ
nalla + piyaṉ = nalla piyaṉ
pāl + iṉitu = pāliṉitu
meyyōṭu uyir cēriṉ uyirmeyyām. itu iyalpē.
vēṟṟumi : 1,8
pe-e. - ka.kā. ni. kā. akaravīṟṟuk kuṟippu.
vi. e. - i. kā.
tiripup puṇarcci
tōṉṟal : (Insertion)
vāḻi + kāy = vāḻikkāy
avari + pantal = avarippantal
kaḷā + paḻam = kaḷāppaḻam, kaḷāmpaḻam
pū + ceṭi = pūcceṭi (pūkkum ceṭi)
... ... ... = pūñceṭi (aḻakiya ceṭi)
paṇatti + koṭu = paṇattik koṭu
ūrukku + pō = ūrukkup pō
vara + col = varaccol
i + nāḷ = innāḷ
veḷ + āṭu = veḷḷāṭu
nāṭu + āṇmi = nāṭṭāṇmi
vēṟṟumi - 2, 4
pe. e. - akara vīṟṟu
iraṭṭal
kuṟilaṭuttamey + uyir kuṟilaṭutta valliṉa meyyēṟiya ukām + uyir
e.ṭu. kuṟu, ciṟu putu, neṭu, uṟu, vēṟṟumi.
ivi eḻuttut tōṉṟal
cekkār + kuṭi = cekkārakkuṭi cāriyiyum eḻuttum
puḷi + paḻam = puḷiyam paḻam
kaṇ + potti = kaṇṇāmpotti cāriyiyum iraṭṭalum
ivi cāriyi tōṉṟal.
tirital (Mutation)
nal + cey = naṉcey
veḷ + kalam = veṇkalam
cem + tāmari = centāmari
vem + nīr = vennīr
vēmpu + ili = vēppili
taṇ + nīr = taṇṇīr
uḷ + nākku = uṇṇākku
kal + tāḻi = kaṟṟāḻi
nal + nilam = naṉṉilam
uḷ + tu = uṇṭu, īṉ + tu = īṟṟu.
ivi tirital,
keṭutal (Omission) or Elision 
makara vīṟṟuppeyar peyarit taḻuval
maram + vēr = maravēr
tolkāppiyam + nūl = tolkāppiya nūl ivi keṭutal.
aṉṟu + kūli = aṟṟikkūli
maṇ + kaṭṭi = maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi
ivi tōṉṟalum tiritalum.
paṭṭiṉam = piḷḷiyār = paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷiyār
nakaram + āṉ = nakarattāṉ
ivi tōṉṟalum keṭutalum
vēmpu + kāy = vēppaṅkāy
paṉi + tōppu = paṉantōppu
ivi tōṉṟalum tiritalum keṭutalum.
marūuppuṇarcci
cila coṟkaḷ olineṟippaṭi oḻuṅkāyp puṇarātu maruvippuṇarum, appuṇarcci marūuppuṇarcciyām. maruvutal neṟi tiṟamputal.
e.ṭu. 	ātaṉ + tā = (āntā) - ānti
		akam + ki = aṅki
		nāḻi + uri = nāḻuri - nāṭuri 
		maka + kaḷ = makkaḷ
uḷḷaṅki (uḷ + aṅki) eṉpatu aṅkiyiṉ naṭuppakuti. itil am eṉpatu cāriyiyaṉṟu.
enti, tanti eṉpaṉa marūuppuṇarcciyalla; ivi em + tā, tam + tā eṉap piriyum, tā eṉpatu tantiyik kuṟikkum peyarccol.
tā + tā = tātā - tāti (tantiyiṉ tanti) - iyalpup puṇarcci
... ... ... = tāttā (tantiyiṉ tanti) - tiripuppuṇarcci.
tāti eṉpatiṟ pōṉṟē, enti, tanti, ānti, pūnti eṉpavaṟṟilum ti eṉpatu tā eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
o.nō.	empi(ṉ)	eṅki evvi emmuṉ enti
			numpi(ṉ)	nuṅki nuvvi nummuṉ nunti
			tampi(ṉ)	taṅki tavvi tammuṉ tanti
empiṉ = emakkup piṉ piṟantāṉ. ki = taṅki (piṅ.). iḷaṅkiḷi eṅki = emakkuc ciṟiyavaḷ. em + avvi = evvi. itil avvi eṉṉum aṉṉiyiṉ peyar aṉṉi pōlum akkiyik kuṟittatu. iccol cila paḻañceyyuṭ kaḷil taṅkiyik kuṟippatu. kātal paṟṟiya marapu vaḻuvamiti yeṉa aṟika. eṉ nanti, eṉ tampi, eṉ taṅki eṉpaṉa vellām vaḻakkup paṟṟiya iṭavaḻuvamitiyē,
emappaṉ 	numappaṉ 	tamappaṉ - takappaṉ
emakki	numakki	tamakki
emiyaṉ	numiyaṉ	tamiyaṉ
kārkālam, vaṭakōṭu, kōvūrkiḻār, nāṭukiḻavōṉ eṉac ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum, kēṭucālam, vēṟukālam, viṟaku taliyaṉ, viṟaku kāṭu, vaṭakari, maḻikālam eṉa ulaka vaḻakkilum, valimikātu vaḻaṅkuvatālum; nāṉku, veṭku eṉṉum tiripu vaṭivukaḷuṭaṉ nālku, velku eṉṉum iyalpu vaṭivukaḷum vaḻaṅki varuvatālum; palku, palporuḷ, cilkāl, valci, valpu eṉac cila coṟkaḷ eṉṟum iyalpākavē yiruppataṉālum; mutaṟkālattil tōṉṟal tirital puṇarcci attuṇik kaṇṭippāyirunta tilliyeṉa uyttuṇaralām.
mūvakip puṇarcciyum oliyicivi maṭṭumaṉṟi, iru poruṭ kiṭippaṭṭa neruṅkiya toṭarpiyum uṇarttutal kāṇka.
avari x tuvari
vāḻi x tāḻi
puṉṉi x teṉṉi
viḷā x palā, taḷā
ōriyalamippu (Uniformity of Words)
orutuṟippaṭṭa coṟkaḷiyellām, mutalō īṟō etukiyō paṟṟi ottavaṭivākkuvatu ōriyalamippām.
oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, toṇṭu (oṉpatu), pattu, nūṟu eṉṉum mutaṟkālattu eṇṇup peyar kaḷellām ukarattil iṟutal kāṇka.
teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku, mēṟku eṉṉum ticippeyarkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē.
pēti, petumpi, maṅki, maṭanti, arivi, terivi, eṉṉum peṇpāṟ paruvap peyarkaḷ aikārattil iṟukiṉṟaṉa.
atō, anta, avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi, ampar, aṅku, avaṇ, atōḷ - atōḷi.
itō, inta, ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivi, impar, iṅku, ivaṇ, itōḷ - itōḷi.
utō, unta, uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvi, umpar, uṅku, uvaṇ, utōḷ - utōḷi.
etō, enta, evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, etu, evi, empar, eṅku, evaṇ, etōḷ - etōḷi.
ittakiya ōriyaloḻuṅkupaṭṭa cuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷ vēṟem moḻiyilum illi. atā, antā, aṉṉā, aṉṉa mutaliya coṟkaṭkum iṅṅaṉamē oṭṭikkoḷka.
oppumi yamippu (Analogy)
oru colliṉ etuki vaṭivil iṉṉoru colli amippatu oppumi yamippām.
etirmaṟic coṟkaḷ :
iyaṟki eṉṉum collamippip piṉpaṟṟi, ceyal eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarōṭu ki yīṟu cērttuc ceyaṟki eṉṟamittatu oppumi yamippē. ēṉiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu :
aṟam, maṟam, iṟappu, piṟappu; iti (putukkolli), muti (paḻaṅkolli); iṇaṅku, piṇaṅku; iṉpu (iṉpam), tuṉpu (tuṉpam); kuṟṟam, naṟṟam; ciṟu, peru; ciṟṟu, peṟṟu (periyatu), ciṉṉañciṟiya peṉṉamperiya; tāy, cēy, teruḷ, maruḷ; nalam; polam; naṉcey, puṉcey; nāṭu, kāṭu; pēraṭi, cīṟaṭi,  nāṭṭam, pāṭṭam; muntu, pintu.
iṉapporuṭ coṟkaḷ;
nāṟṟicik kāṟṟup peyarkaḷuḷ, vāṭi kōṭi eṉpavum koṇṭal teṉṟal eṉpavum oppumi yamippām.
oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ :
koṟṟam, veṟṟam.
toṭarpaṟṟa coṟkaḷ :
viṭi - viṭali, kaṭi - kaṭali, toṭi - toṭali, muṭi - muṭali; ai - (aintu) añcu, pi - pañcu, mi - mañcu, ni - nañcu.  (naṟṟiṇi 353).
nāṉilak karupporuṭ coṟkaḷ
nilittiṇi (tāvaram), iyaṅkutiṇi (caṅkamam) eṉṉum iru vakuppuyirikaḷuḷ, cila pala ovvoru nilattiṟkum ciṟappāka uriyaṉavākum. muntiyal tamiḻar kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻntirunta pōtē, mūviṭac cuṭṭup peyar, viṉāp peyar, muṟippeyar, vaṇṇamum vaṭivum aḷavum cuviyum ūṟum paṟṟiya kuṇappeyar, iṉṟiyamiyāta viṉiccoṟkaḷ mutaliya aṭippaṭit tamiḻc coṟkaḷ amintuviṭṭaṉa. payir pacci, vilaṅku paṟavi, uṇavu vaki, toḻil muṟi, karuvi paṭikkalam, paḻakka vaḻakkam, koḷki kōṭpāṭu, kuṭiyiruppiṭam mutaliya palvēṟu karupporuḷkaḷ paṟṟiya coṟkaḷum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. ataṉpiṉ kuṟiñciyaṭutta mulli nilattiṟkup paraviyapōtu, annilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karupporuḷkaḷ paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ piṟantaṉa. ataṉpiṉ mulliyaṭutta marutanilattiṟkup paraviya pōtu, annilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karup poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ataṉpiṉ, marutattiyaṭutta neytal nilattiṟku maruta nakar mīṉpiṭiyāḷar kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta pōtu, annilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karupporuḷkaḷ paṟṟiya 
coṟkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
kuṟiñciyum mulliyum mutuvēṉilil vaṟaṇṭaniliyē pāli yātaliṉ, annilattup piṟkālat tuṇṭāṉa coṟkaḷellām toḻilum karuviyum paḻakka vaḻakkamum koḷkiyum paṟṟiyaṉavē.
kuṟiñciyaṭuttum mulliyaṭuttum neytalirukkavuṅ kūṭumāyiṉum, kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar kuṟiñciyiṉiṉṟu āṟṟiyoṭṭiyē paṭippaṭiyākap paraviyatākat terivatāl, maruta nilikkup piṉpē neytal ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. mutaṟkāla māntar ceyaṟki nīrnili amikkat 
teriyātirunta kālattil iyaṟki nīrniliyāṉa āṟṟiyē nampiyiruntaṉar. iṟṟit tamiḻakattiṟ pōṉṟē paḻampāṇṭi nāṭāṉa kumarik kaṇṭattilum, perumalittoṭarkaḷ mēl kōṭiyilē iruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu paḵṟuḷiyum kumariyum pōṉṟē āṟṟiyaṭuttē makkaḷ paraviṉ, kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal eṉṟa muṟiyilēyē nilaṅkaḷi aṭintirukka muṭiyum. innilimi iṟṟit tamiḻakattiṟkum ēṟṟatātal kāṇka. muntiyal māntar munnili nākarikattiyum muṟiyē aṭitaṟkēṟṟavāṟu, kuṟiñci mulli marutam ākiya 
munnilamum aṭuttaṭuttiruttal tamiḻnāṭṭiṟpōl vēṟeṅkaṇumilli.
veṟpaṉ, kuṟumpoṟināṭaṉ, ūraṉ, tuṟivaṉ, viṭali eṉṉum aintiṇit taṉiyūrt talivarāṭcikkup piṉ, palavūrk kiḻami pūṇṭa vēḷiriṉ kuṟunila āṭci vantu, ataṉpiṉ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyariṉ perunila āṭci tōṉṟiyapōtu, tamiḻ aintiṇic coṟkaḷum cērnta peruvaḷa moḻiyāyiṟṟu, maruta nilattil nakarkaḷ tōṉṟi nākarikam vaḷarntu palvēṟu toḻilkaḷum kalikaḷum aṟiviyalkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, tamiḻ mēṉmēlum vaḷarntōṅkiṟṟu.
tamiḻ aintiṇi makkaṭkum potuvāyirukkum iḵtoṉṟē, tamiḻarellām ōriṉameṉṟum, toṉṟu toṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭi lēyē vāḻntu varupavareṉṟum, toḻiltaḻuvātu piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyak kulappiriviṉiyāl tūya māperun tamiḻiṉam ciṉṉa piṉṉamākac citivuṇṭateṉṟum, terivikkap pōtiya cāṉṟām.
poruṭṭirivu muṟikaḷ (Semantic Changes)
1. uvamiyāku peyar 
cila peyarkaḷ uvami yaṭippapiyil amintuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. māṅkāy - māṅkāy pōṉṟa neñcāṅkuli (Heart).
takkaṭṭi - takkaṭṭikkāy allatu kaṉipōṉṟa kaṇ paṭṭik kaṭṭi (Sty).
erumi nākku - erumi nākkup pōṉṟa mīṉ.
kiḷimūkku - kiḷimūkku vaṭivāṉa māmpaḻam.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ peyar, paṇpiyākupeyar muṟiyil ap poruḷkaḷiṉ niṟattik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. : cāmpal vāḻi, kākkip piciṉ, pavaḻac cōḷam, kurutikkāntaḷ, poṟkoṉṟi, mayiṟkaḻuttuc cēli.
cila peyarkaḷ uvamit tokiyāy amintuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. ārālmī ṉavari, kōḻikkāl tiyal, pāmpuk kaṟṟāḻi, vuvāl mīṉ.
cila peyarkaḷ uvami yaṭippaṭiyil amintu, teḷivupaṟṟi īṟu peṟṟum tirintum uḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu.	āṉikkompaṉ - oruvakic campānel.
		kāṭikkaṇṇi - kāṭikkaṇ pōṉṟa oruvakic ciṟu 
	tavacam (tāṉiyam), ataṉ payir.
	kutirivāli - kutirivālpōṟ cutirtaḷḷum oru vakic 
	ciṟu tavacap payirum attavacamum.
	nuṇā - nuṇal (maṇal tavaḷi) pōṉṟa kāy kāykkum 
	maravaki.
mūvakit takuti vaḻakku
iṭakkaraṭakkal (Euphemism) maṅkalam (Euphemism) kuḻūuk kuṟi (Coventional terms) eṉṉum mūvakit takuti vaḻakku muṟiyilum, cila coṟkaḷum vaḻakkāṟukaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. 
iṭakkaraṭakkal	oṉṟiṟku iraṇṭiṟkup pōtal, pavvī, 
				mutaṟkuṟi kamalam.
maṅkalam 		koṭittaṭṭutal = pāmpu kaṭittal; 
				perumpiṟitu = cāvu.
kuḻuukkuṟi 		centali = arikkāl,
				karuntali = kāl, taṅkāṉ = ari.
				arumpu = arici.
ōriṉappaṭuttalum (Generalisation) vēṟiṉappaṭuttalum (Discrimination).
ōriṉappaṭuttal	palvēṟu vakipporuḷkaḷi, oru 
				potut taṉmi paṟṟi oru collāṟ 
				kuṟittal ōriṉap paṭuttalām.
e.ṭu. māṭu - peṟṟamum (kāḷiyum āvum) erumiyum.
māṉ - uḻi, pulvāy, navvi, pukar (puḷḷimāṉ), mari, varuṭi, kaṭami, miḻā, kācaṟi, (kattūri) kavari, kutiri mutaliyaṉa.
tumpi - muṉtūmpu (Proboscis) uṭiya yāṉiyum kuḷaviyum.
vilaṅku, paṟavi, maram, mīṉ eṉpaṉa mikavirivāka ōriṉappaṭuttum coṟkaḷ.
perunelli, arinelli, kīḻvāynelli eṉpavaṟṟi nelliyiṉappaṭuttiyatum ōriṉappaṭuttalē.
vēṟiṉappaṭuttal
orē iṉamāṉa allatu otta tōṟṟamuḷḷa poruḷkaḷi, nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭu paṟṟi vevvēṟu collāṟ kuṟittal vēṟiṉappaṭuttalām.
e.ṭu. ili - ili, tāḷ, tōki, ōli, piñcu, kāy.
nilittiṇi - pūñcaṇam, pācam, pāci, kāḷāṉ, pul, pūṇṭu, kōri, payir, taṭṭi, koṭi, tūṟu, ceṭi, maram.
nuḷampu - nuḷḷāṉ, ulaṅku, mutali, iṭaṅkar, aravam.
camitta tavacavuṇavu - cōṟu, kañci, kūḻ, kaḷi.
iṉi, ciṟappuccoṟkaḷaṉṟip potupcoṟkaḷē  vevvēṟaṭi peṟṟup poruḷkaḷi vēṟiṉappaṭuttalummuṇṭu.
e.ṭu. ciṟṟeṟumpu, poṭiyeṟumpu, āyeṟumpu, kuṟuveṟumpu, (cevveṟumpu), maḻiyeṟumpu, maṇṭiyeṟumpu, piḷḷiyār eṟumpu, kaṭṭeṟumpu.



aivakip poruḷtiripu
1. vēṟupāṭu (Variation)
coṟkaḷ kuṟittaṟkuriya poruḷi viṭṭuviṭṭu vēṟoṉṟik kuṟippatu vēṟupāṭām. atu uyarpu, iḻipu, tolviyalpu eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.

ciṟappikki (Specialisation)
pala poruḷkaṭkup potuvāṉa colli avaṟṟuḷ tali ciṟantataṟku āḷvatu ciṟappikkiyām.

peṟumavaṟṟuḷ yāmaṟiva tilli yaṟivaṟinta
makkaṭpē ṟalla piṟa.

pulāluṇpār kaṟi eṉṉum collāṟ pulālik kuṟittalum ciṟappikkiyē.

		allatu toṭṭilil
		uṟaṅkutal
poruṭperukkamum poruḷiḻappum
oru karuttiṉiṉṟu vēṟoru karuttut tōṉṟuvatāl, potuvākac coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ iraṇṭum palavumākap perukuvatuṇṭu. ētēṉumoru ciṟappupporuḷ kuṟitta aṭipeṟṟa collip potupporuḷil, vaḻaṅkiṉ, acciṟappup poruḷaṭi taṉ poruḷi iḻantuviṭum.
e.ṭu.
poruṭperukkam
kāḷi = erutu, maṟavōṉ.
tiru = celvam, aḻaku, teyvattaṉmi,
paḷḷi = paṭukki, paṭukkiyaṟi, vīṭu, kōyil, kalviccāli.

taṇṇīr  	kuḷirnta nīr  	nīr
ākupeyarum ākuviṉiyum aivakittiripu iruvaki :
1. mutaṟporuḷ taḻuviyatu
2. mutaṟporuḷ taḻuvātatu.
coṟṟoṭarākkam (tōrā. ki.mu. 25,000)
toṭariyam eṉṉum muṟṟuc coṟṟoṭar, ikkālattiṟ pōṉṟē mutaṟkālattilum (eḻuvāy payaṉiliyeṉṉum) īruṟuppukkaḷiyō, (avaṟṟoṭu ceyappaṭuporuḷum cērnta) mūvuṟuppukkaḷiyō koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, avi īṟum iṭiniliyumaṟṟa pakāccoṟkaḷākavē yiruntirukkum. aṟi eṉpatu aṟivu eṉṟum, keṭu eṉpatu kēṭu eṉṟum, cey eṉpatu ceykiyeṉṟum, iṉṟum poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
aṟikoṉ ṟaṟiyā ṉeṉiṉum  (kuṟaḷ. 638)
keṭuviṉṟi maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar  (puṟam. 39, 7)
kaḷiṟu kaḷampaṭutta peruñcey yāṭavar  (neṭu. 171)
aṭi, viḻi, taḷ, col, mutaliya ettuṇiyō viṉiyaṭikaḷ, iṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peyarākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. cila maliyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷil nal (nallu) eṉṉum paṇpaṭi naṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭukiṉṟatu. uṇṭu illi eṉpaṉavum, ivi pōṉṟa piṟavum, iṉṟum mukkālattiṟkum potuvām. kuṟippu viṉikaḷ muṉpiṉ varum coṟṟuṇi koṇṭu ikkālattil oru kālatti uṇarttutal pōṉṟē, terinili viṉiyaṭikaḷum mutaṟkālattil uṇarttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷ vaṭivākavē iruppiṉum, potumakkaḷ pēccu eṉṟum urinaṭiyē yeṉpati maṟantuviṭal kūṭātu.
ceyyuḷ naṭi (tōrā. ki.mu. 15,000)
tamiḻa māntar nīṇṭa kālamāka urinaṭiyilēyē pēci vantapiṉ, mati viḷakkattālum uṇarveḻucciyālum tūṇṭap paṭṭu, teyvappaṟṟu, kātal, pōr, vāḻttu, kati, varalāṟu mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷ paṟṟi, eḷiya naṭiyil teṉmāṅkum cintum pōṉṟa icippāṭṭukkaḷum, akavalum veṇkalip pāvum irāmappayyaṉ ammāṉiyum pōṉṟa ceyyuṭkaḷum, iyaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṉar. mōṉiyetuki yamippum coṟ curukkamum poruṭceṟivum avar uḷḷattik kavarntatiṉāl, palvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟiya paḻamoḻikaḷum ovvoṉṟā yeḻuntaṉa.
nālvaki eḻuttu (nōrā. ki.mu. 12,000)
aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkip pēccum paṇṭamāṟṟuriyāṭṭum kātalaṟivippum, vāymoḻiyāka nikaḻttuvatu nērilaṉṟit toliviṟ kūṭāmiyiṉ, nāḷaṭivil nālvakiyeḻuttu muṟikaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyt tōṉṟiṉa. avi,
1. paṭaveḻuttu (Pictograph)
2. karutteḻuttu (Ideograph)
3. aciyeḻuttu (Syllabary)
4. oliyeḻuttu (Phoretic characters)
eṉpaṉa. eḻuttu eṉpataṉ mutaṟporuḷ paṭam allatu ōviyam eṉpatē. eḻututal varital. iṉṟum paṭameḻututal eṉṉum vaḻakkiruttal kāṇka.
eḻutappeṟṟa ilakkiyam (tōrā : ki.mu. 11,000)
oliyeḻuttut tōṉṟiyapiṉ, kati, varalāṟu, kaṇakku, aṟam, araciyal, maṇaviṉpam, pōr, maruttuvam, kaṇiyam.
ici, nāṭakam, camayam, pukaḻcci, vaḻuttu mutaliya pala tuṟikaḷ paṟṟip paṉuvalkaḷum eḻuntaṉa.
ilakku eṉpatu kuṟi allatu kuṟikkōḷ, makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ ciṟanta kuṟikkōḷi eṭuttukkūṟi, avarit tiruttuvatum uyarttuvatumē paṉuvaliṉ allatu nūliṉ nōkkamātaliṉ, aviyiraṇṭum ilakkiyam eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa. ilakku + iyam = ilakkiyam.
paṇṭi viṟpayiṟciyāḷar, muṟiyē, parupporuḷ, nuṇporuḷ, cuḻalāp poruḷ, cuḻalporuḷ ākiya nālvaki ilakkiṉūṭu eytu payiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟiyē,
peruvaṇmi ciṟu nuṇmi calam niccalam
eṉṟu pāratam (vāraṇā. 56) kūṟum, peruvaṇmi eṉpatu marattiṉ aṭi pōlvatu; ciṟu nuṇmi eṉpatu marattiṉ mēluḷḷa ciṟṟili pōlvatu; calam niccalam eṉpaṉa marattucciyil amikkappeṟum tiripaṉṟiyum cuḻalāc cakkaramum pōlvaṉa.
iliyi ilakkeṉpatu iṉṟum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakkākum. ciṟunuṇmikkup perumpālum iliyiyē kuṟiyākak koṇṭamiyāl, ilakku eṉṉum coṟkuk kuṟi eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu.
ilakkaṇam (tōrā. ki.mu. 10,000)
ilakkaṇamum, moḻiyiṉ allatu ilakkiyattiṉ ciṟanta amippiyum naṭiyiyum eṭuttukkūṟuvatiyē kuṟik kōḷākak koṇṭamiyiṉ, appeyar peṟṟatu. ilakku + aṇam = ilakkaṇam.
uḷḷuṟi teyvam oḻintati nilaṉeṉak
koḷḷu meṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil (993), kuṟi eṉṉuñcol ilakkaṇattik kuṟittal kāṇka. ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉṉum irucollum orē mutaṉiliyē. poruḷ vēṟupāṭu paṟṟi īṟu vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. iviyiraṇṭum mutaṉiliyiṉālum īṟṟiṉālum tūya teṉcollēyeṉṟu teḷika. ivi vaṭamoḻiyil muṟiyē lakṣaya lakṣaṇa eṉṟu tiriyum. āyiṉ, aṅku ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷik kuṟippavi vyākaraṇa, sāhitya eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē. lakṣya lakṣaṇa eṉṉum coṟkaṭku mūlamāka vaṭamoḻiyil kāṭṭappeṟum lakṣ eṉṉum coṟku, ilakku (kuṟi) eṉpatē poruḷ. ākavē, ivviru vaṭacollum teṉ colliṉ tiripeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟamām. mēlum, tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl āriya varukikku 8000 āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭateṉṉum uṇmiyum, iti valiyuṟuttuvatu kāṇka.
ilakkaṇattiṟku aṇaṅkam eṉṟoru peyar kuṟikkiṉṟatu cintāmaṇi (103). katirivēṟpiḷḷi avarkaḷ tokutta tamiḻmoḻiyakarātiyil, añcaṇaṅkam-pañca vilakkaṇam. añcaṇaṅkiyam-pañcavilakkiya eṉak kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum irucoṟkum, aṇaṅku eṉpatu mutaṉiliyāyiruttal vēṇṭum. aṇaṅkutal olittal.
mikiyaṇaṅku meynniṟīi mīḷi maṟavar
pukiyaṇaṅkap pūmāri cinti
eṉpatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli (potuviyaṟ paṭalam, 8). aṇaṅku eṉpatu eḻuttiyuṅ kuṟikkumāyiṉ, aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, muṟiyē, ilakkaṇattiyum ilakkiyattiyuṅ kuṟippatu poruttamē, āyiṉ, iccoṟkaḷ tamiḻakattil eṉṟum vaḻakkil iruntatākat teriyavilli. ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉavē toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvaṉa.
tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ, (1) mutal, (2) cārpu eṉa iru vakippaṭum. mutalāvatu iyalpākat tōṉṟiya eḻuttukkaḷ mutaleḻut tukkaḷ, piṉpu avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ cārpākat tirinta eḻuttukkaḷ cārpeḻuttukkaḷ. uyireḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭum meyyeḻuttup patiṉeṭṭumāka mutal eḻuttukkaḷ muppatām. cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟām. ākavē, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ allatu olikaḷ mottam muppattu mūṉṟām.
uyireḻuttukkaḷ, kuṟilintum neṭilēḻumāka iru vakip paṭum. meyyeḻuttukkaḷum, valliṉam āṟum melliṉam āṟum iṭiyiṉam āṟumāka mūvakippaṭum.
mutaleḻuttukkaḷ (Phonemes)
māntaṉ taṉ vāyit tiṟantavuṭaṉ iyalpāka olikkum oli akaram allatu ākāram. kuḻanti piṟanta uṭaṉ aḻum oliyum muḻittal muṟippaṭṭa akara ākāramē. ākavē, mutalāvatu māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiyatu uyiroliyē; atu akaramē.
vāy tiṟavāta niliyil, mūkkuvaḷiyāl olikkum oli makaram. ākavē, meyyolikaḷuḷ mutalāvatu tōṉṟiyatu, itaḻ maṭṭum porutti eḷitāka volikkum makaramākum.
iṅṅaṉam mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya akaramum makaramuñcēriṉ, am eṉṉum aci piṟakkum. orukāl itaṉiṉṟu. amma muṟippeyar kuḻavivaḷarp poliyāyt tōṉṟiyirukkalām. pāluṟuppik kuṟikkum ammam eṉṉum col marumam eṉpataṉ tiripākum. marumam - mammam - ammam.
māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya moḻiyolikaḷuḷ, uyirellām muṉpum meyyellām piṉpumākat tōṉṟavilli. uyirum meyyum oḻuṅkiṉṟi māṟimāṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa. ammuṟiyi iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. āyiṉum, uyiriṉattiyum meyyiṉat tiyum vēṟu pirittu ovvōr iṉattiyuñ cērnta olikaḷ tōṉṟiya muṟiyi oruvāṟu aṟiyalām.
uyir tōṉṟiya muṟi
mutal tōṉṟiyavi : neṭil - ā, ī, ū
iṭittōṉṟiyavi : neṭil - ē, ō kuṟil - a, i, u
kaṭittōṉṟiyavi : neṭil - ai, au kuṟil - e, o
uyirkaḷuḷ, neṭil muṉpum kuṟil piṉpum tōṉṟiṉa.
mutalil tōṉṟiya mūvuyirum, muṟiyē, cēymi yaṇmi muṉmic cuṭṭukkaḷākavē tōṉṟiṉa.
aṇmic cuṭṭiṉum muṉmic cuṭṭu pintiyatātalālum, caṟṟu mikunta muyaṟci vēṇṭutalāṉum, īkāram ūkārattiṟku muntiya tātal vēṇṭum.
ikara īkārattil mōṉiyāka ekara ēkāramum, ukara ūkārattiṉ mōṉiyāka okara okāramum tōṉṟutalāṉum; kocci vaḻakkil iṭam eṉpatu eṭam eṉṟum, uṉakku eṉpatu oṉakku eṉṟum, ikaram ekaramākavum ukaram okaramākavum olippatālum, aṇmic cuṭṭiliruntē ekara ēkāramum, muṉmic cuṭṭiliruntē okara ōkāramum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.

eṉṟu, amippu vakiyil,
akara ikaram aikāra mākum  (54)
akara ukaram aukāra mākum  (55)
eṉṟum, tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, akara ikaram cērntu aikāramum akaravukaram cērntu aukāramum puṇarolikaḷāyt (Diphthongs) tōṉṟiṉaveṉa aṟiyalām. ikaram ukarattiṉum muntiyatātaliṉ, ikarak kūṟuḷḷa aikāram ukarak kūṟuḷḷa aukārattiṉum muntit tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭanūlār puṇarccit tiripip paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷiyum puṇarolikaḷākak koḷvar. akkoḷki tamiḻukku ēṟkātu. avi mōṉit tiripāy eḻuntaviyē.
ōriru coṉ mutalil varum akara ikaram aikāramākavum eḻutap peṟum.
e.ṭu. vaḷ-vay-vayir-vayiram-viram.
ōriru coṉ mutalil varum akara ukaram aukāramākavum eḻutappeṟum, 
e.ṭu. katuvāli (= kuṟukiya vāliyuṭiya paṟavi iṉam - kavutāri (ilakkaṇappōli - kutāri).
iṉi, akarattiyaṭutta yakarameyyum ellāviṭattum aikāra mākavē eḻutappeṟum.
akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
ai eṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟattōṉṟum  (tol. 56)
e.ṭu. vaḷ (= kūrmi) - (vay) - vi = kūrmi. ḷakara mey yakara meyyākat tirivatu peruvaḻakku.
e.ṭu. koḷ - kōḷ - kōy, toḷ - toy, noḷ - noy, piḷ - piy, māḷ - māy, veḷ - vey - veyyōṉ = virumpukiṉṟōṉ. vey - veymmi - vemmi = viruppam.
vemmi vēṇṭal  (tol. uri, 36)
yakara meyyīṟṟu okara mutaṟcol, īreḻuttuccollāyiruntu akara mutalāy māṟiṉ, aikāra vaṭivu peṟum.
e.ṭu. poḷ - poy (uṭṭuḷi) - (pay) - pi.
akaratti yaṭutta vakaramey, oru cila coṟkaḷil aukāramākavum eḻutappeṟum.
e.ṭu. avviyam - auviyam, kavvu - kuvu, vavvāl = vuvāl.
auvi eṉṉum col avvi eṉpataṉ tiripēyāyiṉum, oru pulattiyāriṉ iyaṟpeyarātaliṉ, aukāra mutalatākavē eḻutappaṭal vēṇṭum.
ammi - avvi - auvi.
av, avvāṟu, cavvu, tavvu mutaliya cila coṟkaḷil, akaramaṭutta vakaramey aukāravīṟāka eḻutappaṭuvatēyilli. āyiṉ, ay eṉṉum vaṭivam ay eṉṉum aciyik kuṟittālaṉṟi, eḻuttuttamiḻil nikaḻtaṟku iṭamēyilli. iti nōkkip pōlum,

au eṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟātuviṭṭatu! uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ, 
ai, au iraṇṭum puṇaroliyaṉkaḷ (Dipthongs or Compound Phonemes); ēṉiya taṉiyoliyaṉkaḷ (Monophthongs or Simple Phonemes).
uyir tōṉṟiya vakikaḷ
1. aṅkāttal (vāytiṟattal) - a, ā
2. cuṭṭal - ā, ī, ū
3. mōṉittiripu - i - e, u - o
4. uṇarcciyoli - ai!
5. vāycceyki - av - au
6. kuṟukkam - ā - a, ē - e
7. oppoli - ū (kū)
aṅkāttal eṉpatu maṟṟellā vakikaṭkum aṭippaṭit tuṇiyē yaṉṟi, taṉivaki yaṉṟu.

mey tōṉṟiya muṟi
ma
pa, ka, ṅa
la, ra, ta, na
ca, ña, ya, va
ṭa, ṇa, ḷa, ḻa
ṟa, ṉa
kuṟippu : (1) uyirum meyyum tōṉṟiya muṟiyāka iṅkuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷa īroḻuṅkum, tōrāya (uttēca) muṟiyē yaṉṟi immiyum vaḻuvāta aṟiviyaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭa tulliya muṟiyalla.
2. uyirum meyyum vevvēṟākak kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppiṉum, uyirellān tōṉṟiyapiṉ mey tōṉṟiṉa veṉṟākātu. iruvakiyum ovvoṉṟāy allatu oṉṟum palavumāy māṟi māṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa.
mutalil ākāravuyirum atiyaṭuttu makarameyyum tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum.
yakaram tōṉṟumuṉ īkāra ikaramum, vakaram tōṉṟumuṉ ūkāra ukaramum, tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa tōṉṟumuṉ ellā vuyirkaḷum tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
3. melliṉa meykaḷuḷ, makaram oṉṟē taṉiyē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ēṉiyavellām ṅka, ñc, ṇṭa, nta, ṉṟa eṉat taṉtaṉ valliṉa meyyuṭaṉ iṇintē tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum. 
kuḻavikaḷiṉ toṇṭiyil ṅka eṉṉum oli piṟattal kāṇka.
valliṉameykaḷ taṉittaṉit tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum.
4. māntaṉ allatu muntiyal tamiḻaṉ pēciya iyaṟki moḻi, uyir, mey eṉṉum pakup puṇarcciyiṉṟi, ōraciyum, palavaci yumākiya olikaḷiyum ōcikaḷiyumē koṇṭiruntatu.
meytōṉṟiya vakikaḷ
1. oppoli - k (kākkā), ṇ (kiṇ)
2. vāycceyki - v (av), m (am)
3. iṉattōṉṟal - ṅ (ṅka), ṉ (ṉṟa)
4. puṇarcci - ñ (pū + cōli = pūñcōli)
5. tiripu - n (porum - porun), r (l-r)
6. mutircci - ḻ (ḷa-ḻa), ṟ (ra-ṟa)
7. iyaṟki - t, p
aḷavu (māttiri)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ oliyaḷavu aḷapu allatu māttiri yeṉappaṭum. māttal aḷattal, itu oru vaḻakkaṟṟa viṉi. māttiram eṉṉum collum itaṉaṭip piṟantatē. mā eṉṉum aḷavup peyar mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyarām.
ōr imi allatu noṭiyaḷavu oru māttiri. uyirāyiṉum uyirmeyyāyiṉum, kuṟilukku oṉṟum neṭilukku iraṇṭum māttiriyām. uyirmeyyil uyirum meyyum ākiya īreḻut tiruppiṉum, ammeykku māttiriyilli. taṉimeykku māttiri ari.
ai, au eṉṉum iru neṭilum taṉittu nillātum aḷapeṭuk kātum colluṟuppāy variṉ, oṉṟari māttiriyāyk kuṟukiyolikkum. aikāram colliṭiyil oru māttiri yākavum kuṟukum.
2. cārpeḻuttukkaḷ
uyirum meyyum ākiya mutaleḻuttukkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ cārpiṉāl tōṉṟuvaṉa cārpeḻuttukkaḷ, cārtal oṉṟi yoṉṟu aṭuttal. uyiriṉattic cērntavi iraṇṭum meyyiṉattic cērntatu oṉṟum āka, cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mottam mūṉṟām. avi kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉpaṉavākum. ivaṟṟuḷ ku, u iyaṟki, ēṉiyiraṇṭum puṇarcci viṉi.
tolkāppiyar kūṟiyavāṟu immūṉṟē cārpeḻuttukkaḷ. naṉṉūlār uyirmeyyiyum vēṟu cila eḻuttuk kuṟukkaṅ kaḷiyum aḷapeṭiyiyum cērttut tavaṟākap patteṉa virittu viṭṭār. uyirmey uyirummeyyum cērnta kūṭṭeḻuttē yaṉṟi vēṟeḻuttākātu. eḻuttuk kuṟukkaṅkaḷi yellām cārpeḻutteṉṟu koḷḷiṉ, āytak kuṟukkattic cārpiṟ cārpeṉṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. aḷapeṭi eṉpatu eḻut toli nīṭṭamē yaṉṟit taṉiyeḻuttākātu. ivvuṇmikaḷi yellām nōkkātu, cāppeḻuttut tokiyip perukki yataṟku, māṇavari mayakkutalaṉṟi vēṟoru payaṉumiṉṟām.
kuṟṟiyalikaram
ariyaḷapāyk kuṟukiyolikkum ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaram, atu mūvakiyil tōṉṟum.
1.	yakarattōṭu puṇarnta kuṟṟiyalukarattiripu.
	e.ṭu. varaku + yātu = varakiyātu.
2.	puṇarcciyāl aminta miyā eṉṉum muṉṉiliyacic col.
	kēḷum + aiyā = kēḷumiyā - kēḷumiyā - kēṇmiyā. o.nō: maruḷum - maruṇm.
3.	melliṉa iṭiyiṉa meyyīṟu yakarattōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcci viḷivu.
	maṇ + yāṉi = maṇṇiyāṉi
	vēḷ + yāvaṉ = vēḷiyāvaṉ
ivvakip puṇarcci ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu. 
kuṟṟiyalukaram
taṉineṭiṟkum iru allatu palaveḻuttiṟkup piṉ, valliṉa meyyōṭu kūṭic collīṟāy varum ukaram, itaḻ kuviyātu, ikarattiṟkum ukarattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭu, ari māttiriyāyk kuṟukiyolikkum atu kuṟṟiyalukaram.
e.ṭu. māṭu, cekku, paruppu, piṇṇākku, vantataṟku.
āytam
taṉiccollilum puṇarccollilum, kuṟilukkum valliṉa uyirmeykkum iṭiyil, nuṇṇiya kakaramāka olittu niṟkum eḻuttu āytamām. āyntatu āytam. āytal nuṇukutal.
ōytal āytal niḻattal cā ay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (813) kūṟutal kāṇka.
āytam piṉvarumāṟu nālvakiyil tōṉṟum
(1) puṇarccittiripu
colluṟuppup puṇarcci

veḷ + ku = veḵku
alkutal = curuṅkutal. veḷḷutal = virumputal, veḷ - veṇṭu (ācippaṭu). veḷ - vey - veyyōṉ = virumpiyōṉ.
coṟpuṇarcci

muḷ + tītu = muṭṭītu, muḵṭītu

ivvaki ceyyuṭkē yuriyatām.
āytam ōruyirōṭuṅ kūṭātu taṉittu niṟpatāl taṉinili eṉṟum, puḷḷivaṭivāyiruppatāl puḷḷi eṉṟum, muppuḷḷi eṉṟum, taṉakkuppiṉ varum valliṉa meyyi melivippatāl nalipu eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum.
pattu, paḵtu; kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu; eṉṉum colliṇikaḷi olittuk kāṇka.
iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻukkuriya āytam, tirimoḻi kaṭkuriya polivolikaḷip (Voiced Sounds) piṟappikkum eṉṉum kūṟṟu, tiripuṇarcciyiṉ viḷiveṉa aṟika. āyta voliyiṉ kakaraviṉat taṉmiyi aḵkēṉam, maḵkāṉ eṉṉum cāriyip puṇarppālum kaṇṭukoḷka.
cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaṉuḷ, kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum tām niṟkumiṭattu vēṟoṉṟaṟkum iṭantārāmiyāl, avviraṇ ṭiyum maṟuvoliyaṉkaḷ (Allophones) eṉṉalām. āytam attuṇi oḻuṅkum yāppuṟavum peṟāmiyum maṟuvoli yaṉākātu.

cārntu varalmarapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē  (1)
eṉat tolkāppiyar, nūṉmarapu eṉṉum mutal iyalil. muntu nūlār moḻintavāṟu mutaleḻuttiṟkē ciṟappuk koṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.

kiṉitāyttōṉṟiṭum at tamiḻ
eṉṟu civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟiyatuṅ kāṇka. (kāñci taḻuva. 244)
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumākiya muppateḻuttuk kaḷi maṭṭum koṇṭa tokutikkuk kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, ataṉoṭu 216 uyirmeyyeḻuttuṅ koṇṭa tokutikku neṭuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, peyar. paṉṉīruyirum patiṉeṇ meyyoṭuṅ kūṭa, uyirmeymottam iru 
nūṟṟuppatiṉāṟām.
ellā moḻikaḷilum uyirmeykaḷ kalantutāṉ olikkiṉṟaṉa.
āyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟkuk kūṭṭu vaṭivam mutaṉmutal amittavar tamiḻarē, tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyē vaṭamoḻiyilum piṟa intiya moḻikaḷilum uyirmey vaṭivukaḷ amintaṉa. iṉṉum mēli āriyattilum cēmiyattilum uyirum meyyum 
taṉittaṉi niṟuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. amerikku, cappāṉ mutaliya oru cila moḻikaḷil maṭṭum, taṉivarikkum kūṭṭu varikkum iṭip paṭṭa acivarikaḷ (Syllabaries) ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aviyum kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭaviyē.
āytam uyirēṟappeṟāta meyyāyiṉum, ataṟkut taṉi vaṭiva muṇmi paṟṟi atiyum ōr eḻuttākak koḷḷiṉ, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ mottam iru nūṟṟu nāṟpattēḻām.
peyar mutal, cārpu
cevippulaṉāṉa moḻiyolikaḷ kaṭpulaṉāṉa varivaṭivil eḻutappeṟṟataṉāl, eḻutteṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. eḻuttu eṉpatu varivaṭivik kuṟippiṉ toḻilākupeyar; olivaṭivik kuṟippiṉ kuṟiyākupeyar.
viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum  (1594)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyuḷḷapaṭi, tamiḻil mutalilakkaṇa nūliyaṟṟiyavaṉ, muṟṟat tuṟantavaṉum meypporuḷaṟivu nirampiyavaṉumāṉa muṉivaṉāvāṉ.
muṉital = (ulaka vāḻvi) veṟuttal, veṟuttut tuṟattal, muṉi, muṉi eṉṉum iraṇṭum orē colliṉ vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭivamām. muṉintavaṉ muṉivaṉ, muṉintavaṉ muṉivaṉ.
muṉivu muṉivākum  (tol. col. 386)
antaṇar nūṟkum  (543)
iṉṟīm pāli muṉiyiṉ  (perumpāṇ. 180)
ulakiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ ellām, uyir, mey, uyir mey eṉa mūvaki. uyir tāṉē iyaṅkum kāṟṟu vaṭiviṉatu; mey uyiriṉ utaviyiṉṟi iyaṅkāta uṭampu allatu kaṉapporuḷ; uyirmey uyiroṭu kūṭiya uṭampu. pirāṇi (pirāṇaṉiyuṭiyatu) eṉṟa vaṭacol tamiḻil vaḻakkūṉṟiyapiṉ, ataṟku nērāṉa uyirmey eṉṉum tamiḻc col vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉatuṭaṉ, taṉ poruḷiyum iḻantatu. tamiḻil mutaṉūl kaṇṭa muṉivaṉ, mūvakiyeḻuttukkaḷum oliyiyakkattil mūvakip poruḷ kaḷi ottiruppatu kaṇṭu, apporuḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyē avveḻuttukkaṭkum uvamiyāku peyarāka iṭṭāṉ. tāṉē olikkum eḻuttu uyireḻuttu; uyiroṭu kūṭiyallatu olikkāta eḻuttu meyyeḻuttu; uyirmeyyip pōṉṟa eḻuttu uyir meyyeḻuttu. ittakiya amippu vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇpataṟkaritām.
kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam eṉpaṉa veḷippaṭi.
uyir, āvi eṉpaṉavum; mey, uṭal, uṭampu, uṟuppu, oṟṟu puḷḷi eṉpaṉavum; oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ.
muṟiyum piṟappum
tamiḻ eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉiraṇṭuḷ, muṟiyum oṉṟām. itu mēli moḻikaṭkilli. vaṭamoḻiyuṭpaṭa ēṉiyintiya moḻikaḷellām neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyil tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyaṉavē.
uyir, muntittōṉṟiyatu paṟṟiyum, tāṉāy olippatu paṟṟiyum, neṭuṅkaṇakkil muṉvikkappeṟṟatu. kuṟumi paṟṟik kuṟil muṉṉum, neṭumi paṟṟi neṭil piṉṉum vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ā, ī, ū mūṉṟum, muṟiyē cēymic cuṭṭākavum aṇmic cuṭṭākavum muṉmic cuṭṭākavum iyalpākat tōṉṟiṉa miyāl, piṟavuyirkaḷukku muṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, ākāram vāytiṟanta maṭṭil olippatāl mutalilum, īkāram vāyiṉ piṉpakkattil aṭināviḷimpu mēlvāyp pallip poruntiyolippatāl iṭiyilum, ūkāram vāyiṉ muṉpakkattil itaḻ (utaṭu) kuvintolippatāl kaṭiyilum, vikkappaṭṭaṉa. itiṉiṉṟu, vāyiṉ piṉpuṟattiliruntu muṉpuṟam nōkkiya variciyil tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ amikkap paṭṭati aṟiyalām.
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ muṟiyē īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷiṉ mōṉit tiripātalāl, iyalpākat tōṉṟiya mutal mūṉṟuyirkaṭkum piṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, ēkāram aṭināviḷimpu mēlvāyp pallip poruntiyolippatāl muṉṉum, ōkāram itaḻ kuvintolippatāl piṉṉum, vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ai, au iraṇṭum muṟiyē ai, au eṉṉum puṇaroli kaḷātalāl, aṭināviḷimpu mēlvāyp pallip porunti yolikkum aikāram ēkārattiṉ piṉṉum, itaḻ kuvintolikkum aukāram ōkārattiṉ piṉṉum vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
āytam nuṇṇiya kakaramātalāl, kakarattiṉ muṉ vikkappaṭṭatu.
meykaḷuḷ valliṉamum melliṉamum etiriṉaṅkaḷātalāl, mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiyaviyellām valiyum meliyumāka iv viraṇṭāy muṉ amikkappeṟṟaṉa. iṭiyiṉam avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa nāṉkum piṟameykaḷ oḻuṅku paṭuttappaṭṭataṟkup pintit tōṉṟiyamiyāl, iṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
meykaḷuḷ, ka, ṅa aṭiyaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, ca, ña iṭi yaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, ṭa, ṇa muṉṉaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum ta, na pallaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, pa, ma itaḻakap piṟappiṉavum, ātalāl ammuṟiyē vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
makaramoḻinta melliṉa meykaḷellām taṉtaṉ valliṉa meykkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyamiyālum, valiyolitta piṉ, meliyolippatē muyaṟcik keḷimiyātalālum, valiyum meliyum aṭuttaṭuttu niṟpatu olivēṟṟumi yit teḷivāyk kāṭṭuvatālum, valimuṉṉum melipiṉṉumāka iṇiyiṇi yāy niṟuttappeṟṟaṉaveṉa aṟika.
iṭiyiṉa voliyiṉ iṭittaṉmi valloliyum mel loliyum aṟinta piṉṉarē aṟiyak kiṭattaliṉ, valimeli kaḷiṉ piṉ iṭiyiṉam vikkappaṭṭatu. iṭiyiṉa meykaḷuḷ, yakaram iṭiyaṇṇattilum, rakaram muṉṉaṇṇattilum, lakaram pallaṇṇattilum, vakaram pallitaḻilum, piṟappatāl, avi ammuṟiyē vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
kumarikkaṇṭattil neṭuṅkaṇakkut toṭaṅkiyapōtu, atil amintirunta eḻuttukkaḷ ittaṉiyē. piṉṉar, nāḷaṭivil ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum nāṉkum tōṉṟiṉa, ataṉālēyē avi iṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
annāṉkaṉuḷ ḷa ḻa muṉtōṉṟiyamiyāl muṉṉum; ṟa ṉa piṉtōṉṟiyamiyāṟ piṉṉum vikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ḷa, ḻa iraṇṭum muṉṉaṇṇa varuṭolikaḷāyiṉum (Linguals or Cerebals), ḻakaram ḷakarattiṉum caṟṟup piṉṉiruntu varuṭap peṟuvatāl, muṉvikkappeṟṟatu.
iṟutimey nāṉkum pintit tōṉṟiṉaveṉpataṟku ētuk kaḷāvaṉa :
1. 	ḷa, ḻa iraṇṭum iṭiyiṉattuḷḷum lakarattiṟku muṉṉum vikkap peṟāmi.
2. 	ṟa, ṉa iraṇṭum valimeli yiṇikaḷuḷ ta na iṇikku muṉ vikkap peṟāmi.
3. 	lakaram tiraṇṭu ḷakaramum ḷakaram tiraṇṭu ḻakaramum ātal.
	e.ṭu. kal-karumi. kal + tu = kaḵtu. kaḵṟeṉal = karumik kuṟippu. kal - kāl = karumi. kāl - kāḷ = karumi. kāḷ - kāḻ = karumi.
4. 	rakaram tiraṇṭu ṟakaram ātal.
	e.ṭu. ar-aṟu, oḷir-oḷiṟu, muri-muṟi :
5.	ṉakarantōṉṟumuṉ nakaramē colliṟutiyilum vaḻaṅkiyami
	e.ṭu. verin, paḻunu
6.	tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyum ēṉiyolikaḷiṉ meṉmiyum.
ṟakaram tōṉṟiyapōtu ataṟkiṉamākavē ṉakaramum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiṟṟu. tannakaram ṟakarattiṟku iṉamākāmi kāṇka.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmi, teṉmi muṉmi, meṉmi mutaliya taṉmikaḷi aṟiyāmiyāl, kālṭuvel uḷḷiṭṭa mēli yārāycciyāḷar tamiḻic camaṟkiruta aṭippaṭiyilāyntu, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum muṟiyil,tamiḻ 
neṭuṅ kaṇakku camaṟkirutattip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉṟu muṭivu koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl cila koṇṭāṉa mārum tamiḻppaṟṟaṟṟa tiraviṭat tamiḻp pulavarum, ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa nāṉkum vaṭamoḻi yilillāmiyāl neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu, pitaṟṟalāyiṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiliṉ miyē iṟutivip piṟkuk karaṇiyamāyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyi lillāta ekara okaramum iṟutiyil vikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumē! aṅṅaṉamiṉmi yiṉ, atupōliyuriyeṉa maṟukka.
uruvam
eḻuttu eṉṉum peyarē, tamiḻukku ilakkaṇan tōṉṟumuṉṉarē eḻuntiruntamiyi uṇarttum, eḻutuvatu eḻuttu. eḻututal varital.
eḻutuṅkāṟ kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōl  (kuṟaḷ, 1285)
iṉṉa palapala eḻuttunili maṇṭapam  (paripā. 19,53)
eḻuvutal eṉpatu icikkaruviyiṉiṉṟu oliyeḻuppu talik kuṟikkumēyaṉṟi eḻututalik kuṟikkātu.
paṇṭit tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷellām pirāmiyiṉ tiripāṉa vaṭṭeḻuttuk kaḷeṉṟum, kirantaveḻuttu teṉṉāṭṭil piṟkālattil vaṭamoḻik keṉṟēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭateṉṟum, potuvākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, eḻuttuvaṭivam paṟṟiya cila tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷiyum naṉṉūl nūṟpākkaḷiyum, paṇṭiyēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷiyum nōkkumiṭattu, ikkaruttup poruttamuṭiyatāyt tōṉṟavilli. iṟṟi vaḻakkiluḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟiyē paṇṭi vaḻakkilum iruntateṉṟum, paṭṭayaṅkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa vaṭṭeḻuttu veṭ ṭeḻuttu eṉap peyar peṟṟirukkalāmeṉṟum, kirantaleḻuttu paṇṭit tamiḻeḻuttiṉ tiripēyeṉṟum, atu ēṭṭiṟkuriya āṇiyeḻutteṉṟum koḷvatē poruttamākat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷum ētukkaḷumāvaṉa :
1.	mikap paḻimiyāṉa tamiḻēṭṭilum iṟṟiyeḻuttu vaṭivēyiruttal.
2.	ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iṟṟiyeḻuttu vaṭivit toṉṟu toṭṭateṉak kūṟal.
tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttum āṇ(ṭu)
eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi.  (98)
eṉṟu 13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta pavaṇanti muṉivar kūṟiṉār.
ki.mu. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa tolkāppiyattiṉ nūṉmarapu eṉṉum mutaliyalil,
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram  (2)
āytam eṉṟa - muppāṟ puḷḷiyum.
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvā kummē  (14)
meyyiṉ iyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal  (15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē  (16)
puḷḷi yillā ... uyirtta lāṟē  (17)
eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivviyalpukaḷiṟ cila paṭṭaya veḻuttiṟkup poruntavilli.
(3)	paṭṭayaveḻuttu vaṭṭamāyirāmi. vaṭṭeḻuttu eṉpatu vaṭivu paṟṟiyatāyiṉ, atu ēṭṭeḻuttiṟkē (kiranta vaṭiveḻuttiṟkē) poruntu vatākum
(4)	ēṭṭiṟku vaḷikōṭṭeḻuttum paṭṭayattiṟku nēr kōṭṭeḻuttumē icivāyiruttal.
(5)	tamiḻeḻuttu acōkak kalveṭṭiṟkum vaṭamoḻi varaviṟkum muntiyatātal.
(6)	vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakku tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkip piṉpaṟṟiyē amittiruttal.
(7)	mēṉāṭukaḷilum karuvikkēṟpa eḻuttu vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭirun tami.
intiya nākarikam āriyarateṉṟum, tamiḻ ellā vakiyilum vaṭamoḻi yip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉṟum mēliyaṟiñar kuruṭṭut taṉamākak koṇṭirunta aṭippaṭit tavaṟukaḷē, tamiḻeḻuttu pirāmiyiṉiṉṟu tirintateṉṟu koḷḷaṟ kiṭantantaṉa.
paṇṭiyeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟ cila iṉṟu māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. avi varumāṟu :

ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ neṭilkaṭkup pintit tōṉṟiya miyāl, avaṟṟi vēṟupaṭuttikkāṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ mēṟ puḷḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷip pēcuvōriṉ muṉṉōr, ekara okarakkuṟilkaḷ tamiḻil tōṉṟumuṉ kumarik kaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkē ceṉṟatiṉālēyē, ammoḻikaḷil iṉṟum akkuṟilkaḷ illi. ammoḻikaḷiṉ mūlamākiya pirākirutaṅkaḷi vēta āciriyar pēciṉatiṉālēyē, avar vētattilum avi illi.
tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇ(ṭu)
eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi
eṉṟu 13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattiṉatāṉa naṉṉūl (98) kūṟuvatāl, tamiḻ ēṭṭeḻuttu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvateṉat tuṇiyalām. āṇṭu eṉpatu akkālattu (muṟ kālattil) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatākum. ekara okara uyirum uyir meyyum puḷḷipeṟṟa oṉṟē, paṇṭiyeḻuttiṉ vēṟṟumi yeṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār pavaṇanti muṉivar. avar kālattilum avi puḷḷi peṟṟaṉaveṉiṉ, ati ēṉ vitantu kūṟal vēṇṭum? tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum eṉpati lēyē atu aṭaṅkiviṭumē! ātalāl, atu uriyaṉṟeṉka.
eytum eṉpatu vaḻakkam paṟṟiya kālavaḻuvamiti.
piṟkālattil vēṟṟaracu vantuviṭṭataṉālum, tamiḻik kākkum poṟuppu centamiḻppulavar kiyiliṉmiyālum, ilakkaṇa maṟiyā māntar ēṭṭilum kallilum tappuntavaṟumāy eḻuti vantamiyē, vīramā muṉivar muṉṉi muṟippaṭi tiruttiṉār eṉa aṟika.
māttiri
eḻuttukaṭku māttiri iṉṉiṉṉa aḷapeṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭṭiruppiṉum, kūppīṭu (viḷi), oppāri (pulampal), icip pāṭṭu, teruviṟpaṉi (paṇṭamāṟṟu) mutaliya ulakiyaṟ ceytikaḷil, avi cevippāṭu, tuyaram, iṉimi mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi, vēṇṭumaḷavu allatu iyaṉṟa aḷavu nīṇṭolikkum. atu aḷapeṭi eṉappaṭum. aḷapeṭuttal māttiri kaṭan toluttal. uyiraḷapeṭi, oṟṟaḷapeṭi eṉa aḷapeṭi iruvaki. oṟṟumey.
uyiraḷapeṭiyi varivaṭiviṟ kuṟikkumpōtu neṭiliṉ piṉṉum, kuṟiliṉ piṉṉum iyalpākavuriya aḷaviṟku mēṟpaṭṭa ovvoru māttirikkum ovvoru muṟiyāka, avvakkuṟil allatu iṉakkuṟil vaṭivu cērtteḻutappaṭum. iciyil aḷapeṭikkuṟilkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi neṭilkaḷum toṭarntum viṭṭum olikkum; piṟavaṟṟil toṭarntē olikkum.
oṟṟaḷapeṭiyāyiṉ, ellāviṭattum, kūṭiya ovvōr arimāttirik kum oru muṟiyāka avvam meyvaṭivē cērtteḻutappaṭum.
meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ valliṉam aḷapeṭukkā.
ici kuṟintaviṭattu ati niṟikkac ceyyuḷilum aḷapeṭi nikaḻum.
ulakavaḻak kaḷapeṭiyum ceyyuḷvaḻak kaḷapeṭiyum piṉvarumāṟu peyarpeṟum.
ulaka vaḻakku 			ceyyuḷ vaḻakku
iyaṟki yaḷapeṭi	ceyaṟki yaḷapeṭi
ulakiya laḷapeṭi		ceyyuḷiya laḷapeṭi
ceyyuḷiṟ kuṟil aḷapeṭuppiṉum, neṭilvaṭiviṉ piṉṉarē kuṟil vaṭivu kuṟikkappaṭum. valliṉattuṭaṉ ḷakara ḻakaramum ceyyuḷil aḷapeṭukkā.
kuṟṟiyalukara mēṟiya kakaram kakarattōṭu puṇariṉ, akkuṟṟiyalu karam kālmāttiriyāyk kuṟukum.
e.ṭu. kokkukkāl.
noṭittal viṉiyil noṭikkak karututal kālmāttirikkum, viral poruttutal ari māttirikkum, viral tirittal mukkāl māttirikkum, veṭittal orumāttirikkum aḷavu nikaḻcciyām.
uṉṉal kālē ūṉṟal ariyē
muṟukkal mukkāl viṭuttal oṉṟē
palukkoliyam (Phonetics)
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ, mūkkoliyuyir allatu melliṉa uyir (Nasal vowel) eṉa oṉṟu tamiḻil illavē yilli.
avaṉ, vantēṉ, maram, pōṉōm mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa melliṉa meykaḷē, cōmpalāṟ cevviyāyp palukkap peṟāmal mūkkoliyaḷavāy olikkiṉṟaṉa. ivvoliyi īṟṟayaluyirmēlēṟṟi, ataṉi mūkkoli uyir eṉpatu tiripuṇarcciyē. avar, tāy, makaḷ, vantāl mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa iṭiyiṉamey, ōriṭattum mūkkoliyāy oliyāmiyi olittuk kāṇka.
moḻimutal ukaram itaḻkuvittē olikkappeṟuvatāl, kuṟṟiyalukaram coṉmutaṟkaṇ varavē varātu. tiraviṭap pulavar cilar karutukiṟapaṭi kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟumaṉṟu; olikkuṟuk katti allatu tiripik kāṭṭutaṟkē, ataṉmītu puḷḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu.
itaṉiyē,
meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal  (104)
kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa  (105)
eṉa māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyar, āyiṉ, iti yuṇarātu kuṟṟiya lukaramum meyyīṟeṉap piṟaḻak koṇṭaṉar āriyavaḻit tamiḻāciriyar cilar.
ai, au iraṇṭum tamittu niṟkumpōtu, ai, au eṉa iru māttiriyē olikkum; colluṟuppāy varumpōtē ay, av eṉa oṉṟari māttiriyāyk kuṟukum.
āytam kakaravoliyic cārnta teṉpati, atu toṉṟu toṭṭu olikkappaṭṭuvarum muṟimiyiṉālum, ēṉaccāriyi ēṟkumpōtu aḵkēṉam eṉak kakarattip paṟṟuk kōṭākak koḷvatālum, kuṟuṅkaṇakkilum neṭuṅkaṇakkilum kakara meykku muṉ vikkappaṭṭiruppatālum, paṇṭi neṭuṅ kaṇakkēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyil ka - ḵ eṉa variyap peṟṟamiyālum, aṟiyalām.
āytam varum coṟkaḷiyellām āyntu pārppiṉ, atu perumpālum lakara ḷakarat tiripākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iti nōkkumiṭattu, kumarikkaṇṭattil ōriṭattu olittiri pākavō puṇarccit tiripākavō āytam tōṉṟi iruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
cilar āytatti vaṭamoḻi vicarkkameṉak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, atu akaramēṟiyum olikkumeṉak kūṟi,
aṟṟā laḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu
peṟṟāṉ neṭituykku māṟu.  (943)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar aḷavaṟin tuṇṇuka eṉṉum pāṭattil taḷi cariyā yiruttaliṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷa vēṇṭiya tiṉṟām. nayaṉila colliṉuñ colluka eṉṉum kuṟaḷil (197). colka eṉpatu taḷikkēṟpac colluka eṉa vantiruttal kāṇka.
iṉi, lakara ḷakara vakaram eṅṅaṉam āytamākat tiriya muṭiyumeṉṟu palar mayaṅkalām. pala olittiripukaḷ ellār vāykkum ēṟṟaṉavākavum, cila olittiripukaḷ ōriṭattu oru cilar vāykkē icintaṉavākavum, amintuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāṟ piṉṉavi piṟar mayaṅkutaṟku iṭamākiṉṟaṉa.
ḻakaram pala coṟkaḷil kakaramākat tirikiṉṟatu.
e.ṭu. kuḻi-kuki, muḻi-muki, cuḻiyam-cukiyam, toḻuti-tokuti, niḻal - nikar = oḷi, maḻavu - makavu.
o.nō : viḻi - L. vigil, E. wake.
lakara ḷakaram ḻakarattiṉ iṉamākavum ataṉiṉum melintum iruppataṉāl, kakarattiṉ meliyākiya āytamākat tirintaṉa.
vakaram kakaramāvatu peruvaḻakkātal teḷḷitu.
e.ṭu. āvā-ākā, civappu - cikappu, ceviḷ-cekiḷ, tuvar-tukir.
āytam kakara meliyātaliṉ, vakaram āytamākat tiritalum iyalpē. itaṟku varumoḻi mutaṟ kakaramum tuṇi ceyyum.
av + kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya
iṉi, ikkālattuc cilar āytaveḻuttip piṟa tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum iṭṭu ja, ṣa F, Z mutaliya  piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷik kuṟippatu, tamiḻiyl piṟkum marapiṟkum
muṟṟum māṟāṉatum tollāciriyar kaṭṭaḷikku muraṇāṉatum ākumeṉṟum, tamiḻip piṟa moḻikaṭkup pintiyatākak kāṭṭumeṉṟum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷi yellām tamiḻeḻut tilēyē tiritteḻututal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, allākkāl tamiḻ nāḷaṭivil vēṟu moḻiyāka māṟiviṭumeṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum. āytavolikkut tamiḻolikaḷi ayalolikaḷāka māṟṟum mantira vāṟṟal illi. oliyip poṟuttamaṭṭil, āytavarivaṭiveḻutuvatum ayaṉmoḻi varivaṭiveḻutu vatum oṉṟē. eḻutteṉpatu uṇmiyil oliyēyaṉṟi varivaṭivaṉṟu.
tamiḻ mikattoṉmiyum muṉmiyum meṉmiyum vāynta moḻiyātaliṉ, ataṉ valliṉameykaḷum piṟamoḻi valliṉa meykaḷi nōkka melvalliṉamē. itik kālṭuvelārum nōkkātu, tamiḻ valliṉatti vaṭamoḻi aivakuppiṉ mutal varicik koppākak koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. tamiḻ valliṉameykaḷ iraṭṭittālaṉṟi āriya valliṉameykku īṭākā.

mēlum, tamiḻ valliṉameykaḷ polivolikaḷum (Voiced) ākā, piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ polivolikkum poliyā volikkum (Voiceless) iṭippaṭṭatē tamiḻ valliṉavoli. atu iraṭṭitta viṭattē naṉṟāy valittum, taṉittuvariṉ, coṉmutalil oli viḻuttattiṉāl (Accent) caṟṟē valittum, colliṭiyiluṅ kaṭiyilum melliṉa meyyi aṭuppiṉum aṭukkāviṭiṉum polintum, olikkum. ivviyalpi nelli māvaṭṭattu nāṭṭup puṟa makkaḷ pēcciltāṉ naṉṟāyk kāṇa muṭiyum.
ṟṟa, ṉṟa eṉṉum iṇi meyyolikaḷi, muṟiyē, tt, nd eṉṉum āṅkila meyyiṇi pōlap piḷaviṉṟi yolippatē tamiḻmarapām, aṅṅaṉa maṉṟip pirāmaṇarip piṉpaṟṟi, ttr, ntr eṉa vitiroliyuṭaṉ (trill) piḷavupaṭap palukkuvatu vaḻuvām.
pirāṉmēyar (Fronhmeyer) eḻutiya paṭimuṟi maliyāḷa ilakkaṇam (A Progressive Grammar of the Malayalam Language) eṉṉum nūliṉ 9 ām pakkattil kūṟiyiruppatik kavaṉikka. paṇṭic cēranāṭṭut tamiḻē āriyattōṭu cērntu maliyāḷamākat tirittiruppatāl, maliyāḷa ṟakarattiṟkuc coṉṉatu tamiḻ ṟakarattiṟkum okkum.
ṟakaram taṉittum iraṭṭiyum ṉakarameyyaṭuttum varum mūvēṟu nilimi paṟṟi, mūvēṟu olikaḷi aṭiyum.
1. 	ṟa
	itu valiya allatu muraṭṭu rakaram. itaṟkoppāṉatu āṅkilatti lilli.
2. 	ṟṟa
	itu sit eṉpatilumuḷḷa t pōl, allatu sitting eṉpatiluḷḷa tt pōl olippatu.
3. 	ṉṟa
	itu send eṉpatiluḷḷa nd pōl olippatu.
ṅka, ñca, ṇṭa, nta, mpa, ṉṟa eṉṉum melivaliyiṇikaḷē tamiḻiluṇṭu, ṅkka (nk), ñcca (nc), ṇṭṭa (nt), ntta (nth) mppa (mp) ṉṟṟa (nt) eṉṉum vaṉmelivaliyiṇikaḷ tamiḻil nikaḻa immiyum iṭamilli.
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali, tamiḻ iyalpi aṟiyātu, āṅkila varipeyarppil (Transliteration) koṭu eṉpatik koṭṭu eṉṟum, pāmpu eṉpatip pāmpu eṉṟum olikkumāṟu tavaṟākak kuṟittiruppatiṉāl, atip piṉpaṟṟiya parō-emeṉō tiraviṭa oppiyal akaramutaliyum aṅṅaṉam vaḻuppaṭak kuṟittuḷḷatu.
munnili yeḻuttukkaḷ
tamiḻ ulakap peru moḻikaṭkuḷ mika muntit tōṉṟiya melliya iyaṉmoḻiyātalāl. atiṟ piṟamoḻikaḷiṟ pōl ellā eḻuttukkaḷum coṉ mutaliṭi kaṭiyākiya mūviṭattum varuvatilli. 
mutaṉiliyeḻuttukkaḷ 
coṉmutalil uyirum uyirmeyyuntāṉ varum; āytamum meyyum varavē varā.
ṅa, ṭa, ṇa, ra, la, ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum oṉpatumeyyum uyirōṭu kūṭi uyirmeyyākavum coṉmutal varā. uyirōṭu kūṭic coṉmutal varum meykaḷuḷḷum, ka ta na pa ma eṉṉum aintē paṉṉīruyiroṭuṅ kūṭivarum. ēṉiyavaṟṟuḷ, ñakaram nāluyiroṭum, yakaram īruyiroṭum, vakaram eṭṭuyiroṭum, kūṭivarum. ēṉiyavaṟṟuḷ, ñakaram nāluyiroṭum, yakaram īruyiroṭum, vakaram eṭṭuyiroṭum, kūṭivarum, aviyāvaṉa:
ña, ñā, ñe, ño, ya (yavaṉar), yā : va, vā, vi, vī, ve, vē, vi, vu.
iṭiniliyeḻuttukkaḷ
colliṭiyil āytamum meyyum uyirmeyyumē varum; taṉiyuyir varātu. uyiruḷ, kuṟilmaṭṭum aḷapeṭik kuṟiyāka varum.
k, c, t, p ākiya nāl valliṉa meykaṭkuppiṉ, taṉṉuyir meyyēyaṉṟi vēṟṟuyirmey varātu. itu uṭaṉili meym mayakkam eṉappaṭum. piṟa meykaṭkuppiṉ piṟavuyirmeykaḷ cilavum palavum varum. itu vēṟṟunili meymmayakkam eṉappaṭum. mayakkam kūṭṭam.
r, ḻ ākiya irumeyyum iraṭṭā; ēṉiya iraṭṭum.
y, r, ḻ; ākiya mummeykkuppiṉ taṉimey varalām. piṟavaṟṟiṉ piṉ varā.
e.ṭu. vāyppu, tērcci, cīḻkki.
mra, mla; vra, vla eṉṉum kūṭṭukkaḷ tamiḻil evvakiyilum nikaḻā.
ṇña, ṉña; ñya, ṇya, nya, mya, vya, ṉya; mva eṉṉuṅ kūṭṭukkaḷ kūṭṭuc colliṭiyilaṉṟi varā.
iṟutiniliyeḻuttukkaḷ
āytamum valliṉameyyum colliṟutiyil varavē varā. piṟa meykaḷum uyirmeyyumtāṉ varum.
nāi (nāy) eṉṟu eḻutap peṟṟa paṇṭi vaḻakku iṉṟilli. uyirkkuṟil colliṭiyiṟpōṉṟē colliṟutiyilum aḷapeṭik kuṟiyāy varum.
r, ḻ, eṉpaṉa taṉikkuṟilukkuppiṉ collīṟāy varuvatilli.
tamiḻil eccollilum evviṭattilum piṟamoḻi yeḻuttum eḻuttumuṟiyum varutal kūṭātu. piṟamoḻic col iṉṟiyamiyātatum moḻipeyarkka muṭiyātatumāyiṉ, tamiḻ muṟikkēṟpat tiritteḻutap peṟum. iṅṅaṉam varampīṭu ceyyākkāl, tamiḻ nāḷaṭivil vēṟu moḻiyāy 

māṟiviṭum. itaṉālēyē vaṭacollic ceyyuṭ colvaki nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟākak kuṟitta tolkāppiyarum, muṉṉōriṭṭa varampi mīṟātu.

viḻuttam (Accent)
oru colliṉ ōr aciyil viḻum oliyuṟippu viḻuttam eṉappaṭum. tamiḻil viḻuttam (1) iyalpuviḻuttam, (2) coṟpiri viḻuttam (3) colvaki viḻuttam eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
(1) iyalpuviḻuttam
iyalpu viḻuttam iyalpāka nikaḻvatu; atu eṉṟum colliṉ mutalil viḻum.
e.ṭu. koṟṟaṉ, vantāṉ
oliyuṟippu varavarak kuṟiyumātalāl, coṉmutalil eṭuttalum (Acute), iṭiyil nalitalum (Circumflex), iṟutiyiṟ (Grave), paṭuttalum  nikaḻum.

aḻuttam (Stress or Emphasis)

mūvakiccol
mutaṟkālattil ilakkaṇavakiccol peyar, viṉi, iṭi eṉa mūṉṟākavē yiruntaṉa. ituvē ellāmoḻi kaṭkum potuvāṉa aṟiviyaṉ muṟip pākupāṭām.
nālvakiccol
āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻ kaṟṟut tamiḻāciriyarāṉa piṉ, iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol eṉṉum moḻi iyalvakic coṟkaḷoṭu vaṭacolliyum cērttuk koṇṭatu pōṉṟē, peyarccol, viṉiccol, iṭiccol eṉṉum ilakkaṇavakic coṟkaḷoṭum uriccol eṉpatic cērttu, iru vakiyilum coṟkaḷi nannāṉ kākkiṉar.
uriccol eṉpatu ceyyuṭkēyuriya col. col eṉpatu colvaṭiviyuṅ kuṟikkum. āriyar ayalārāṉatiṉāl, tamakkut teriyāta aruñcoṟkaḷit tokuttup poruḷ urittuk koṇṭaṉar.
1. peyarccol
oru poruḷiṉ peyarē peyarccol, peyvatupeyar, peytal iṭutal peyariṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
peyarccol, poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉṉum aṟuporuḷ paṟṟi, aṟuvakippaṭum. irutiṇi paṟṟi, uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi, viravuttiṇi eṉa mūvakippaṭum; mūviṭam paṟṟi, taṉmi, muṉṉili, paṭarkki eṉa mūvakippaṭum. ciṉi = uṟuppu.
irutiṇikkum potuvāṉa peyar viravuttiṇip peyar allatu viravuppeyar eṉappaṭum.
v.L.: āṇ, peṇ, tāy, piḷḷi.
taṉmi muṉṉilippeyarkaḷ, eṇ paṟṟi orumi paṉmi eṉa iruvakippaṭum. paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ, pāl paṟṟi āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aivakippaṭum.

mutaṟkālattil ol eṉpatē viṉiyākavumiruntatu. iṭi-viṉi peyar eṉṉum immuṟi ellāccoṟkum taṉippaṭṭa niliyil ottataṉṟu. cila iṭiccoṟkaḷ nēraṭiyāyp peyarākiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. ella (ellā) - eluva - eluvaṉ (tōḻaṉ), eluvi (tōḻi).
ellā eṉṉum viḷiyoli iṉṟu ēla, ēlā eṉṟu vaṭivilum poruḷilum tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
cila coṟkaḷ mutalaṭiyilēyē peyarākavō viṉiyākavō tōṉṟi viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. kākā- kākam, kākkā - kākki (peyar) kara - kari (viṉi).
ivvirucollum āṅkilattil nērmāṟāy āḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nām kākam karikiṟatu eṉkiṟōm, āṅkilarō ‘The crow caws’ eṉkiṉṟaṉar.
cila viṉiccoṟkaḷ peyariṉiṉṟē piṟakkiṉṟaṉa.

naṉ eṉpatu iṟṟittamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉālum teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu.
naṅkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṟum tamiḻil ceyyuḷ vaḻakkāyiṟṟu; 
naṅkaḷ itaṉ vantēṉ
yāṅkaḷ eṉṉum taṉittaṉmip paṉmip peyar vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉatiṉāl, iṉṟu ataṉ iṭattil nāṅkaḷ eṉṉum uḷappāṭṭut taṉmip paṉmippeyar taṉ uḷappāṭṭup poruḷiyiḻantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ē eṉpatu uyarcci kuṟittalāl, iyalpāka māntaṉukkuḷḷa nāṉ eṉṉum cerukku allatu taṉṉalam paṟṟi, ati aṭiyākak koṇṭu taṉmip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyirukkalām. vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇiyar taṉmiyiṭatti uttama puruṭaṉ eṉṟum, āṅkilar ataṉi First Person eṉṟum, kuṟittatiyum nōkkuka.
ēpeṟ ṟākum.  (tol. uri. 7)
ekku, eṭṭu, eṇ, empu, evvu, eḻu, ēṇ, ēntu, ēr, ēṟu mutaliya coṟkaḷil ekara ēkāram uyarcci kuṟittal kāṇka.
iṉṟu vaḻakkilillāta vaṭivaṅkaḷellām paṇṭit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉa aṟika.
muṉṉilip peyar
	orumi paṉmi		iraṭṭippaṉmi
mutalnili	(ūṉ)	(ūm)		(ūṅkaḷ)
2-ām nili 	(nūṉ) 	(nūm) 		(nūṅkaḷ)
3-ām nili 	nīṉ 	nīm 		nīṅkaḷ
4-ām nili 	nī 	nīyi (nī+ir)	nīṅkaḷ
piṟikkōṭṭiluḷḷavi vaḻakku vīḻntaṉa.
nī eṉpatu nīṉ eṉpataṉ kaṭikkuṟi. atu ir īṟupeṟṟa nīyir eṉṟākip piṉpu nīvir eṉat tirintu, nīr eṉat tokkatu.
nīṉ, nīm eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷ iṉṟum nelli vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
muṉṉilip peyariṉ vēṟṟumi yaṭikaḷ :
(ūṉ) - uṉ 		(ūm) - um 	(ūṅkaḷ) - uṅkaḷ
(nūṉ) - (nuṉ) 		(nūm) - num 	(nūṅkaḷ) - nuṅkaḷ
nīṉ-niṉ 		nīm - (nim) 	nīṅkaḷ - (niṅkaḷ)
iṉṟu 4-ām nili eḻuvāy vaṭivaṅkaṭkum mutal nili vēṟṟumiyaṭikaḷē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ ū eṉṉum muṉmic cuṭṭaṭiyāyt tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
mutaṟkālap paṭarkkic cuṭṭuppeyar
		orumi 		paṉmi		iraṭṭippaṉmi
mutalnili (āṉ)  		(ām)  		(āṅkaḷ)
2 ām nili tāṉ 		tām 		tāṅkaḷ
paṭarkkip pakarappeyariṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭikaḷ
tāṉ-taṉ 			tām-tam 		tāṅkaḷ-taṅkaḷ
ā eṉṉum cēymic cuṭṭaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantuḷḷa tāṉ, tām eṉṉum paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ, avaṉ, avaḷ mutaliya aimpāṟ cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyapiṉ, taṟcuṭṭup peyarkaḷāy (Reflexive Pronouns) māṟiviṭṭaṉa. (Auto) eṉṉum kirēkkaccollum, mutaliṟ cuṭṭup peyarāyiruntu piṉpu taṟcuṭṭup peyarāy māṟiyuḷḷami iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ikkālap paṭarkkic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ (Demonstrative Pronouns)
cēymic cuṭṭup peyar : avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi
aṇmic cuṭṭup peyar : ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivi
muṉmic cuṭṭup peyar : uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvi
ivi av, iv, uv; al, il, ul; eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭikaḷi ṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyaviyākum.
al-atu, il-itu, ul-utu.
ukarac cuṭṭaṭip peyarkaḷ iṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟaṉa; āyiṉ, yāḻppāṇattil vaḻaṅkuvatākat terikiṉṟatu.
avarkaḷ, ivarkaḷ uvarkaḷ eṉpaṉa iraṭṭip paṉmi vaṭivaṅkaḷākum.
makkaḷ nākarikamaṭintapiṉ, akavi aṟivu atikāram mutaliyavaṟṟil iḻintōṉik kuṟikka orumippeyariyum, ottōṉik kuṟikkap paṉmip peyariyum, uyarntōṉik kuṟikka iraṭṭip paṉmippeyariyum, muṉṉiliyilum paṭarkkiyilum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. itu ulakavaḻakkēyaṉṟic ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaṉṟu. taṉmiyiṭattiṟku iraṭṭippaṉmi vēṇṭāviṭiṉum, oppumi paṟṟi atilum amintuḷḷatu.
mēṟkāṭṭiya mūviṭappeyarkaḷuḷ, taṉmiyum muṉṉiliyum (aimpāṟ cuṭṭumpeyar tavira ēṉiyavellām), ṉakara meyyīṟṟāl orumiyiyum makara meyyīṟṟāṟ paṉmiyiyum, oḻuṅkāy uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ivvīṟukaḷ tōṉṟiya vaki varumāṟu:
ollutal = poruntutal, oṉṟātal.
ol - oṉ = oṉṟu, oṉ - aṉ, o. nō : E one-an.
oṉ eṉṉuñ collē aṉ eṉṟu tirintu yāṉ (nāṉ), nīṉ, tāṉ, eṉṉum peyarkaḷil ṉakara meyyaḷavāka niṉṟu orumi yuṇarttukiṉṟatu. aṉṉīṟu uyiroṭu puṇariṉ mutal keṭum.
ē + aṉ = ēṉ, ū - aṉ = ūṉ, ā + aṉ = āṉ.
o.nō: maka + aṉ = makaṉ. kō + aṉ = kōṉ (kōvaṉ)
ummutal kūṭutal, um eṉṉum viṉi iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟu, ataṉ aḷḷit tiripāṉa am eṉṉum collum, ataṉ vaḻiyaṭiyāṉa kum eṉṉuñ collumē vaḻakkiluḷḷaṉa.
am-amal - amali = tiraḷi. amal-amar. amartal = poruntutal. am-ami. amital = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal, am-ampu-ampal = kuvital, kuviyum mukiḻ. ampal - ampalam = avi, kūṭṭam, maṉṟam.
ma. ampalam, ka. ampala, tu. ampila.
ampalam - va. ampara. ciṟṟampalam - va. citampara (Cidambara)
um - kum. kummutal = kūṭutal. kum - kummal. kum - kumpu - kum pal.
um eṉṉum col kūṭutalik kuṟittataṉālēyē, atu eṇṇupporuḷ allatu kūṭṭupporuḷiṭic collākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
e.ṭu. eḻuttum collum poruḷum.
nāṉum varuvēṉ = nāṉ kūṭa varuvēṉ, iti oru ciṟu piḷḷiyum ceytuviṭum = iti oru ciṟu piḷḷi kūṭac ceytuviṭum.
ivvaḻakkukkaḷil, ummic collum kūṭaṟcollum muṟṟum poruḷottiruttal kāṇka.
ivvummic collē, yām, nīm, tām eṉṉum peyarkaḷil makarameyyaḷavāka niṉṟu paṉmiyuṇarttukiṉṟatu. kālṭuvelār karuttum ituvē. ammīṟu pōṉṟē ummīṟum, uyiroṭu puṇariṉ mutal keṭum.
ē + am = ēm, ū + am = ūm, ā + am = ām
o.nō : maṇa + am = maṇam, kā + am = kām (āci)
mūvakic cuṭṭuppeyar muṟiyilēyē, e, ē, yā, eṉṉum viṉāvaṭi kaḷiṉiṉṟu aimpāl viṉāppeyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
viṉāppeyarkaḷ (Interrogative Pronouns)
e : evaṉ, evaḷ, evar (evarkaḷ), etu, evi
ē :  ēvaṉ, ēvaḷ, ēvar (ēvarkaḷ), ētu, ēvi
yā : yāvaṉ,. yāvaḷ, yāvar (yāvarkaḷ), yātu, yāvi
ē-e : ē-yā, e-ev, e-el-etu-ettu-entu. ē-ēl-ētu.
uyartiṇi āṇpālik kuṟikkum evaṉ eṉṉum viṉāppeyarum, aḵṟiṇi yirupāliyum uṇarttum evaṉ eṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, vevvēṟām.
ētu eṉṉum col, ‘eppaṭik kiṭittatu?  eṉṉum poruḷil kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟākavum varum.
e.ṭu. uṉakku itu ētu?
etu eṉṉum col ciṟitu aṟiyappaṭṭa poruḷ paṟṟiyum, yātu eṉṉum col ciṟitum aṟiyappaṭāta poruḷ paṟṟiyum viṉāvāka varum.
e.ṭu. kutirivāli yātu? - ciṟitum aṟiyappaṭātatu. varaku ikkūlaṅkaḷuḷ etu? - ciṟitu aṟiyap paṭṭatu.
yāvar eṉṉuñ col, yār eṉat tokku uyartiṇi muppāliyuṅ kuṟippatuṭaṉ, ulaka vaḻakkil ār eṉṟum maruvum.
yā eṉṉum viṉāvaṭi, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟ palaviṉpāl viṉāp peyarākavum vaḻaṅkum.
el-eṉ-eṉṉatu (orumi), eṉṉa (paṉmi).
ēl-ēṉ.
āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉāa.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (32) kūṟuvatāl, viṉāveḻuttu mutaṟkālattil neṭilākavē yiruntateṉpatum, atu ēkāramē yeṉpatum, uyttuṇarappaṭum.
ē-yā-ā-ō
yā colliṉ mutalilum, ā-ō colliṉ īṟṟilum, ē avvīriṭattum, viṉāvāka varum.
oru poruḷip paṟṟi etu eṉṟu viṉavuvatu, ōriṭattiluḷḷa pala poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟi eṭuttukkāṭṭac colvatu pōliruttalāl, eḻucciyi allatu uyarcciyi uṇarttum ēkāram viṉāveḻuttākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
paṭarkkippeyarp pākupāṭukaḷ
taṉmiyum muṉṉiliyumaṉṟip paṭarvatu paṭarkki paṭartal = parantu cellutal.
aṟuporuṭpeyar
ellāp poruḷkaḷum, poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉa aṟuvakippaṭum. ātalāl avaṟṟiṉ peyarum aṟuvakiyām.
poruḷ eṉṟatu. kāṭcipporuḷākavō karuttup poruḷākavō uḷḷa oru taṉipporuḷi.
ētēṉum oru poruḷiruppiṉ, atu irukka iṭamum kālamum vēṇṭum. poruḷkaḷ, iyaṟkiyāyiṉum ceyaṟkiyāyiṉum, perumpālum pala vuṟuppukaḷi yuṭiyaṉa. ovvoru poruṭkum cila taṉmikaḷuṇṭu. attaṉmikaḷē uṟuppiyum uṭaliyum tuṇikkoṇṭu toḻilāka veḷippaṭum. iṅṅaṉam āyntu poruḷkaḷi āṟāka vakuttatum avaṟṟi muṟippaṭuttiyatum, viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉivaṉāṉa mutaṉūlā ciriyaṉiṉ, meypporuḷaṟiviyum tarukkat tiṟaṉiyum teḷḷitiṟ kāṭṭum.
poruṭpeyar iyalvariyaṟi
pol-poru-poruḷ.
kuṟiyīṭṭip poṟuttamaṭṭil, kuṇam eṉiṉum paṇpu eṉiṉum okkum.
muttiṇip peyar
uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi, avviraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉa viravuttiṇi eṉat tiṇi mūvakippaṭum.
e.ṭu. 	uyartiṇippeyar - avaṉ, māntaṉ, murukaṉ 
		aḵṟiṇippeyar - atu, maram, kal
		viravuttiṇippeyar - tāy, piḷḷi, āṇ, peṇ
		tiṇ - tiṇi = tiraṭci, tokuti, vakuppu.
aimpāṟpeyar
āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl eṉa uyartiṇippāl mūṉṟum, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aḵṟiṇippāl iraṇṭum ākap pāl aintām.
paku-pakal-pāl = pirivu.
āṇpāl, peṇpāl eṉṉum iraṇṭum orumip pālkaḷ, āṇpeṇ vēṟu aḵṟiṇiyuyirikaḷuḷḷum iruppiṉum, mutuṉūlāciriyaṉ ataṟku ilakkaṇattil iṭantaravilli. ēṉeṉiṉ, moḻiyi amitta potu makkaḷē aṅṅaṉam amittuviṭṭaṉar. ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyamputalē nūlāciriyaṉ ceyal.
kumarikkaṇṭap potumakkaḷ kūrmatiyum uyariya oḻukkamum uṭiyarātaliṉ, nallatik kaṭippiṭittut tīyati nīkkum nākarikap paṇpāṭṭiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta pakuttaṟivi aḷavi yākak koṇṭu, atiyuṭiya makkaḷi uyartiṇiyeṉṟum, aḵtillātavaṟṟi yellām uyiriruppiṉum illāviṭiṉum aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟum vakuttu, ataṟkēṟpa, makaṉ varukiṟāṉ, makaḷ varukiṟāḷ eṉṟu uyartiṇiyiṟ pāl pirittum, kāḷi varukiṟatu, āvu varukiṟatu eṉṟu aḵṟiṇiyiṟ pāl pirikkātum, ellāp peyarc coṟkaḷiyum viṉic coṟkaḷiyum amittuviṭṭami, eṉṟum tamiḻaṉ eṇṇi makiḻtaṟkum taṉ muṉṉōrip paṟṟip 
perumi pārāṭṭutaṟkum uriyatoṉṟām. iṅṅaṉam vēṟēm moḻiyilu millāta ciṟanta ilakkaṇa vamippu, iṟṟip pula makkaḷiṉum aṟṟip potumakkaḷ aṟivāṟṟalilum nākarikap paṇpāṭṭilum cālac ciṟantava reṉpataṟkut takka moḻiccāṉṟām.

mutumakaḷ
muṟippeyar (Name of Relationship)
tanti - appaṉ (tamappaṉ-takappaṉ), attaṉ - accaṉ, āyaṉ - āñaṉ, āñā, āñāṉ - āñi, ai, aiyaṉ, tā, nāyaṉ, (nāyaṉār) appu.
tāy - ammi - avvi, aṉṉi - aññi, atti - ātti - ātti.
acci, ācci, aiyi - āy, taḷḷi.
makaṉ - putalvaṉ.
makaḷ - putalvi.
maka(potu) - makavu, piḷḷi, cēy, matali, kāṉmuḷi (orumi); makkaḷ (paṉmi).
aṇṇaṉ - aiyaṉ (tamiyaṉ), āyāṉ, aṇṇāṭci (aṇṇātti), aṇṇāḷvi, tammuṉ, mūttōṉ, muṉṉōṉ.
tampi - tampiṉ - tampi, iḷaval, iḷiyāṉ, piṉṉōṉ. tampi maṉivi koḻunti.
akki - akkicci (akkiyacci), (tamakki). tavvi (tam avvi), tanti, mūttāḷ, muṉṉi, atti - acci, accaṉ.
taṅki-ki, taṅkicci (taṅkiyacci), ceḷḷi, iḷiyāḷ, iḷaṅkiḷi, iḷicci, piṉṉi, piṉṉi.
aṇṇaṉ tampi (potu) - uṭaṉ piṟantāṉ - uṭappiṟantāṉ, uṭaṉ vayiṟṟōṉ.
akki taṅki (potu) - uṭaṉ piṟantāḷ - uṭappiṟantāḷ, uṭaṉvayiṟṟōṉ.
aṇṇaṉ maṉivi - aṇṇi, āyanti (aiyaṉtēvi), naṅki, attācci.
akki kaṇavaṉ - attāṉ, māmaṉ.
marumakaṉ - marumāṉ, marukaṉ, maṇavāḷappiḷḷi.
marumakaḷ - marumāḷ, maruki, maṇāṭṭup peṇ.
peṇkoṭuttōṉ - māmaṉ.
māmaṉmaṉivi - māmi.
kaṇavaṉ - āṇmakaṉ, akamuṭiyāṉ, kaṇṇāḷaṉ, koḻunaṉ, koṇkaṉ, koṇṭāṉ, nāyakaṉ, maṇavāḷaṉ - maṇāḷaṉ, vīṭṭukkāraṉ.
maṉivi - akamuṭiyāḷ, illāḷ, nāyaki, peṇ-peṇṭu - peṇṭāṭṭi, kaṇṇāṭṭi, maṇavāṭṭi, vīṭṭukkāri.
kaṇavaṉ tampi - koḻuntaṉ, kaṇavaṉ tāy atti.
kaṇavaṉ taṅki - koḻunti.
kaṇavaṉ aṇṇaṉ - attāṉ, mūttār (maccāṇṭār)
kaṇavaṉ akki - nāttūṇ, nāttūṇāḷ, attācci.
maṉivi aṇṇaṉ - mūtta aḷiyaṉ, attāṉ (attimakaṉ, ammāṉ makaṉ)
maṉivi tampi - iḷiya aḷiyaṉ.
maṉivi akki - mūtta aḷiyāḷ, aṇṇi.
maṉivi taṅki - iḷiya aḷiyāḷ, koḻunti.
orakattāṉ - ōrkuṭi. maṇāḷaṉ, ōrkuṭiyiṟkoṇṭōṉ (cakalaṉ).
ōrakatti - ōrakattāḷ, ōrppaṭi, ōrppaṭiyāḷ, ōrppaṭicci.
tantiyaṇṇaṉ - periyappaṉ, mūttappaṉ, periyiyā.
tanti tampi - ciṉṉappaṉ, ciṟiyatakappaṉ, ciṉṉiyā, kuṭṭiyappaṉ kuñciyappaṉ.
tantiyuṭaṉ piṟantāḷ - atti (ciṉṉatti, periyatti), tāyiṉ aṇṇaṉ - periyammāṉ.

tampi - ciṉṉammāṉ, iḷiyammāṉ	ammāṇṭār
tāyiṉ tampi akki - periyammi, periyatāy, periyāy periyātti; ammāṉ maṉivi - ammāṉ tēvi - ammanti ammāmi (pirā), tammāmi, tantuvi, pāṭṭaṉuṭaṉ piṟantāḷ. attippāṭṭi appi - akki (yā).
tāyiṉ taṅki - ciṉṉāttāḷ, ciṉṉammi, ciṟiyatāy, ciṉṉāy, ceyyāḷ ciṟṟātti, tottā, citti (ciṟṟi, ciṟṟāy), piṉṉi, kuñciyātti, kuñci yācci (tantiyiṉ tampi maṉivi).
tantiyiṉ tanti - appaccaṉ, tātā, tāti, tāttā, mūttappaṉ.
tantiyiṉ tāy - appacci, appātti, appāttāḷ appatti, appāy.
tāyiṉ tanti - ammāccaṉ.
tāyiṉ tāy - ammācci, ammāy, amiñi, ammāttāy, āycci.
peṟṟōr tanti (potu) - pāṭṭaṉ, pōṟṟi - pōtti - mūttappaṉ.
peṟṟōr tāy (potu) - pāṭṭi, ācci, āy, (āyaḷ).
pāṭṭaṉ tanti - pūṭṭaṉ, (appāṭṭaṉ), koḷḷup pāṭṭaṉ koppāṭṭaṉ (koṭpāṭṭaṉ).
pāṭṭaṉ tāy-pūṭṭi, koḷḷuppāṭṭi (koṭpāṭṭi) kōppāṭṭi, pāṭṭaṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāḷ.
pūṭṭaṉ tanti - oṭṭaṉ, cīyāṉ (cēyāṉ).
pūṭṭaṉ tāy - ōṭṭi, cīyāḷ (cēyāḷ) appāṭṭaṉ.
makaṉ makaṉ		peyaraṉ - pēraṉ - pērāṇṭi
makaḷ makaṉ
makaṉ makaḷ		peyartti - pērtti
makaḷ makaḷ
pēraṉ makaṉ - koḷḷuppēraṉ, koṭpēraṉ.
pēraṉ makaḷ - koḷḷup pērtti, koṭpērtti.
cakkaḷatti (cakakkaḷatti) eṉṉum irupiṟappic colli (Hybrid) okkaḷatti eṉṟum, campanti eṉṉum vaṭacolli uṟavāṭi eṉṟum, moḻipeyarttuc collal vēṇṭum.
iyaṟpeyar (peṟṟōr iṭṭapeyar)
e.ṭu. aruṇmoḻittēvaṉ, tiruvaraṅkam, parimēlaḻakaṉ.
ciṟappup peyar
e.ṭu. kākkipāṭiṉi, makaṉi muṟiceytāṉ.
paṭṭappeyar
e.ṭu. toṇṭarcīrparavuvār, talikkōl (kaṇikiyar paṭṭam), kaliyiḷiñaṉ (B.A.) pulavaṉ.
virutup peyar
e.ṭu. mummuṭiccōḻaṉ, kaṅkikoṇṭāṉ.
pulamip peyar
e.ṭu. aṟiñaṉ, paṇṭitaṉ, pulavaṉ, nāvaṉ, pāvalaṉ
pakaṭippeyar (Nickname)
e.ṭu. ūṉpotipacuṅkuṭiyaṉ, narikuḷippāṭṭi (valakkāram mikkavaṉ).
kulappeyar
e.ṭu. vēḷāḷaṉ, pāṇaṉ.
kulappaṭṭappeyar
e.ṭu ceṭṭi nāṭāṉ, piḷḷi, mutali, itu ikkāla vaḻakku.
kuṭippeyar
e.ṭu. kuṟukkiyaṉ, cēkkiḻāṉ.
iṭa(nila) viyaṟpeyar
e.ṭu. tiṇinili - kuṉṟavaṉ, kāṭavaṉ, potuvaṉ, marutavāṇaṉ, nāṭṭunili - cōḻiyaṉ, koṅkaṉ, māṟōkkattāṉ, ūrnili - uṟantiyāṉ, kūṭalāṉ, nakaramāntar.
kālaviyaṟpeyar
e.ṭu. mūtiriyāṉ, vēṉilāṉ
talimip peyar
e.ṭu. tiṇinili - veṟpaṉ, kuṟumpoṟināṭaṉ, ūraṉ.
kulanili - ampalakāraṉ, nāṭṭāṇmik kāraṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ.
vāḻnilippeyar
e.ṭu. illaṟattāṉ, ilvāḻvāṉ, tuṟavi, aṭikaḷ, muṉivaṉ.
ciṉimutaṟpeyar
e.ṭu. karikālaṉ, peruntaliyaṉ.
uṭimiyiyaṟpeyar
e.ṭu. celvaṉ, nāṭukiḻavōṉ.
paṇpiyaṟpeyar
e.ṭu. aṉpaṉ, nallōr
toḻiliyaṟ peyar
e.ṭu. eḻuttāḷaṉ, taccaṉ, porunaṉ, kaṭunaṭiyaṉ.
mataviyaṟ peyar
e.ṭu. civaneṟiyaṉ, tirumālneṟiyaṉ, camaṇaṉ.
iṭappeyar 
e.ṭu.
kuṟiñci : ciṟukuṭi, kuṟicci, mali, kōṭu, kuṉṟu-kuṉṟam, kā, cōli, puḻi, kaṭavu, kaṇavāy.
mulli : pāṭi, cēri, paṭṭi, vāṭi, ānantal.
marutam : muṟkālam - ūr, ciṟṟūr, pērūr, mūtūr, puttūr, nallūr, āṟṟūr, kuḷattūr, cēṟṟūr, āmūr, il, kuṭi, iruppu, vāḻvu, vayal, paṇṇi, nāṭu, maṅkalam, kuḷam, ilañci, ēri, āṟu - āṟi, kiṇaṟu, ūruṇi, (nellūr, nelvēli, neṉmēṉi, neṟkōṭṭi).
piṟkālam - nakar - nakaram, eyil, kōṭṭi, puri, puram, kōvil - kōyil.
neytal : 
muṟkālam - kuppam, kaḻi, kāyal, kāṉal, kari, tuṟi, koṇku, cērppu.
piṟkālam - paṭṭiṉam, pākkam (alivāy, pukār, kūṭal).
pāli : kaṭam, kāṭu, kāṉam, kuṭikkāṭu, mutukuṭi, paṟantali, nattam, valaci.
mutaṟkālattil, ovvoru tiṇinilattilum ovvoru vakuppārē vāḻntiruntaṉar. marutanilattil, nāḷaṭivil, ūr virivum toḻiṟperukkamum kalviyaṟivum nākarikavaḷarcciyum vaṇika muṉṉēṟṟamum pēraracāṭciyum matil cūḻnta mānakara mippum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, nīr vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭu, pēriyāṟukaḷ kaṭalōṭu kalakkum kayavāyil paṭṭiṉam eṉṉum tuṟinakarkaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟaṉa. 
cila ciṟappuvaki yiṭappeyarkaḷ
urimi nilaṅkaḷ
kāṇi allatu paṟṟu-potuvūḻiyam ceytavar kuṭumpattiṟkum pōril iṟanta maṟavar kuṭumpattiṟkum, naṉkoṭiyākak koṭukkap peṟṟa muḻuvurimi nilam.
muṟṟūṭṭu - pulavar mutaliyōrkkuk koṭukkappeṟṟa muḻuvurimi nilam.
iṟiyili - varinīkkappeṟṟa māṉiyanilam.
aṟappuṟam - kalviccālikaṭkum maṭaṅkaṭkum viṭap peṟṟa iṟiyili.
aṭiciṟpuṟam - cōṟṟuccattirattiṟku viṭappeṟṟa iṟiyili.
kōyiṟpuṟam - kōyil vaḻipāṭṭiṟku viṭappeṟṟa iṟiyili.
paṇṭāravāṭi - kuṭikaḷukku urimiyāṉavūr.
cila vūrppeyarkaḷ
nattam - pōriṉāṟ pāḻāṉa vūr. valaci
kuṭiyēṟṟam - makkaḷ putitāyk kuṭiyēṟiya vūr.
āvaṭi - Deot.
taṅkumiṭaṅkaḷ
pācaṟi allatu kaṭṭūr - pōrkkaḷattiṟ paṭi maṟavarkkamitta ilikkuṭil tokuti (War-camp).
pāḷiyam - paṭikaḷuṭaṉ potumakkaḷum taṅkiyiruppatum kaṭit teruvuṭaṉ kūṭiyatumāṉa iṭam (Military Encampment).
paṭivīṭu - paṭimaṟavar niliyāka vatiyum iṭam (Cantoment).
tāvaḷam - ayalūril taṅkiyirukkumiṭam (Lodging).
nilappāṅkukaḷ
karical - kaḷimaṇ nilam.
cival - cemmaṇ nilam.
tēri - paranta kaṭaṟkari maṇal nilam.
kallāṅkuttu - vaṉṉilam.
murampu - kaṉṉila mēṭu (teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakku).
nāṭṭup pirivukaḷ
maṭappam - 500 ciṟṟūrkkut talimiyāṉavūr.
perumpirivu - maṇṭalam.
ciṟuperumpirivu - kōṭṭam, vaḷanāṭu.
ciṟupirivu - kūṟṟam, nāṭu.
kīḻcciṟupirivu - taṉiyūr, paṟṟu (pala ciṟṟūrt tokuti)
ciṟṟūr - uṭkiṭi.
kālappeyar
kōl - kāl - kālam.
viṭiyaliliruntu oru nāḷ pappattu nāḻiki koṇṭa āṟu ciṟupoḻutākavum, mēḻa (cittiri) mātattiliruntu ōr āṇṭu ivviru mātaṅkoṇṭa āṟu perumpoḻutākavum vakukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟupoḻutu
kāli, naṇpakal, eṟpāṭu (cāyuṅkālam), māli, yāmam, vikaṟi.
naṇpakal naṭuviṟku uccivēḷi, urumam eṉpaṉa tūya ulaka vaḻakkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷ. ucci vēḷikku muntiya pakal muṟpakal; pintiya pakal piṟpakal.
perumpoḻutu
iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār (maḻi), kūtir (kuḷir), muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi, vēṉil eṉpatu kōṭi.
ciṉippeyar (Name of Organs)
cil-ciṉ-ciṉi.
nilittiṇi : vēr, aṭi, kiḷi, ili, pū, kāy, kaṉi, mutaliyaṉa.
iyaṅkutiṇi : pātam, kāl, ari, vayiṟu, mārpu, kaḻuttu, tali mutaliyaṉa.
kuṇappeyar (Abstract Noun)
vaṇṇam, vaṭivu, aḷavu cuvi, ūṟu, nāṟṟam, iyal, ceyal eṉak kuṇam eṇvakippaṭum.
vaṇṇam
veḷḷi, karuppu, nīlam, pacci, mañcaḷ, civappu, kattari (Violet) eṉa vaṇṇam eḻuvaki.
nīr-nīl-nīlam = kaṭal nīriṉ niṟam. nīr = kaṭal, tali ciṟanta nīrniliyum piṟa nīrnilikaṭku mūlamumāyiruppatu kaṭalē.
nīrolit taṉṉa  (maturikkāñci, 569)
kāṉakkōḻiyu nīrniṟak kākkiyum  (cilap. 10:116)
kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ = tirumāl.
nīḷ-nīr, nilam niṟpatu; nīr nīḷvatu. kattari(ppū) eṉpatu ulakavaḻakku. cilar ati īral niṟam eṉpar. īral eṉpatu cuvaroṭṭi.
kāḷi vakiyil pukar (kapilam), pulli, mayili, karumayili mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum; kōḻivakiyil cāmpal, kamparici mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum, āṭivakiyil kempu, kāvi, nīrkkāvi, paḻukkāvi, (Orange) tuvar, kaḷippākku, veṅkāyam, māntuḷir, kiḷippacci, pācip payaṟu, īyam, iḷanīlam, mayiṟ kaḻuttu, vāṉavil, mukil vaṇṇam (mēkavarṇam) mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum, makkaḷ mēṉivakiyil taṅkam, pacali, putuniṟam, mā mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa.
kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar eḵkuc ceviyum kūrṅkaṇṇum nuṇ matiyum uṭiyarātaliṉ, vaṇṇaṅkaḷiyellām nuṭpamāy vakuttu avaṟṟiṟku vevvēṟu peyariṭṭiruntaṉar. paṇṭi yilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē iruntatiṉālum, aviyum aṭiyōṭu iṟantupaṭṭami yālum, pala vaṇṇap peyarkaḷum iṟantoḻintaṉa. iṉṟum mīṉpeyarkaḷil veṇṇiṟavakikaḷik kuṟikka, veḷḷi, veḷḷi, veṇṇey, veḷuvi mutaliya coṟkaḷ āḷappeṟṟiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
vaṭivu
vaṭṭam, cataram, mukkōṇam mutaliya parappu vaṭivukaḷum, uruṇṭi, uruḷi, kūmpu mutaliya kaṉa vaṭivukaḷum, palvakiya, vaṭṭam cataram eṉpavi teṉcoṟkaḷē.
aḷavu
eṇṇal, eṭuttal, mukattal, nīṭṭal eṉa aḷavi nālvaki.
eṇṇal
eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ ciṟṟilakkam (kīḻvāyilakkam), pērilakkam (mēlvāyilakkam) eṉa iruvakippaṭum. ciṟṟilakkamum kīḻvāy,mēlvāy eṉa irutiṟattatu.
