2. colliṭattu varuvaṉa
colliṭattuvarum iṭiccoṟkaḷ, (1) muṉṉoṭṭukkaḷ, (2) varici yiṭiccoṟkaḷ (3) iṇippuc coṟkaḷ (4) palporu ḷiṭiccoṟkaḷ (5) īṟukaḷ, (6) iṭinilikaḷ, (7) cāriyikaḷ, (8) vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ, eṉa eṇvakippaṭum.
1. muṉṉoṭṭukkaḷ (Prefixes)
e.ṭu.
al-a : avalam.
al : aḵṟiṇi, alvaḻi, aḵmoḻi.
miku - mī : mīkkūṟṟu, mīkcelavu, mīntōl.
nal - na : nakkīraṉ, nacceḷḷi, nattattaṉ, nappacali.
akavalippaṭuttutal, iṭiccerukal, uṭkōḷ, uṭaṉpiṟappu, uḻitarutal, uḻiccelvāṉ, ūṭuruval, kaṭittēṟal, kīḻppaṭital, talikkūṭal, naṭuttīrppu, piṟpōkku, puṟaṅkūṟṟu, maṟumuḷpāytal, muṉṉēṟṟam, mēṟpārvi, vaḻimoḻital, veḷiyiṭutal eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ mutaliluḷḷa akam iṭi, uḷ, uṭaṉ, uḻi, uḻi, ūṭu, kaṭi, kīḻ, tali, naṭu, piṉ, puṟam, maṟu, muṉ, mēl, vaḻi, veḷi eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭukkaḷum iṭiccollāka āḷappeṟṟa piṟacoṟkaḷē. ivaṟṟiṉ nēr āṅkila allatu āriyaccoṟkaḷ perumpālum tūya muṉṉoṭṭuk kaḷāyiruttal kāṇka. āriyaccoṟkaḷ tiricoṟkaḷātaliṉ, tōṟṟam maṟintuḷḷaṉa; tamiḻccoṟkaḷ ellām iyaṟcoṟkaḷātaliṉ, tōṟṟanteḷivāyum tamittu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa vāyumuḷḷaṉa. ituvē ivitammuḷ vēṟṟumi.
2. varici yiṭiccoṟkaḷ
ām-oṉṟām, mutalām, nūṟām.
āvatu - oṉṟāvatu, nūṟāvatu, āyirattāvatu.
3. iṇippuccoṟkaḷ (Conjunctions)
aṭukkiṇippuc coṟkaḷ
um-aṟamum poruḷum iṉpamum vīṭum eṉa uṟutip poruḷ nāṉku.
ē-eḻuttē aciyē cīrē taḷiyē aṭiyē toṭiyē eṉac ceyyuḷuṟuppukkaḷ āṟu.
maṟunili yiṇippuc coṟkaḷ :
e.ṭu. āyiṉum, ākilum - 
vīṭākilum maṉiyākilum uṭaṉē vāṅkiyāka vēṇṭum.
ātal - pulavar ciṉṉāṇṭārātal pulavar kantāṇṭārātal pukivaṇṭi niliyattil uṅkaḷoṭu talikkūṭuvār.
āvatu - oruvaṉukkut taṉmatiyāvatu coṉmatiyāvatu iruttal vēṇṭum.
eṉiṉum - celvareṉiṉum vaṟiyavareṉiṉum ic cattirattil vantu taṅkalām.
eṉiṉum - ēṉum
centamiḻc celvik kuk kaṭṭuriyēṉum ceyyuḷēṉum eḻuti viṭukka.
o-iṉṟō nāḷiyō maḻi tappātu varum.
āyiṉum, ākilum, ātal, āvatu, ēṉum eṉṉum iṇippuc coṟkaḷ, yār, ētu mutaliya viṉāppeyariṉ piṉ variṉ, orē muṟi amiyum.
e.ṭu.	yārākilum oruvar varuka.
		ētēṉum oṉṟu koṭu.
uṭaṉuṟa viṇippucoṟkaḷ (Correlative Conjunctions).
maṭṭumaṉṟu ..... um. eṭu. ikkālattil oruvar ēntāka (vacatiyāka) vāḻavēṇṭumeṉiṉ tamiḻi maṭṭu maṉṟu, āṅkilattiyum kaṟka vēṇṭum.
āka eṉṉum col nēralkūṟṟi (Indirect Speech) muṭikkuñ collōṭu iṇikkum.
e.ṭu. avaṉ nāḷi varuvatākac coṉṉāṉ.

4. palporu ḷiṭiccoṟkaḷ
ā.
itu cuṭṭu, viṉā, etirmaṟi, irakkam, viyappu, nōvu, viḷi mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum.
um.
itu iṇippu, uyarvu, iḻivu, etirmaṟi, eccam, muṟṟu, ukappu (choice) iciniṟi mutaliya palaporuḷkaḷi uṇarttum.
urinaṭikkum ciṟupāṉmi iciniṟi vēṇṭappeṟum.
e.ṭu. perumpālum, potuvuṭimikkārar kaṭavuḷ nampikki yillātavarē.
taṉmakaṉ kalittalivaṉ tērvil mutaṟṟaramāyt tēṟiṉā ṉeṉṟu kēḷvippaṭṭa tāy, avaṉip peṟṟa poḻutiṉum peritum makiḻntāḷ.
ē.
itu viṉā, pirinili, tēṟṟam. iṇippu iciniṟi, īṟṟaci, viḷi mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum.
ērōtu aracaṉ kālattilē, yūtēyānāṭṭilē, pettalakēm eṉṉum ciṟṟūrilē, oru māṭṭut toḻuvattilē, iyēcu perumāṉ piṟantār. itil iciniṟi vantami kāṇka.
ō.
itu viṉā, etirmaṟi, pirinili, maṟunili, aiyam, irakkam, viyappu, iḻivu, oḻiyici, viḷi mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum.
5. palvaki ilakkaṇa vīṟukaḷ
taṉmippeya rīṟukaḷ
orumi : oṉ-ṉ
paṉmi : um-m, m(um) + kaḷ
muṉṉilippeya ḻīṟukaḷ
orumi : oṉ-ṉ, (nīṉ-nī)
paṉmi : um-m, m(um) + kaḷ
              (nī + ir = nīyir-nīvir, nīr)
paṭarkkippeya rīṟukaḷ
āṉpālīṟukaḷ
āḷ eṉṉum uyartiṇiccol, āḷkiṟavaṉ eṉṉum karuttil āṇpāliyum, āḷappaṭukiṟavaḷ eṉṉum karuttil peṇpāliyum uṇarttum.
āṇpāli yuṇarttum iṭaṅkaḷ :
1. āṭavaṉ nallā ḷilā takuṭi (kuṟaḷ. 1030)
2. tiṟavōṉ. āḷallāṉ celvakkuṭiyuṭ piṟattalum (tiri. 7).
3. pōrmaṟavaṉ piṇampiṟaṅka āḷeṟintu (pu.ve. 2, 7)
4. kālāḷ. āḷveḷḷam pōkavum (pu.ve. 7,13)
5. kaṇavaṉ. āḷillā maṅkik kaḻaku (vākkuṇṭām, 3)
āḷaṉ   =	1. āḷpavaṉ
2. kaṇavaṉ. āḷaṉ illāta tukkam aḻutālum 
		  tīrātu.
peṇṭāḷutal eṉṉum vaḻakkāṟu, maṉivi kaṇavaṉāl āḷap peṟutali uṇarttum.
āḷ eṉṉuñ col pāṟpotumi nīṅki āṭavaṉik kuṟittaṟku, āṇ eṉṟu tirintatu. āṇ eṉṉum collē, āṉ eṉṟu tirintu āṇpālīṟāṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. ṇakaram vaṭatiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ potuvāka ṉakaramākat tirivatāl, āṇ eṉṉum col aṅku āṉ eṉṟutāṉ irukkum, āṉīṟu piṉṉar aṉ eṉṟu kuṟukiṟṟu.
e.ṭu. avaṉ, celvaṉ.
aṉṉīṟu piṉpu al eṉat tirintatu. ṉakara meyyīṟu lakara meyyīṟākat tirital iyalpē.
e.ṭu. tiṟam - tiṟaṉ - tiṟal, ceyveṉ - ceyvaṉ - ceyval, āl - āṉ (3-ām vē.u.). mēl - mēṉ.
aṇṇal, iḷaval, cemmal, vaḷḷal mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ allīṟu āṇpālīṟē. kuricil eṉpatu kurucal eṉpataṉ tiripāyirukkalām. o.nō. parical - paricil. āṉīṟu oṉ eṉṟum tiriyum.
e.ṭu. muṉṉāṉ - muṉṉōṉ.
māntaṉiyum piḷḷiyiyum kuṟikkum maka eṉṉum col aṉṉīṟupeṟṟu makaṉ eṉṟākum. atuvum ōr āṇ pālīṟām.
e.ṭu. perumakaṉ, tirumakaṉ, turimakaṉ.
makaṉ eṉṉum īṟu māṉ eṉṟu maruvum.
e.ṭu. perumakaṉ - perumāṉ, tirumakaṉ - tirumāṉ. marumakaṉ - marumāṉ.
māṉ īṟu maṉ eṉṟu kuṟukum.
e.ṭu. vaṭamaṉ.
aṉṉīṟu peṟṟa appaṉ, aiyaṉ, aṇṇaṉ eṉṉum muṟip peyarkaḷum, arumi kuṟitta āṇpālīṟāka vaḻaṅkum.
e.ṭu. kaṇṇappaṉ, poṉṉiyaṉ, karuppaṇṇaṉ.
pāl tōṉṟāta mutaṟkālattil ikaravīṟu āṇpāl, peṇpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl ākiya orumippāl mūṉṟiyum uṇartti vantatu avvaḻakku iṉṟumuḷatu.
e.ṭu.	toḻilāḷi, viṟakuveṭṭi - āṇpāl
		kiḷavi, kayaṟkaṇṇi - peṇpāl
		maṇveṭṭi, kāṭikkaṇṇi - oṉṟaṉpāl.
aṉṉīṟu peṟṟa āḷaṉ eṉṉum collum, ikaravīṟu peṟṟa āḷi eṉṉum collum āṇ pālīṟu pōṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
e.ṭu.	vēḷāḷaṉ, tāḷāḷaṉ,
		mutalāḷi, maliyāḷi.
iṉi, uṭimiyum urimiyum uṇarttum kāraṉ eṉṉum collum āṇpālīṟām.
e.ṭu. vīṭṭukkāraṉ, vaṇṭikkāraṉ.
tiyaṟkāraṉ, vēlikkāraṉ, koḷḷikkāraṉ, kūlikkāraṉ eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, tiyal vēlikkum vēli ceytaṟkum kūlit toḻilukkum uriyavaṉ allatu attoḻilkaḷi uṭiyavaṉ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum.
kaṭumi = mikuti, valimi. kaṭu-kaṭi-kari-kāram = kaṭumi, mikuti, valimi, atikāram, urimi. kāram - kāraṉ = urimiyāḷaṉ, uṭiyavaṉ.
vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa kru eṉṉum viṉiyaṭiyākap piṟantu, ceypavaṉik kuṟikkum kāra eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu kāraṉ eṉṉum āṇpālīṟu tirintatāka vaṭavar kūṟuvatu poruntātu. āṭṭukkāraṉ, kaṭikkāraṉ, kappaṟkāraṉ, kāyccaṟkāraṉ, kuṭikkāraṉ, kuruvikkāraṉ, kōḻikkāraṉ, contakkāraṉ, taṭṭukkāraṉ, tōṭṭakkāraṉ, nilattukkāraṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, paṇakkāraṉ, pāḷiyakkāraṉ, piḷḷikuṭṭikkāraṉ, puṉceykkāraṉ, puḷḷikkāraṉ, perumikkāraṉ, poṟāmik kāraṉ, māṭṭukkāraṉ, muṭṭikkāraṉ, veḷḷikkāraṉ mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta peyarkaṭku, urimip poruḷaṉṟic ceykipporuḷ ciṟitum poruntāmi kāṇka.
aṇṇakkāraṉ, tampikkāraṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷil kāraṉ eṉpatu muṟip peyariṉatāyum māṟiyuḷḷatu. ivvulaka vaḻakku ilakkiya naṭikku ēṟkātu.
peṇpālīṟukaḷ
āḷ eṉṉum col, āṭavaṉukku āṭpaṭukiṟavaḷ eṉṉum karuttil peṇpālīṟāyiṟṟu. cēranāṭṭut tamiḻiṉ tiripākiya maliyāḷattil, āḷ eṉṉuñ col annāṭṭu vaḻakkiṟ kēṟpa ōkāramutaṟ collāyt tirintu, maṉiviyik kuṟik kiṉṟatu. atu teluṅkil ālu eṉṟu tirintu peṇṇik kuṟik kiṉṟatu. kūmoḻiyilum iṅṅaṉamē ātalāl, paṇṭit tamiḻi lum ivvaḻakku iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟum maṉiyāḷ eṉṉum muṟip peyarum vantāḷ eṉṉum viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarum, maṉiyōḷ, vantōḷ eṉa maliyāḷatti yottut tamiḻilum tirital kāṇka.
āḷ īṟu aḷ eṉṟu kuṟukum.
e.ṭu. avaḷ, makaḷ.
aḷḷīṟu peṟṟa makaḷ eṉṉum peyarum māḷ eṉṟu maruvip peṇpālīṟām.
e.ṭu. vēṇmakaḷ - vēṇmāḷ, perumakaḷ - perumāḷ.
(perumāl - perumāḷ = makāviṭṭuṇu)
cila aṉṉīṟṟu āṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ peṇpālil ai yīṟākat tiriyum.
e.ṭu. āciriyaṉ - āciriyi, aiyaṉ - aiyi, civaṉ - civi, paṇṭitaṉ - paṇṭiti, parattaṉ - paratti, paraṉ - pari, valavaṉ - valavi. paṇṭiyorumi yīṟākiya ikara vīṟum peṇpālīṟām.
e.ṭu.	celvaṉ - celvi, pulavaṉ - pulatti.
		kaḷvaṉ - kaḷḷi, kiḻavaṉ - kiḻatti.
cila āṇpāl aṉṉīṟu peṇpāliṉ iṉi eṉṟu tiriyum.
e.ṭu. pāṇaṉ - pāṇiṉi - pāṭiṉi.
cila āṇpāl aṉ īṟu peṇpālil aṉi eṉṟu tiriyum.
e.ṭu. pārppaṉaṉ - pārppaṉi.
aṉṉiyik kuṟikkum ammi, acci eṉṉum peyarkaḷ arumi paṟṟip peṇpālīṟām.
e.ṭu. kaṇṇammi, taṅkicci.
atti acci eṉṉum aṉṉip peyarkaḷ, kulamum toḻilum paṟṟiya peṇpālīṟāy vaḻaṅkum.
e.ṭu. maṟatti, maruttuvacci.
atti, acci eṉpaṉa itti, icci eṉṟun tiriyum.
e.ṭu. vēṭṭuvitti, kaṭṭuvicci.
cila peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ, āṇpāṟkuriya maṟamum āṇmiyu muṇartta āṇpālīṟu koḷḷum.
e.ṭu. ammi - ammaṉ, pēṭu-pēṭaṉ.
ammi eṉṉuñ colliyottu, akki eṉṉum collum akkaṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
āṇmi koṇṭa peṇṇip pēṭaṉ eṉpatu pōl peṇmi (peṇṭaṉmi) koṇṭa āṇip pēṭi eṉpatu marapu. ivviraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉatu pēṭu eṉṉuñ col. āṇum peṇṇum allātatu allatu kalantatu ali. ippeyarkaḷ viṉi koḷḷumpōtu, pēṭaṉ vantāṉ, pēṭi vantāḷ, pēṭu vantatu,ali vantatu eṉa āṇtaṉmiyum collīṟum paṟṟi varum. itu ilakkaṇa marapu.
ulaka vaḻakkil, pēṭiyip peṭṭiyaṉ eṉṟum, āṇmiyaṟ ṟavaṉip peṇṇiyaṉ allatu aṇṇakaṉ eṉṟum, uruvam paṟṟip pēṭi vantāṉ, peṇṇiyaṉ vantāṉ, aṇṇakaṉ vantāṉ eṉṟum, kūṟuvatē marapām. aliyāyiṉ, āṇaliyi vantāṉ eṉṟum, peṇṇaliyi vantāḷ eṉṟum, uruvattiṟ kēṟpak kūṟuvar.
āḷaṉ eṉṉum āṇpālīṟu, peṇpālil āṭṭi eṉṟu tiriyum. āḷ + ti = āṭṭi. ti eṉpatu atti eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam.
e.ṭu. kaṇṇāḷaṉ - kaṇṇāṭṭi, tiruvāḷaṉ - tiruvāṭṭi, veḷḷāḷaṉ - veḷḷāṭṭi.
kāraṉ eṉṉum āṇpālīṟu, peṇpāliṟ kāri eṉṟu tiriyum.
e.ṭu. keṭṭikkāraṉ - keṭṭikkāri, paṇakkāraṉ - paṇakkāri.
māṉīṟṟu āṇpāṟ peyarkaṭ kotta peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ, māṭṭi eṉṉum īṟu koḷḷum.
makaḷ - māḷ + ti = māṭṭi.
e.ṭu. tirumāṉ - tirumāṭṭi, perumāṉ - perumāṭṭi.
tiru eṉṉum col srī eṉṟum, tirumāṉ eṉṉum col srīmat eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. srīmat eṉpataṉ peṇpāl srīmati - tirumati.
cila peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyilum teṉ moḻiyilum vevvēṟu vakiyil amintu, oṉṟupōl tōṉṟum.
e.ṭu. pati-patnī (va.) = maṉivi.
pattaṉ - pattiṉi (te.) = kaṇavaṉiṭattiṟ patti pūṇṭavaḷ, kaṟpuṭi maṉivi.
cila peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ ikara vīṟṟuṭaṉ icci yīṟuṅ koḷḷum. ivi iraṭṭip peṇpālām.
e.ṭu. kuruvikkāraṉ - kuruvikkāricci, veḷḷikkāraṉ - veḷḷikkāricci.
māṟōkkam eṉṉum koṟki nāṭṭār, paṇṭi nāḷil ciṟumiyip peṇmakaḷ eṉṟaṉar; iṉṟu vaṭārk kāṭṭār peṭṭip pacaṉ eṉpār. piyaṉ - payaṉ - pacaṉ.
palarpālīṟukaḷ
ārtal = poruntutal, kūṭutal, niṟital.
ār = poruttu, niṟivu.
palar kūṭutal eṉṉum karuttil ār eṉṉuñ collē palarpālīṟāyiṟṟu.
e.ṭu. taṭṭār, pollār.
ārīṟu ar eṉak kuṟukum.
e.ṭu. avar, palar, kollar.
ārīṟu ōr eṉṟum tiriyum.
e.ṭu. periyār - periyōr, muṉṉār - muṉṉōr.
ār īṟu peṟṟa makār eṉṉum peyarum mār eṉa maruvip palar pālīṟām. maka - makār - mār.
e.ṭu. aṇṇaṉmār, tēvimār.
ceyyum eṉṉum etirkālaviṉimuṟṟōṭu ārīṟu cērumpōtu, oru mār tōṉṟum. atu viṉiyīṟu. ceyyum + ār = ceyyumār - ceymār - ceyvār.
uyartiṇikku uriyataṉṟeṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭa kaḷ īṟu, ciṟupāṉmi attiṇikkum varum.
e.ṭu. makkaḷ, kōkkaḷ, kurukkaḷ, tirukkaḷ, nāṅkaḷ, nīṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ, āṭkaḷ, āṇkaḷ, peṇkaḷ, piyaṉkaḷ, makkaḷ eṉṉum peyar tolkāppiyattilum uḷḷatu.
uyartiṇi yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē  (484)
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē  (1532)
maka + kaḷ = makkaḷ. itu marūuppuṇarcci.
o.kō: aka + kaḷippu = akkaḷippu.
makkaḷi iḻintōr, ottōr, uyarntōr eṉa muttiṟattārāka vakuttu, avari muṟiyē, nī, nīr, nīṅkaḷ; avaṉ (avaḷ), avar, avarkaḷ eṉṉum coṟkaḷāṟ kuṟippatu tūya ulakavaḻakkam. paṇṭiyilakkaṇamellām ceyyuḷ naṭikkē eḻutappeṟṟa tiṉāl ivvaḻakku atil iṭampeṟavilli.
aṭikaḷ eṉpatu tiruvaṭi nōkkik kaṭavuḷik kuṟikkum. atu tuṟaviṉāl teyvattaṉmi peṟṟa periyōrik kuṟikkum pōtu irupāṟ potuvām.
e.ṭu. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ.
ārīṟum kaḷḷīṟum uyarvupaṟṟi orumikkum varum.
e.ṭu. makaṉār, nakkīraṉār, aṭikaḷār, vaḷḷalār, nīṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ, kurukkaḷ.
ceyyuḷnaṭi, urinaṭi eṉa ilakkiya naṭi iru vakippaṭum. ceyyuḷnaṭikku vilakkappaṭṭa ulaka vaḻakku urinaṭikku varum eṉa aṟika. ulakavaḻakkeṉpatu uyarntōr vaḻakkēyaṉṟi iḻintōr vaḻakkaṉṟu.
oṉṟaṉpālīṟukaḷ
tiṇipāl tōṉṟāta mutupaṇṭik kālattil, oṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum oṉ eṉṉuñ col orumiyuṇarttiṟṟu. atu puṇarcciyil tokku ṉ aḷavāyum niṟkum. 
e.ṭu. nāṉ, nīṉ, tāṉ.
oṉṉīṟu aṉṉīṟākavum tiriyum. aṉṉīṟum āṉīṟām.
e.ṭu. neṭuṅkaḻuttaṉ (oṭṭakam), coṟiyaṉ, (tavaḷi), kaṭuvaṉ; uḷḷāṉ, karippāṉ, kattarippāṉ.
āṇpāl aṉṉīṟupōl, paṇṭi yorumi aṉṉīṟum al eṉat tiriyum.
e.ṭu. uḷḷāṉ - uḷḷal, neṭuṅkaḻuttaṉ - neṭuṅkaḻuttal.
cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷum mutaṟkālattil īṟāyiruntu orumi yuṇarttiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ ikaramē iṉṟu vaḻakkiluḷatu. i=itu.
e.ṭu. kutirivāli, paṉiyēṟi, (mīṉ).
alli, palli, puli (pulli), kiḷi (kiḷḷi) mutaliyaṉavum, kuṉṉi, naṉṉi mutaliyaṉavum, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷē.
aimpālīṟu tōṉṟiyapōtu, atu, itu, utu eṉṉum cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ oṉṟaṉpā līṟāyiṉa.
a-am-aṉ-al-atu. i-im-iṉ-il-itu. u-um-uṉ-ul-utu.
e.ṭu. (kiḻatu) - kiḻaṭu, karitu - karicu, ērutu - erutu.
ciṟitu peritu eḷitu valitu eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ īṟu, itu eṉṉum cuṭṭuppeyarē.
valatti, karutti, ciṟutti, venti eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, muṟiyē, valattatu, karuttatu, ciṟuttatu, ventatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatiyum, aikāravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ pala mutalil akara vīṟṟāvāyiruntatiyum, nōkkum pōtu, akaramum atu eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭiyīṟupōṟ payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭamēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
palaviṉpā līṟukaḷ
mutaṟkālattilirunta paṉmiyīṟu, kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa um eṉṉum collē. aṟamumporuḷum eṉṉun toṭaril, um eṉpatu kūṭutaṟ poruḷik kuṟittal kāṇka. accol iṉṟu apporuḷil vaḻakkaṟṟuppōyiṉum, ataṉ aṭippiṟanta kum eṉṉuñ col apporuḷil vaḻaṅkutali nōkkuka.
kummutal kūṭutal. kum - kummal, kum - kumpu - kumpal.
ummīṟṟiṉpiṉ tōṉṟiya paṉmiyīṟu kaḷ eṉpatē. atuvum kūṭutal eṉṉum poruḷatē.
kaḷḷutal = kūṭutal, kalattal, poruntutal, ottal. kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭṭam, kūṭumiṭam, avikkaḷam, ērkkaḷam,pōrkkaḷam, tiṇikkaḷam mutaliya coṟkaḷi nōkkuka. kaḷam - kaḷaṉ. kaḷam - kaḷar - kaḷari. kaḷam - (kaḷaku) - kaḻaku - kaḻakam.
kaḷḷa eṉpatu ōr uvamavurupu.
kaḷḷa matippa vella vīḻa  (tol. 1235)
kaḷḷa = porunta, okka.
palaporuḷkaḷ kūṭutal eṉṉuṅkaruttil, kaḷ eṉṉuñ col paṉmiyīṟāyiṟṟu. maraṅkaḷ = marakkūṭṭam.
aimpālīṟu tōṉṟiyapōtu, akaraccuṭṭum ataṉaṭip piṟanta avi eṉṉum colliṉ viyīṟum palaviṉ pālīṟāka variyaṟukkappeṟṟaṉa.
a-am-av-(ava)-avi.
i-im-iv-(iva)-ivi.
u-um-uv-(uva)-uvi.
e.ṭu. pala, nallavi.
pālpakā aḵṟiṇip peyar
orumiyīṟum paṉmiyīṟum peṟātu iyalpāka irukkum aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷellām, orumikkum paṉmikkum potuvām. avaṟṟiṉ eṇ, avaṟṟiṉ viṉimuṟṟālum muṉpiṉ varum collālum aṟiyappaṭum. itu vaḻā niliyām.
e.ṭu.	maram vaḷarkiṟatu. 
		kutiri ōṭukiṟatu.				orumi
		oru kāy eṉṉa vili?        }
		maram vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa.
		kutiri ōṭukiṉṟaṉa.			paṉmi
		nūṟukāy vāṅkiṉēṉ.        }
uyartiṇiyilum, āṇpālīṟum peṇpālīṟum peṟātu avviraṇṭiṟkum potuvāyirukkum oru cila peyarkaḷ im muṭipu koḷḷum, itu vaḻuvamitiyām.
e.ṭu.
pērukku aintuvarum.  - orumi
āyiram pērik koṉṟavaṉ ari maruttuvaṉ  (paṉmi)
peṟṟatāyip pēṇāta mūṭar eṉpatil, tāy eṉa orumiyil vantatu vakupporumi yeṉappaṭum. tāyar eṉṟu paṉmivariṉ, ovvoruvarkkum naṟṟāyar palar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutalum aṅṅaṉam kūṭāmiyum, kāṇka.
paṇpuppeyarīṟukaḷ
paṇpuppeyarīṟukaḷ cuṭṭaṭiccol, ciṉippeyar, iṭap peyar, nīrppeyar eṉṉum nālvakiyil tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. cuṭṭaṭiccol.
a-ai 	e.ṭu.	tolli, pacci
a-am	  		nalam, ciṉam āḻam, taṉam (taṉmi)
am-aṉ	  	tiṟam - tiṟaṉ
aṉ-al	  	tiṟaṉ-tiṟal
   al	  		iyal
antu + ai	  	aranti
atu-tu	  		cētu (civappu)
tu-ṟu	  		naṉṟu
atu-ati-ti	  	maṟati, amiti
ti-ṟi		  	naṉṟi
ti-ci		  	māṭci, vaṟaṭci
al-aḷ	  	mañcal-mañcaḷ
aḷ-aṇ	  	muraṇ
al-ar	  	mayal-mayar
ar		  	naṉṉar
am-av-avu 	maḻavu
avu-avi-vi	  	maṟavi
avu-apu-vi	  	māṇpu, paṇpu, aṉpu
pu + am	  		nuṇ-nuṭpu-nuṭpam, iṉ-iṉpam
al+pu	  		iyalpu
avu-aku	  	kuḻavu-kuḻaku
aku-ku	  		naṉku, aḻaku
iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi yirucuṭṭiṟkum oṭṭikkoḷka.
e.ṭu. i-vekuḷi, il-eḻil (aḻaku), itam - perumitam.
ciṉippeyar
e.ṭu. taṟukaṇ (añcāmi)
iṭappeyar
e.ṭu. akam-vañcakam, tali-uṟutali, kaṇ-iṭukkaṇ nīrppeyar nīr-neṭunīr (maṟavi). mi-taṉmi, naṉmi.
kuṇaṅkaḷ perumpālum viṉivāyilāy veḷippaṭuvatāl kuṇappeyarkaḷum perumpālum viṉiyaṭippeyarkaḷākavē yuḷḷaṉa.
iṉippu puḷippu mutaliyaṉa collāl toḻiṟpeyarāyiṉum, poruḷāṟ paṇpuppeyarām.
añcāmiyum maṟamum kaṇṇāl veḷippaṭutaliṉ, ak kuṇattik kuṟikkak kaṇ eṉṉum col īṟāyiṟṟu. kaṭuṅkaṇ maṟavar eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
kaṇ kāl ki tali mutaliya ciṉippeyarkaḷ, iṭap peyarākavum iruvaki vaḻakkilum āḷappeṟum.
tamiḻnāṭu veppanāṭātaliṉ, kuḷircciyit tarum nīriṉ peyarum maḻiyiṉ peyarum iṉiya taṉmiyik kuṟikkalāyiṉa. piṉṉar avi taṉmi eṉṉum potup poruḷil āḷap peṟṟaṉa.
kākki karavā karintuṇṇum ākkamum
aṉṉanī rārkkē yuḷa  (527)
cīrmi ciṟappoṭu nīṅkum payaṉila
nīrmi yuṭiyār coliṉ  (195)
neṭuṅkaṭalun taṉṉīrmi kuṉṟum taṭinteḻili
tāṉalkā tāki viṭiṉ  (17)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷkaḷi nōkkuka.
nīr = nīriṉ kuḷirnta taṉmi, taṉmi. nīrmi = ciṟanta taṉmi - taṉmi. mi = karumukil, maḻinīr, nīr.
nīr, mi eṉṉum irucollum paṇpuppeyarīṟāṉa piṉ, nīr eṉṉum collum taṉmi yeṉṉum potup paṇpup peyar pōl miyīṟu peṟṟateṉka.
toḻiṟ peyarīṟukaḷ
toḻiṟpeyar vakikaḷ
1.	mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar : e.ṭu aṭi, kaṭṭu.
2.	mutaṉilinīṇṭa toḻiṟpeyar. e.ṭu. uṇ-ūṇ puṟappaṭu-puṟappāṭu, kūppiṭu-kūppīṭu - kūppāṭu.
3.	mutaṉili valitta toḻiṟpeyar, e.ṭu. virumpu - viruppu, nīntu-nīttu.
4.	mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭitta toḻiṟpeyar. e.ṭu. karutu, karuttu, pēcu-pēccu.
5.	mutaṉili īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar. e.ṭu. ceyki, paṭippu.
6.	mutaṉili nīṇṭu īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar. e.ṭu. naṭi-nāṭakam, paṭi-pāṭam.
7.	mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭittu īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar. e.ṭu. āṭu-āṭṭam, a, ā.
	āṭṭam ākkam eṉpaṉa āṭutal, ākutal eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuṅkāl, taṉviṉiyaṭip piṟantaviyē.
8.	mutaṉili valittu īṟupeṟṟa toḻiṟpeyar. e.ṭu. añcu-accu-accam, virumpu-viruppu-viruppam.
9.	īṟu tirinta toḻiṟpeyar. e.ṭu. vel-veṉ, vēḷ - vēṇ.
10.	palavīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar. e.ṭu. kala-kalappu, kalappaṭam, yā-yāppu-yāppuṟavu.
toḻiṟpeyarīṟu vakikaḷ
(1) ciṉippeyarkaḷ (2) iṭappeyarkaḷ (3) cuṭṭaṭikaḷ, (4) aḷavukuṟitta coṟkaḷ, (5) paṇpuppeyarīṟu, (6) viṉimuṟṟīṟu.
1. ciṉippeyarkaḷ
ki eṉpatu ākupeyarāyk kiyiṉāṟ ceyyum toḻiliyuṅ kuṟikkum. itu karuviyākupeyar.
iravār irappārkkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
kiceytūṇ māli yavar  (1035)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiluḷḷa kiceytu eṉṉum toṭarukku, kiyiṉāṟ ceytu eṉṟu uri kūṟuvatiṉum, ciṟanta toḻilākiya uḻavic ceytu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruttamām.
toḻilik kuṟikkum ki eṉṉum col toḻiṟ peyarīṟāvatu poruttamē.
e.ṭu. cey-ceyki, kal-kaṟki, nampu-nampikki.
ki eṉṉum ciṉippeyarum cey eṉṉum viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tirintatē. cey-(key)-ki.
toḻiṟpeyarīṟāṉa maṟṟoru kippeyar pāṇi eṉpatām.
e.ṭu. ciri-cirippāṇi = cirikki (cirippu).
itu nelli vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. pāṇi ki, paṇṇuvatu pāṇi. kiyiṉāṟ ceyyum icikkālavaṟuppum pāṇi yeṉappaṭum.
(2) iṭappeyarkaḷ
akam - nampakam, vañcakam, tāṇṭakam - tāṇṭavam.
tali - viṭutali.
cuṭṭaṭiccoṟkum iṭappeyarkkum vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum. akam eṉpatu cuṭṭaṭic collāyiṉ aku + am eṉṟu piriyum; iṭappeyarāyiṉ piriyātu.
e.ṭu.	kaḻaku-kaḻakam, ulaku- ulakam.
		nampu - nampakam, vañci-vañcakam, naṭi-nāṭakam.
(3) cuṭṭaṭiyacikaḷ
e.ṭu. am-maṇam, kaṇiyam, oṭṭam;cam-kaṇicam; tam-kaṇitam.
al-pāṭal, niṭṭal; kal-koṭukkal; cal-vaḷical; tal-vāḻtal, kēṭṭal, kāṇṭal, kaṟṟal, tiṉṟal; val (vu+al) - pārval.
i-pōṟṟi; ci-nīṭci, kāṭci, vīḻcci, ti-maṟati, kuḷirtti, koṇṭi; vi-aḷavi, kēḷvi.
(u)-ku-kaṇakku, cu-(toṭucu)-toṭicu, muṭiccu; tu-varattu; pu-naṭappu, paṭippu, koṭuppu, naṭpu, vu-kaḷavu, iḻivu, uḻavu, cōrvu.
ā-uṇā
(ā)-ai-koḷḷi, koli naṭi, taṭi; ti-naṭatti; vi(vu+ai)-aḷavi, iḻuvi, pārvi.
ām-kuḻām
āl-eḻāl
cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷ uyireḻuttuppaṟṟi oru vakiyāka oḻuṅku paṭuttap paṭṭiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟkellām mūlam muṟkūṟiya vāṟē eṉṟu aṟintu koḷka.
e.ṭu. a-av-avu-aku-ku.
aku-akku-ku.
cila īṟukaḷ pōli muṟiyil vēṟīṟākavum tiriyum.
e.ṭu.	kaṭiyal-kaṭical-kaṭital.
		kaṇiyam-kaṇicam-kaṇitam
		aṭivu-aṭavu-aṭaku
		uḻavu-uḻappu (uḻapperutu=uḻaverutu)
cila viṉi mutaṉilikaḷ palvaṭivu koṇṭu vaṭiviṟ kēṟpa īṟēṟkum.
e.ṭu. pō-pōtal, pōku-pōkku (valiyiraṭṭal), pōtu-pōtuki. iṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, ki tal eṉṉum īrīṟukaḷi ellā viṉi mutaṉiliyum avaṟṟiṉ palvaṭivukaḷum ēṟkum.
cila viṉi mutaṉilikaḷ toḻiṟpeyarākum pōtu ciṟitum peritum tiriyum.
e.ṭu. nampu-nampikki, kuti-kūttu.
cila īṟukaḷ iraṇṭum palavum cērntu kūṭṭīṟām.
e.ṭu. al+am - alam. e.ṭu. poṭṭalam.
alam īṟu piṉvarumāṟu tiriyum.
alam-aḷam aṇam - aṭam.
alam-aṉam-aṉi-āṉi.
alam-atam-aram-aravu. atam-acam.
e.ṭu.	tappaḷam, kaṭṭaṇam, kaṭṭaṭam.
		vañcaṉam, vañcaṉi, vārāṉi.
		viḷamparam,tēṟṟaravu, uppacam.
akara mutalīṟukaḷ otta ikara ukara mutalīṟukaḷuḷ uḷa.
il-eḻil (eḻucci). itam-tappitam, im-(urim) - (urin) - uriñ = urāytal.
um-porum - porun = poruntutal.
(4) aḷavu kuṟitta coṟkaḷ
māṉam = aḷavu. varumāṉam = varum aḷavu. peṟumāṉam = peṟumatippu.
kāṭu = mikuti. kaṭuttal mikutal. kaṭu-kāṭu, veḷḷakkāṭu, piḷḷikkāṭu eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkku.
māṉam - tīrmāṉam, kāṭu - vēkkāṭu. vitippu eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum. vitippāṭu eṉṉuñ col, akappāṭu, aṭippāṭu, aruḷappāṭu, kaṭappāṭu, kuṟipāṭu, veḷippāṭu eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pōl, paṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi nīṇṭa toḻiṟ peyarē.
cāppāṭu kūppāṭu eṉpavi, cāppīṭu kūppīṭu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripām.
(5) paṇpup peyarīṟu
e.ṭu. mi - vantami, varukiṉṟami, vārāmi.
(6) viṉimuṟṟīṟu
atu-tu-vantatu, varukiṉṟatu varuvatu, varātatu.
ituvum cuṭṭaṭic collāyiṉum, viṉimuṟṟīṟā yiruttal paṟṟi vēṟu kūṟappeṟṟatu. vantatu = vanta atu varātatu -varāta atu.
toḻiṟpeyarīṟukaḷiṉ ciṟappup poruḷ
oru viṉi mutaṉili pala īṟukaḷ peṟṟup palvēṟu toḻiṟ peyarum paṇpup peyarum toḻilāku peyarum ākalām. mutaṉili oṉṟēṉum īṟu vēṟupaṭapporuḷ vēṟupaṭum.
e.ṭu.
nampu- nampikki = uṇmiyākak koḷḷutal (Belief)
nampakam (Faith) = vicuvācam (va.)
nampu (mutaṉilit to. po.) = nampāci (Hope)
nampum mēvum naciyākummē  (tol. uri. 31)
kal-kaṟṟal = kaṟkuñ ceyal
	kaṟki = paṭippu (Learning)
	kalvi = (Education) nāṭṭup paṭippu muṟi.
	kali = kalvittuṟi allatu payiṟcikkalvi.
	kaṟpu = taṉikkātaloḻukkam.
naṭa - naṭattal = naṭakkuñ ceyal
	naṭakki = nāṭṭu vaḻakku, oḻukum muṟi.
	naṭatti = oḻukkam
	naṭappu = nikaḻkālattatu (That which is current)
	naṭi = moḻippōkku  (Style) vācalukku
aṭutta uṭpakkam (Threshhold)
	naṭavi = vaḻi, moḻivaḻaṅkumiṭam
	naṭavu = āṭci
	viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarīṟukaḷ
(1) viṉimuṟṟīṟukaḷ
taṉmi orumi : ēṉ e.ṭu. vantēṉ
taṉmip paṉmi : ēm, ōm e.ṭu. vantēm, vantōm
muṉṉili orumi : āy
(ī-ē-ai-āy) e.ṭu. vantāy
muṉṉilip paṉmi : īm, īr e.ṭu. vantīm, vantīr
īṅkaḷ, īrkaḷ e.ṭu. vantīṅkaḷ, vantīrkaḷ
paṭarkki -
ā. pā. āṉ e.ṭu. vantāṉ
pe. pā. āḷ e.ṭu. vantāḷ
pa.pā. ār e.ṭu. vantār, vantārkaḷ
o. pā. atu e.ṭu. vantatu
pala. pā. a, avi, aṉa e.ṭu. vanta, vantavi, vantaṉa
(2) cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ
ceytavaṉ, ceytavaḷ, ceytavar (ceytavarkaḷ), ceytatu, ceytavi
ceytavaṉ = ceyta avaṉ, iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyavum. ivvaṭivam paṭarkkikkē yuriyatu.
(3) ā ō āṉa īṟukaḷ
e.ṭu. vantōṉ vantōḷ, vantōr (vantōrkaḷ)
ivvaṭivam uyartiṇip paṭarkkikkē uriyatām.
palvaki viṉimutalīṟukaḷ
(1) ceyvāṉīṟukaḷ
i. itu mūviṭa vorumippālilum eṇṇilum varum.
e.ṭu. maramēṟi, kalluppoṟukki, toṭṭāṟ ciṇuṅki, kolvi, veṭṭi mutaliya peyarkaḷ aṭiyillātuvariṉ, mūviṭa vorumip pāṟkum eṇṇiṟkum potuvām.
āṉ, itu peṇpāloḻinta mūviṭa vorumip pāṟkum eṇṇiṟkum potuvāy varum.
e.ṭu. ōtuvāṉ, kāṟṟaṭippāṉ (Air-pump).
uyartiṇi āṉīṟum aḵṟiṇi āṉīṟum vevvēṟāyiṉum, vaṭivorumi paṟṟi oṉṟāyk kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
(2) uṭiyāṉīṟukaḷ
aṉ-āṉ : ivi peṇpāloḻinta paṭarkki yorumip pālkaḷil varum.
e.ṭu. karikālaṉ, vēlāṉ, arivayiṟaṉ (ari viḷiccal nelmaṇi), kaḷiyāṉ (vāṉampāṭi)
i. itu āṇpāloḻinta mūviṭa vorumippālkaḷil varum.
e.ṭu. taṭaṅkaṇṇi, nīrmuḷḷi.
(3)  illāṉīṟukaḷ
ili, aṟi. ivi mūviṭa vorumippālilum eṇṇilum varum.
e.ṭu. aṟivili = aṟivillāta nāṉ, nī.
aṟivillātavaṉ - vaḷ - tu.
kātaṟi = kātillāta nāṉ, nī.
kātillātavaṉ - vaḷ - tu.
(4) koṇṭāṉīṟu
koḷḷi koḷi. ivi mūviṭa vorumippāṟkum eṇṇiṟkum potuvām.
e.ṭu. pittukkoḷi = pittukkoṇṭa nāṉ, nī pittuk koṇṭavaṉ - vaḷ - tu.
aṭikoḷi = aṭikoṇṭatu.
(5) ceyappaṭu poruḷ īṟu
am-m. e.ṭu. tolkāppiyaṉ - tolkāppiyam = tolkāppi yaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. 
cēṉāvariyaṉ - cēṉāvariyam = cēṉāvariyaṉāl urikkap peṟṟatu.
(6) iṭappeyar marūuīṟukaḷ
e.ṭu. anti - uṟanti, karanti, kaḷanti, kuṭanti.
ai - aḷaki, uñci, tañci, tarumi, karuvi, maḻavi, mukavi, nelli, āṟi, ceṉṉi.
ci - iḷaci, tuṟici, paṉaci.
vi - kōvi, putuvi.
kāñci, tirucci mutaliyavi īṟu karutāta kuṟukkaṅkaḷ.
4. vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ
moḻi potumakkaḷ amippātaliṉ, ataṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta kūṟukaḷāṉa vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷum avarkaḷ amittaṉavē. vēṟṟumikaḷi ēḻeṉṟum eṭṭeṉṟum variyaṟuttatum, avaṟṟi varicippaṭuttiyatum, avaṟṟiṟkup peyariṭṭatum, karuvi vēṟṟumiyiyum uṭaṉikaḻcci vēṟṟumiyiyum oṉṟa cērttu oru vēṟṟumi yākkiyatumē, mutaṉūlāciriyaṉum vaḻinūlāciriyarumāṉa, ilakkaṇiyar ceyta viṉikaḷām.
vēṟṟumi yeṭṭum allatu oṉpatum. ilakkaṇa nūlār vakutta varici yoḻuṅkil tōṉṟiyirukka muṭiyātu. avvav vēṟṟumikkaruttut tōṉṟiyapōtu avvav vēṟṟumiyurupu tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
oruvaṉ mutalil tāṉē viṉimutalāyiruntu oṉṟu ceyvatē iyalpātalālum, oruvaṉ varalāṟṟi ōrūril oruvaṉ iruntāṉ eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvatē marapātalālum, mutalāvatu eḻuvāyk karuttut tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṟku urupu tēviyilli; iyalpāṉa peyarē pōtum. tirimoḻikaḷi lēyē eḻuvāy vēṟṟumikkum urupu amikkappaṭṭuḷatu. tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻi yeṉpataṟku, atil eḻuvāyuru piṉmiyum oru cāṉṟām.
oruvaṉ oruttiyuṭaṉ allatu iṉṉoruvaṉuṭaṉ pēcumuṉ, avari viḷikkātu irukkamuṭiyātu. itu moḻi tōṉṟāta niliyilum nikaḻum.
oruvaṉ oru viṉi ceyyiṉum oṉṟum ceyyātu cummā viruppiṉum, avaṉ irukka ōr iṭam vēṇṭum. ātalāl, iṭakkaruttu aṭuttut tōṉṟi yirukkalām.
malikkukiyil vatinta anākarika māntaṉukkum niliyāṉa taṉiyiṭam vēṇṭiyiruttatiṉāl, uṭimik karuttut tōṉṟi yirukkum.
māntaṉ viṉikaḷuḷ, cilavaṟṟiṟku oṉṟum tēviyilli; cila vaṟṟiṟku ētēṉum vēṇṭum. eḻutalum naṭattalum tāmāka nikaḻum āyiṉ paṟittalum uṇṭalum kāykaṉipōṉṟavi yiṉṟi nikaḻā. uṇṇutal uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta viṉi. oṉṟum putitāyc ceyyāta anākarikak kālattilum māntaṉ iyaṟkiyuṇavi uṇṭu vantāṉ. ataṉāl, ceyporuṭ karuttut tōṉṟi irukkum.
kiḻaṅkit tōṇṭuvataṟkum vilaṅku paṟavikaḷik kol vataṟkum, kallum kōlum pōṉṟa karuvikaḷ tēvippaṭṭa pōtu, karuvik karuttut tōṉṟiyirukkum.
paṟittum akaḻntum vēṭṭiyāṭiyum koṇṭu vanta poruḷ kaḷi, maṉivimakkaṭkum uṟṟār uṟaviṉarkkum koṭutta pōtu, koṭikkaruttut tōṉṟiyirukkum.
maramēṟik kaṉipaṟittavaṉum maliyēṟit tēṉeṭuttavaṉum, kīḻiṟaṅkiyapōtu allatu tavaṟi viḻuntapōtu, allatu kāykaṉi marattiṉiṉṟu kīḻē viḻuntapōtu, nīkkak karuttu tōṉṟiyirukkum.
māntaṉ kūṭivāḻum uyiriyātalāl, ōriṭattil iruppiṉum, oṉṟic ceyyiṉum, ōriṭam celliṉum, peṟṟōruṭaṉ allatu maṉiviyuṭaṉ allatu makkaḷuṭaṉ allatu nāyuṭaṉ (allatu karuviyuṭaṉ) irukkavum ceyyum cellavum nērnta pōtu, uṭaṉikaḻccik karuttut tōṉṟiyirukkum.
iṅṅaṉam eṇ allatu toṇvēṟṟumikaḷum, kuṟiñci vāḻkki niliyilēyē muntutamiḻar moḻiyil tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum.
vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷiṉ varalāṟu
uruttal tōṉṟutal, uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, uṭampu, taṉipporuḷ. uru - uruvu - uruvam. uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumi vaṭivamāṉa col allatu aci.
mutal vēṟṟumi yurupu allatu vaṭivam iyalpāṉa peyarē.
2 ām vēṟṟamiyurupu (ceyporuḷ).
ai. āy - ai. peṭṭiyāyc ceytāṉ = peṭṭiyic ceytāṉ.
3 - ām vēṟṟumiyurupu
āl - āṉ (karuvi)
il eṉṉum (7-ām vēṟṟumi) iṭapporuḷurupu karuvip poruḷilum āḷap peṟum.
e.ṭu. miyil eḻutu = miyāl eḻutu.
ceruppilaṭittāṉ = ceruppālaṭittāṉ (mēli vaṭārk kāṭṭu vaḻakku).
la-ṉ. o.nō : mēla-mēṉa. il-āl-āṉ.
uṭaṉ-ōṭu-ōṭu (uṭaṉikaḻcci), kūṭa.
ullutal = poruntutal, kūṭutal. ul-uḷ-uṭu-uṭaṉ.
4-ām vēṟṟumiyurupu (koṭi)
ku. okka eṉṉum col ku eṉṟu tirintirukkalām.
avaṉokkak koṭuttāṉ - avaṉukkukkoṭuttāṉ
5-ām vēṟṟumiyurupu (nīkkam)
iṉ, itu iṭapporuḷurupāṉa il eṉpataṉ tiripē.
iliruntu (il+iruntu) allatu iṉiṉṟu (il+niṉṟu) eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkuk kūṭṭuccollurupē. ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil iṉ eṉat taṉic collurupāyk kuṟukittirintatu. iṉiṉṟu eṉṉum puṇarcciyum itaṟkup peritum utaviṟṟu.
marattiliruntu, marattiṉiṉṟu eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkē, iyaṟki yākavum poruḷnirampiyum mūlattit teḷivāyk kāṭṭu vatākavum iruttal kāṇka. iṟaṅkum allatu viḻum nili, iruttal niṟṟal ākiya iraṇṭil oṉṟākavē yirukkum.
6 - ām vēṟṟumiyurupu (uṭimi)
atu - ātu (orumi); a (paṉmi).
ivi uṭimip poruḷuṇarttum kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟuk kaḷiṉ muṟimāṟṟam. ātalāl, ivi yiraṇṭum eṇkāṭṭum.
e.ṭu.
kuṟippuviṉimuṟṟu 		āṟām vēṟṟumippeyar
ki eṉatu (orumi)			eṉatu ki
kikaḷ eṉa (paṉmi)		eṉa kikaḷ
uṭiya eṉpatu īreṇṇiṟkum potuvāṉa vurupām.
aṉ, iṉ eṉpaṉavum uṭimi vēṟṟumi yurupāka varum.
e.ṭu. itaṉ poruḷ, nīriṉ taṉmi.
7 ām vēṟṟumiyurupu (iṭam)
iṭiccollāka niṉṟu iṭapporuḷuṇarttum ellāc coṟkaḷum 7 ām vēṟṟumiyurupām. vēṟṟumi yeṉṟatu poruḷ paṟṟiyē yaṉṟic coṟpaṟṟiyaṉṟu. āyiṉ, āriyam pōṉṟa tirimoḻikaḷil vēṟṟumi colliyē taḻuvi niṟkum.
iṭapporuḷurupukaḷuḷ, ciṟappākak koḷḷappeṟuvatu ulaka vaḻakkil il; ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil kaṇ.
iṭam, pakkam; ōram, naṭu; uḷ, veḷi; kīḻ, mēl; muṉ, piṉ; ākiya ellā vaki yiṭapporuḷilum 7 ām vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ varum. iṭam eṉṉum collum illurupēṟpa tuṇṭu.
e.ṭu. eṉṉiṭam, eṉṉiṭattil.
8 ām vēṟṟumiyurupu (viḷi)
peyarkaḷ viḷikkappaṭum niliyil aṭiyum vaṭivē 2 ām vēṟṟumiyurupām. avvaṭivu peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟip poṟut tatu. viḷiyēṟṟa peyarkaḷ perumpālum tiriyum, ciṟupāṉmi tiriyā.
tiriyum peyarkaḷ cēymic cuṭṭāyiṉ, aḷapeṭukkum; tiriyāp peyarkaḷ cēymic cuṭṭāyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟku muṉ aḷapeṭutta ē allatu ō eṉṉum viḷiyoli cērkkappeṟum; appeyar kaḷiṉ īṟum ciṟupāṉmi aḷapeṭukkum. tiriyum peyarkku muṉṉum ē allatu ō cērkkap peṟuvatuṇṭu.
e.ṭu. iyalpuviḷi : pēriṉpam, nampikki, tiripuviḷi :
īṟu mikutal - teyvamē, makaṉē
īṟu keṭutal - aiya, iḷañceḻiya
īṟu tirital - tampī, piḷḷāy
īṟṟayal tirital - māṇavīr, nampimīr
īṟukeṭṭu ayal tirital - aḻakā, naṇpā
īṟu keṭṭu ayal tirintu īṟu mikutal - aiyavō, ammēyō
cēymi viḷi
kaṇṇā aaaa
ēee aṇṇā aaaa
ōoo aiyā aaaa
ēee pēriṉpam
ēee maturammmm
ella eṉṉum viḷiccol
viḷiyolikaḷuḷ, ella eṉpatu toṉṟutoṭṭu varuvatum, iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkuvatum, ilakkaṇattil iṭam peṟṟatum tamiḻiṉ muṉmiyiyum piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ piṉmi yiyum uṇarttuvatum, āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻukkumuḷḷa aṇukkattik kāṭṭuvatum ākum.
ella eṉpatu, mutaṟkālattil kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum oruvari yoruvar viḷikkum potuvoliyāyiruntatu. itiyē,
muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ keḻutakip potuccol
nilikkuri marapiṉ iruvīṟṟum urittē  (1166)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟum. itu ellā eṉṟu īṟu nīṇṭu ellē eṉṟum tirintatu.
nillāṅku nillāṅ kivartaral ellā nī eṉṉum marutakkaliyil (30), ellā eṉpatu talivi talivaṉi viḷittatu.
ellā viḵtottaṉ eṉpeṟāṉ kēṭṭikkāṇ eṉṉum kuṟiñcikkaliyil (25), ellā eṉpatu tōḻi taliviyi viḷittatu.
ellē! - tuṭikoḷiṭi maṭattōḻī eṉṉun tiruvāy moḻiyaṭiyil (5,3,5). ellē eṉpatu talivi tōḻiyi viḷittatu.
ellā eṉpatu piṉpu ēlā eṉṟu mutal nīṇṭatu. kuṟavaṉ makaḷāṇi kūṟēlā kūṟēl eṉṉum paripāṭalaṭiyil (8:69) ēlā eṉpatu tōḻi talivaṉi viḷittatu.
karūrppakkattil, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyi ēlā eṉṟa viḷippatu, iṉṟum kallā makkaḷiṭi vaḻakkamāyirukkiṉṟatu.
nelli nāṭṭār, ciṟuvariyum kīḻōrāṉa āṭavariyum iṉṟu ēla, ēlē eṉṟu viḷikkiṉṟaṉar. kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyi ēḻā eṉṟu viḷippatu annāṭṭuk kīḻōr vaḻakkam. ēla ēḻa-ēḻā.
ēḻa eṉpatu piṉpu ēṭa eṉṟu tirintatu.
ēṭā aḻiyal eḻuntitu koḷḷāy
eṉṉum maṇimēkaliyaṭiyil (14, 12), ēṭā eṉpatu cintātēvi āputtiraṉi viḷittatu. ēṭa-ēṭā; ivi āṭūu (āṇpāl) viḷi. ēṭi, ēṭī eṉpaṉa makaṭūu (peṇpāl) viḷi.
ēṭa eṉpatu piṉpu aṭa - aṭā - aṭē eṉat tirintatu. ittiripukaḷum āṭūu viḷiyām. aṭi-aṭī eṉpaṉa makaṭūu viḷiyām.
nillaṭīi yeṉak kaṭukiṉaṉ peṇṇeṉa niṉittāṉ eṉpatil (kampa. āraṇi. 93), aṭi eṉpatu ilakkumaṇaṉ cūrppaṉakiyi viḷittatu.
aṭa, aṭā eṉṉum coṟkaḷ kaḻivirakkak kuṟippākavum vaḻaṅkum.
aṭā eṉpatu teluṅkil arā - rā eṉṟum, aṭē eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil arē -rē eṉṟum, tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. intiyil aṭē eṉpatu arē-rē eṉṟum, aṭaṭa eṉpatu arara eṉṟum, vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ṭa-ra, pōlittiripu. o.nō: kuṭampi - kurampi, paṭavar - paravar.
ella eṉṉum viḷiyiṭic colliliruntu, cila peyarc coṟkaḷum piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
ella-eluvaṉ = tōḻaṉ eluvaṉ-eluval. eluvi = tōḻi. ēṭa-ēṭaṉ = aṭiyāṉ.
ellā eṉṉum tamiḻccollum, hallo eṉṉum āṅkilac collum, oliyum poruḷum ottiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
mutaṟkālattil, vēḷum vēḷiṉiyumākiya talivaṉum talivi yum oruvariyoruvar matippāka viḷitta ellā eṉṉum col, ēla, ēḻa, ēṭa, aṭa, aṭā, aṭē, aṭī eṉap paṭippaṭi yāy vaṭivutirintum matippukkuṉṟiyum pālpirintum, nāḷaṭi vil iḻimakkaḷi viḷikkum collāyiṟṟu.
cila vēṟṟumi marapukaḷ
uṭaṉikaḻcciyi oru taṉivēṟṟumiyākkiṉ, vēṟṟumi mottam oṉpatām.
ovvoru vēṟṟumiyum, ovvoru allatu oru cila iṭic collāl uṇarttappeṟum palvēṟu karuttukkaḷiṉ tokutiyēyaṉṟi, oru taṉippaṭṭa karuttaṉṟu. iraṇṭām vēṟṟamipporuḷāka, irupatteṇ karuttukkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyar. āyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ ellām aṭaṅka villi. ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟāviṉi eṉa ilakkaṇiyar vakutta viṉic colvakikku allatu aikāra vēṟṟumikku ēṟṟa karuttukkaḷellām iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip poruḷē allatu ceyporuḷvakiyē. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi vēṟṟami kaḷum karuttut tokutikaḷē eṉa aṟika. oru vēṟṟumikkup pala karuttukkaḷiruppiṉum aviyellām oru parupporuḷiṉ nuṇ virivukaḷil aṭaṅkum. apparupporuḷ paṟṟiya vēṟṟumi eṭṭu allatu oṉpatu āyiṉa.
aciyurupu, collurupu eṉa vēṟṟamiyurupu iruvakippaṭum. cila karuttukkaḷic collurupāṟ kuṟippatē ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peruvaḻakkām. collurupu ētēṉumō raciyurupoṭu kūṭi varuvatē perumpāṉmi. aṅṅaṉam varuṅkāl oru vēṟṟumip poruṭku vēṟoru vēṟṟumiyurupum varum.
vēṟṟumi	poruḷ	collurupu	eṭuttukkāṭṭu
3-āvatu 	tuṇikkaruvi	koṇṭu	eḻuttāṇikoṇṭu
		ai+koṇṭu	kallikkoṇṭu
			iṭṭu	uḷiyiṭṭu
5-āvatu	uṟaḻporuvu	ai+viṭa	ativiṭa
			ai+kāṭṭilum	atikkāṭṭilum
6-āvatu	uṭimi	uṭiya 	kaṇṇaṉuṭiya
uṭimik karutti vēṟṟumit tokiyāṟ kuṟippatē ulaka vaḻakkiṟ perumpāṉmiyām. mūviṭap pakarap peyarkaḷ ivvaḻakkil neṭumutal kuṟukum.
e.ṭu. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ māḷiki, eṉ makaṉ.
aciyurupāṟ poruḷ nirampāta pōtu oru collum ataṉuṭaṉ cērkkappeṟum.
e.ṭu. avaṉ eṉakkāka iṅku vantāṉ.
orē poruḷ, colvāṉ karuttuppaṟṟiyum muṭipuc col paṟṟiyum, vevvēṟu vēṟṟumiyāṟ kuṟikkap peṟum.
e.ṭu.
añciyiṉ makaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉi (uṭimi) - 6 ām vē.
añcikku makaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉi (uṟavumuṟi) - 4 ām vē.
tiruvaḷḷuvari nikarppār yār? (viṉimuṭipu) - 2 ām vē.
tiruvaḷḷuvarukku nikar yār? (peyarmuṭipu) - 4 ām vē.
oru vēṟṟumi vēṟoru vēṟṟumiyiyun tuṇik koḷḷum.
e.ṭu. aṟikkuḷ eṉpatil 7 - āvatu 4 - āvatit tuṇik koṇṭami kāṇka.
5. cāriyikaḷ
tāmākac cērāta colluṟuppukkaḷiyum coṟkaḷiyum cārntu iyikkum acikaḷum coṟkaḷum, cāriyi eṉappaṭum. iyittal icittal. tāmāka olikkāta eḻuttukkaḷi olittaṟkum, olikkum eḻuttukkaḷi eḷitāy olittaṟkum, avaṟṟic cārntuvarum olikaḷum cāriyi eṉappaṭum. ākavē, eḻuttuc cāriyi, coṟcāriyi eṉac cāriyi iruvakiyām.
eḻuttuccāriyi
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ kuṟilukkuk karamum neṭilukkuk kāramum cāriyiyām, neṭilkaḷuḷ, ai, au eṉṉum iraṇṭiṟkum kāṉ eṉpatu ciṟappuc cāriyi.
āytattiṟkuc cāriyi ēṉam eṉpatām. atu cērumpōtu āytattiṟku muṉ akaramum piṉ kakarameyyum cērntu aḵkēṉam eṉṟākum.
meyyeḻuttiṟku a cāriyi. atu meykkuppiṉ varum. ka eṉpatu cāriyi yēṟṟa meyyeḻuttiṟkum ka eṉṉum kuṟilukkum potu vāyiruppatāl, meyyeḻutti vitantu kuṟikkum pōtu kakaramey eṉpatu marapu.
uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ, kuṟilukkuk karam cāriyi; neṭilukkut taṉiccāriyi illi. ataṉāl meyyiyum neṭiliyum pirittuk kakara ākāram (kā), kakara īkāram (kī) eṉṟa muṟiyiṟ collappeṟum.
coṟcāriyi
a, attu, am, aṟṟu, aṉ, ām, iṟṟu, iṉ, u, ai mutaliyaṉa coṟcāriyi.
e.ṭu. taṭṭārappāṭṭam, eṉakku, paṭṭiṉattāṉ, kuḷattup pāyccal, puḷiyampaḻam, avaṟṟi, ataṉi, kallāṅkoḷḷi patiṟṟuppattu, patiṉoṉṟu, vēriṉi, avaṉukku, paṇṭik kālam.
ivaṟṟuḷ, aṟṟuccāriyip puṇarcciyum iṟṟuc cāriyip puṇarcciyum iṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkaṟṟaṉa. avikaḷ eṉpatu miki paṭakkūṟum vaḻūuccollum, atukaḷ eṉpatu iḻivaḻakkum, ākum. ātalāl, avaṟṟi ivaṟṟi evaṟṟi eṉṟē kumari nāṭṭup potumakkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
cāriyikaḷum cuṭṭaṭiyiṉavē.
taṉmiviṉi yīṟukaḷ
1.	taṉmiyorumip peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi. ēṉ-eṉ-aṉ-al. aṉ, al etirkālattil maṭṭum varum.
2. ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉi yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi
	cey + atu = (ceyyatu) - ceytu-tu.
	tu-ṭu, tu-ṟu.
3.	etirkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu ceyyum + ēṉ = (ceyyumēṉ) - ceyyuvēṉ - ceyvēṉ - ceykēṉ - ceyku - ku.
	va. ka, pōli. o.nō: āvā - ākā, civappu - cikappu.
4. 	ukaraccuṭṭaṭiyāyp piṟantatu (etirkālam). 
	utu. e.ṭu. ceyyutu, kūṟutu, varutu.
utu īṟu tuvvaḷavākak kuṟuki, aṭinīṇṭa lakara ḷakara vīṟṟu viṉi mutaṉilikaḷōṭu kūṭumpōtu, ṟukara ṭukaramākat tiriyum.
e.ṭu.		cel-cēl+tu = cēṟu.
			koḷ-kōḷ+tu = kōṭu.
o.nō.	cel-cel+tal = cēṟal ( toḻiṟpeyar)
			koḷ-kōḷ+tal = kōṭal (toḻiṟpeyar)
taṉmippaṉmi yīṟukaḷ
1.	taṉmip paṉmip peyarkaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi. ēm-em, (nām) - ām-am, ām-ōm.
2.	ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi.
	tu + um (paṉmiyīṟu = tum.)
	tum - ṭum. tum-ṟum.
3.	etirkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu.
	ku + um (paṉmiyīṟu) = kum.
4.	ukaraccuṭṭaṭiyāyp piṟantatu (etirkālam).
	utu + um (paṉmiyīṟu) e.ṭu. ceyyutum, kūṟutum, varutum.
	tu-ṟu. cēṟu + um (paṉmiyīṟu) = cēṟum.
	tu-ṭu. kōṭu + um (paṉmiyīṟu) = kōṭum.
5.	ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu.
	ceyyum - um.
muṉṉiliviṉi yīṟukaḷ
mukkāla nikaḻcciviṉi yīṟukaḷ :
muṉṉilip peyarkaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi 
orumi (nī) - ī - i.
ī - ē - ai - āy.
e.ṭu vantī - vantē - vanti - vantāy.
o.nō: cī-cē.ci (ikaḻccik kuṟippu).
uri - urāy, kuḻi - kuḻāy.
paṉmi : (nīm) - īm. īm + kaḷ = īṅkaḷ.
(nīr) - īr - ir. īr + kaḷ = īrkaḷ.
(nīr) - īr - ir. īr + kaḷ = īrkaḷ.
nīm ceytīm eṉpatu iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
eppōtu vantīṅkaḷ eṉṉum vaḻakkiyum nōkkuka.
ēvalviṉi yīṟukaḷ
orumi (1) īṟaṟṟatu. e.ṭu. cey.
(2) etirkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu. cey + utu + ī = ceyyutī - ceyti.
vaḻipaṭuvōri vallaṟitīyē eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭil (10:1) varum aṟitī eṉpataṟku, aṟivi eṉṟu paḻiyavuri poruḷ kūṟi yiruppatiyum, aṟitī - aṟiti = aṟivāy eṉṟu cāminātiyar aruñcoṟkuṟippu varintiruppatiyum, ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
ceṉṟī (ceṉṟu+ī) eṉpatu. ceṉṟutavuka eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum utaviviṉi. niṉmē (nillum + ē) eṉpatu nilluṅkaḷēṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ārvaviṉi. nillum eṉpatu ukarantociṉ niṉm eṉṟākum. o.nō: pōlum - pōṉm. ēṉ eṉpatu ē eṉakkuṟintu niṉṟatu. ulaka vaḻakkilum varaṭṭē eṉa vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. niṉ+mē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa pakuppu, ati orumiyeṉak koḷḷac ceytirukkiṉṟatu. itiyaṟiyātu
muṉṉili muṉṉar īyum ēyum
annili marapiṉ meyyūrntu varumē
eṉa nūṟpā yāttār tolkāppiyar (934) atip piṉ paṟṟiṉār pavaṇantiyār.
(3) ceyyāy eṉṉum muṉṉili yorumiviṉi kuralvēṟu pāṭṭāl, cey eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. āy eṉpatu ēvaloru miyīṟeṉṟu kūṟātu.
ceyyāy eṉṉum muṉṉili viṉiccol
ceyyeṉ kiḷavi ākiṭaṉ uṭittē
eṉṟē tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatāl (933), ataṉ akukiya 
vaḻakkup pulaṉām. āy eṉpatu muṉṉili yorumi yīṟēyāyiṉum. ēvalorumi viṉi ati ēṟkavēṇṭu meṉṉum yāppuṟa villi. eṉa aṟika.
paṉmi :
(1) muṉṉilip paṉmip peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavi 
(ūm) - um. 			e.ṭu. ceyyum
um+kaḷ			e.ṭu. ceyyuṅkaḷ
um+(nīm-īm-im-)iṉ	e.ṭu. ceyyumiṉ-ceymmiṉ
miṉ+kaḷ			e.ṭu. ceymmiṉkaḷ
miṉ eṉṉum īṟṟiṉ makaram neṟi (viti) mutal eṉa aṟika.
kaḷ eṉpatu paṭarkki yīṟēṉum iraṭṭip paṉmi kuṟittu mūviṭattum varum.
e.ṭu. nāṅkaḷ, nīṅkaḷ, avarkaḷ.
ceyyumiṉ eṉpatiluḷḷa ummiṉ (um+iṉ) eṉṉum iraṭṭip paṉmiyi, ceyyuṅkaḷ eṉpatiluḷḷa uṅkaḷ (um+kaḷ) eṉpatu pōlak koḷka. ceymmiṉ eṉpataṉ iraṭṭip paṉmip poruḷ maṟintapiṉ, kaḷḷīṟu piṟavaṟṟōṭu cērntatu pōl ataṉōṭum cērntatu. im mukkip paṉmiyip peṇ peṇṭāṭṭi eṉpatu pōṉṟa mīmicic collākak koḷka.
(2) etirkāla viṉimuṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu cey + utu + īr = ceyyutīr - ceyyutir - ceytir.
(3) ceyyāy eṉpataṟkottatu ceyyīr eṉṉum vaṭivam.
viyaṅkōḷviṉi yīṟukaḷ
viyaṅkōḷviṉi yīṟukaḷ mūvakiyil tōṉṟum.
(1) toḻiṟpeya rīṟukaḷ
al e.ṭu. ceyal
ollum vakiyāl aṟaviṉi yōvātē
cellum vāyellām ceyal  (kuṟaḷ. 33)
tal. e.ṭu. ceytal.
iyaṟkip poruḷi iṟṟeṉak kiḷattal  (tol. 502)
(2) akaravīṟṟu viṉiyecca īṟu.
ceyya-a. e.ṭu. varappuyara !
naṭakka-ka. e.ṭu. vāḻka !
(3) ceyal vāypāṭṭu īyal eṉṉum tuṇiviṉit toḻiṟ peyar.
īyal-īyar-iyar-iya-i.
e.ṭu. niliyal - nilīyar-niliyar, vāḻiyar-vāḻiya-vāḻi
nilīiyar atti nīyē yoṉṟē  (puṟam. 3:5)
naṭukkiṉṟi niliyarō atti yaṭukkattu  (puṟam. 2)
uḷḷēṉ vāḻiyar yāṉeṉap paṉmāṇ  (puṟam. 365)
eṅkō vāḻiya kuṭumi taṅkō  (puṟam. 365)
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi  (cilap. 11:22)
viyaṅkōḷviṉi ēval, vāḻttu, cāvippu, vañciṉam eṉṉum nāṟporuḷpaṟṟi varum. ēval eṉṟatu vēṇṭukōḷ allatu matippēval.
piṟaṉā yiṉaṉkol irīiyareṉ ṉuyireṉa
eṉpatu cāvippu (puṟam 210)
avarppuṟaṅ kāṇē ṉāyiṟ ciṟanta
pērama ruṇka ṇivaḷiṉum pirika
eṉpatu vañciṉam (puṟam. 71).
viyaṅkōḷviṉi mūviṭa aimpālīreṇṇiṟkum potuvām! āyiṉ, ēvalum vāḻttum paṟṟit taṉmi yiṭattil varātu.
piṟaviṉi yīṟukaḷ
taṉviṉi piṟaviṉiyākum vakikaḷ
1. mutaṉili valittal
	e.ṭu. nīṅku-nīkku, poruntu - poruttu.
2. iṭinili valittal
	e.ṭu. tēyntatu-tēyttatu, tīrntāṉ-tīrttāṉ.
3. mutaṉili valiyiraṭṭal
	e.ṭu. pōku-pōkku, tēṟu-tēṟṟu.
4. īṟupeṟutal
	ī-vī-vi.pi.
ceyya + ī = ceyyavī-ceyvī-ceyvi.
naṭappa+ī=naṭappavī-naṭappī-naṭappi.
ceyyavīttāṉ = ceyyavutaviṉāṉ, ceyyaviṭṭāṉ, ceyyac ceytāṉ.
īttuvakkum iṉpam (kuṟaḷ 228.) emakkīttaṉiyē (puṟam. 911) eṉa ītalviṉi valittum iṟanta kālaṅ kāṭṭutal kāṇka.
vi.pi. piṟaviṉi yīṟukaḷāṉapiṉ, viṉikaḷ avaṟṟi ēṟṟa peṟṟi oṉṟum palavum ēṟṟaṉa.
ceyvippi, naṭattuvi - irumaṭippiṟaviṉi.
ceyvip pippi, naṭattu vippi - mummaṭippiṟaviṉi.
ivi ceyvippittāṉ, ceyvippippittāṉ eṉa nikaḻcci viṉiyām. ellāviṉikaḷum mutaṉiliyaḷavil ēvalvaṭi vāyiruttaliṉ, piṟaviṉi mutaṉilikaḷi ēval viṉiyeṉṟu vitantu kūṟa vēṇṭiyatilli.
ottu-attu-tu. ottu-oṭṭu-aṭṭu. ottutal = poruntutal, oṟṟutal, icital.
o-a. o.nō : kompu-kampu, moṇṭi-maṇṭi. mottiki-mattiki.
vāḻa + attu = (vāḻattu) - vāḻttu - vaḻuttu.
tāḻa + attu = (tāḻattu) - tāḻttu.
oṭṭu - aṭṭu. vara + aṭṭu = varaṭṭu.
attu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi tuvvīṟākak kuṟinta piṉ, ataṉ tiripākac cu ṭu ṟu īṟukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
e.ṭu.	pāy + tu = pāyttu - pāyccu
		nīḷ + tu = nīṭṭu, kāṇ + tu = kāṭṭu.
		nāl + tu = nāṟṟu, tiṉ + tu = tīṟṟu.
oru cila viṉikaḷ uvu īṟu peṟṟup piṟaviṉiyākiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.	eḻu-eḻuvu, koḷ-koḷuvu.
		eḻuvutal = eḻacceytal, ōciyeḻupputal.
		koḷuvutal = koḷḷacceytal.
iv uvu īṟu ovvu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉiyiṉ citivākum. ovvutal = ottal, poruntutal, icital.
eḻa + (ovvu) = eḻuvu, koḷa + (ovvu) = koḷuvu. ukarattiṉ muṉ akaram tokkatu.
(5) tuṇiviṉipeṟutal
e.ṭu. ūṟavi, kāyappōṭu, niṟpāṭṭu, (niṟpu + āṭṭu) maṟakkaṭi, (maṟakka + aṭi) eḻaccey, varappaṇṇu.
(6) aḷapeṭuttal
ikkālattil kaṭṭu, maṇṭu mutaliya kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu viṉic coṟkaḷ kaṭṭi, maṇṭi eṉa ikaravīṟēṟṟu iṟanta kāla viṉiyeccamāvatu pōl, mutaṟkālattil iru, uṭu mutaliya muṟṟukaravīṟṟu viṉiccoṟkaḷum ikaravīṟēṟṟu iri, uṭi eṉa iṟantakālaviṉi yeccamākiyirukkalām. ivvaṭivaṅkaḷi aḷapeṭippaṭuttip piṟaviṉiyākki yirukkalām.
iri-irīi = irutti, uṭi - uṭīi = uṭutti, uṟūu = uṟutti, koḷi - koḷīi = koḷutti.
koḷi (koḷḷi) eṉpati aḷḷi, eḷḷi, taḷḷi, tuḷḷi eṉpaṉapōṟ koḷka.
(7) iyalpāyiruttal
e.ṭu. katavu tiṟantatu-katavit tiṟantāṉ, kuṟavi piṟantatu - viṟakip piḷantāṉ.
ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyīṟukaḷ
ceyappāṭṭuviṉi vaṭivaṅkaḷ
1. akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccam + tuṇiviṉi
e.ṭu.	eḻutappaṭu, eḻutappaṭṭatu.
		vāḻttuppeṟu, vāḻttup peṟṟāṉ.
peṟu eṉpatu perumpālum pēṟupaṟṟiyum paṭu eṉpatu perum pālum pāṭu paṟṟiyum, varum. pāṭu = kēṭu, ceyyappaṭuki.
e.ṭu. paricaḷikkap peṟṟāṉ, uyarttappeṟṟāṉ. kollap paṭṭāṉ, taḷḷappaṭṭāṉ, uṇṇap paṭṭatu.
2. toḻiṟpeyar + tuṇiviṉi.
e.ṭu. koliyuṇṭāṉ, kolippaṭṭāṉ, kuttuppaṭṭuc cettāṉ.
3. viṉimutaṉili + tuṇiviṉi
e.ṭu. kolluṇṭāṉ, veṭṭuṇṭāṉ.
ivaṟṟiṉ viṉimutaṉiliyi mutaṉilit toḻiṟ peyarākavuṅ koḷḷalām.
tamiḻiṟ ceyappāṭṭuviṉi peruvaḻakkaṉṟu. perumpālum, ceyappāṭṭuviṉip poruḷic ceyviṉi vaṭiviṟ kūṟuvatē tamiḻar vaḻakkam.
e.ṭu.	puliyaṭittuc cettāṉ
		maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eḻutiya nūl
		taccaṉ ceyta peṭṭi
peyarecca vīṟukaḷ
terinilip peyareccam
a-i, kā, īṟu, e.ṭu. ceyta (ceytu + a)
a-ni. kā, īṟu, e.ṭu. ceykiṉṟa (ceykiṉṟu + a) - ceykiṟa.
um.e.kā. īṟu. e.ṭu. ceyyum (cey+um).
u-um (muṉmic cuṭṭaṭiccol).
kuṟippup peyareccam
a (mukkālappotu), e.ṭu. nalla.
iṟantakāla nikaḻkāla kuṟippuppeyarecca vīṟākiya a anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum cēymiccuṭṭākum. ceytu, ceykiṉṟu, nal eṉpaṉa muṟkālattil muṟṟuc coṟkaḷāyum irutiṇi yimpāl mūviṭappotuvāyum iruntatiṉāl, viṉiyā laṇiyum peyarāki,
ceyta eṉpatu, ceytēṉākiya anta, ceytēmākiya anta, ceytāyākiya anta, ceytīrākiya anta, ceytāṉākiya anta ceytāḷākiya anta, ceytārākiya anta, ceytatākiya anta, ceytaṉavākiya anta eṉṟu poruḷ paṭṭirukkum.
iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyirucoṟkaṭkum oṭṭuka.
ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccam, 6 ām vēṟṟumip peyar pōla muṉpiṉṉāka māṟiya ceyyum eṉṉum etir kālaviṉi muṟṟāyirukkalām.

ceyteṉa = ceytāṉeṉṟu collumpaṭi, ceytapiṉ; ceytatiṉāl.
ipporuḷi ēṉippā leṇṇiṭaṅkaṭkum oṭṭuka.
i eṉpatu ikaraccuṭṭu. atu aṇmi kuṟiyātu cuṭṭaḷavāy niṉṟatu. valatu, peritu eṉpavaṟṟil atu, itu eṉpaṉa cēymi yaṇmi kuṟiyātu niṟṟal kāṇka.
atu, i eṉpaṉa mutaṟkālattil viṉimutalīṟāy iruntiruk kalām.

			ōṭi			ōṭi = ōṭiṉavaṉ
			eccam			viṉimutaṟ peyar
			naṉṟu = naṉṟāy	naṉṟu = nallatu.
pu, ā, ū eṉpavaṟṟiṉ mūlamum poruḷum viḷaṅka villi. ivi ceytu, ōṭi eṉpavaṟṟiṟkup piṟpaṭṭaviyākum.
ā eṉpatu vaṭatiraviṭa vaḻippaṭṭa intiyil, iṟantakāla viṉi muṟṟīṟākavum viṉiyeccavīṟākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ceytu eṉpatu mutaṟkālattil viṉimuṟṟākavum irutatiṉāl, āvīṟum tamiḻil aṅṅaṉam iruntirukkalām.
nikaḻkāla viṉiyecca vīṟu
ceyya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuccollē, nikaḻkāla viṉiyecca mākap paṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷellāvaṟṟilum tavaṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu āṅkilattil Infinitive Mood eṉṟu collappeṟum etirkālaviṉi yeccamēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
uriyum ilakkaṇamum uṭpaṭa, paṇṭik tamiḻilakkiya mellām, potuvāyiṉum ciṟappāyiṉum, ceyyuḷ vaṭivi lēyē iruntaṉa ataṉāl, paṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ceyyuḷ naṭikkē eḻutappeṟṟaṉa. pala coṟkaḷum col vaṭivukaḷum ceyyuḷ naṭiyil iṭam peṟuvatilli. muṉṉūl naṭiyiyē piṉṉūlkaḷum marapākap pōṟṟi vantaṉa. āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻkaṟṟut tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāciriyarumāṉa tiṉāl, tam aṟiyāmiyāl kālṭuvelārip pōṉṟē pala tavaṟukaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉar. kiṉṟu eṉṉum iṭinilikoṇṭa cey kiṉṟāṉ. ceykiṉṟa eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉivaṭivam, eṅṅaṉamō iṭikkaḻakattiṟkup piṉ ceyyuḷil iṭam peṟātu pōyiṟṟu. ataṉāl ceyyum eṉṉum etirkāla viṉiyiyē nikaḻkālattiṟkum pulavar payaṉpaṭutti vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, paṇṭi nikaḻ kālaviṉiyiṉ eñcukuṟikaḷ, ilak kiyattilum ilak kaṇattilum toṭarntu iruntē vantiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
kiṉṟu iṭinilikoṇṭa viṉiyālaṇiyum peyariṉ marūukkaḷ, tolkāppiyattil āṅkāṅku taṉip paṭavaruvatuṭaṉ, orē nūṟpāviṟ pala oruṅku kūṭiyum varukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. 
puṇariyal niliyiṭipporuṇilik kunavum
viṉiceyal maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum
vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ urupā kunavum
acinilik kiḷavi yāki varunavum
iciniṟik kiḷavi yāki varunavum
tattam kuṟippiṟ poruḷcey kunavum
oppil vaḻiyāṟ poruḷcey kunavum  (tol.735)
utana eṉpatu utavuna eṉpataṉ tokuttal. utavukiṉṟa - utavu kuṉṉa - utavukuna - utavuna. ceykiṉṟa - ceykuṉṉa - ceykuna. utavukiṉṟa = utavukiṉṟavi.
porukiṉṟāṉ - porukuṉṉāṉ - poruṉṉāṉ - porunaṉ.
ceyyiṉ allatu ceytāl eṉṉum vāypāṭṭeccam nilippāṭṭu viṉiyeccamātaliṉ (Subjunctive or Conditional Mood). cariyāṉa etirkāla viṉiyeccamākātu. ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭṭa mukkāla viṉiyecca vāypāṭu kīḻkkāṇum muṟiyilēyē iruttal kūṭum.

ceytu		ceytukoṇṭu	ceyya
(Past Participle)	(Present Participle)	(Future Participle or 
							  Infinitive Mood)
ceytukoṇṭu eṉṟu tamiḻilum, cēcikkoṉi eṉṟu teluṅ kilum, nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam ulakavaḻakkil toṉṟu 
toṭṭu  vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum collēyaṉṟi, ceyya eṉṉum col nikaḻ kālatti uṇarttātu. ceytu koṇṭu irukkiṟāṉ, ceyya irukkiṟāṉ eṉṉum iru toṭariṉ poruḷiyum. oppunōkki uṇmi teḷika.
ceyya eṉṉum col iyalpāka etirkālattiṟkē uriyatēṉum, muṉṉikaḻcci, oruṅku allatu uṭaṉ nikaḻcci, piṉṉikaḻcci eṉṉum mukkāla niliyiyum uṇarttumāṟa āḷappeṟum.
e.ṭu.	maḻipeyyak kuḷam niṟintatu - muṉṉikaḻcci
		maṇiyaṭikkak kaḻuti kattiṟṟu - oruṅku nikaḻcci
		payirviḷiya maḻi peytatu - piṉṉikaḻcci (e.kā.)
ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉikaḻcci piṉvarun toṭarālum, oruṅku nikaḻcci āṭciyiṉālumē aṟiyappaṭum, ceytu eṉum coṟpōl, ceyya eṉṉum col taṉiniṉṟu iṟantakālatti yuṇarttātu. aṅṅaṉam, ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum coṟpōl, atu taṉi niṉṟu nikaḻkālatti yuṇarttātu. mēlum, nikaḻkāla nikaḻcci vēṟu; oruṅku nikaḻccivēṟu. āyiṉum, nīril āḻpavaṉ ciṟu kōliyum paṟṟuvatupōl,ilakkaṇiyar oruṅku nikaḻc ciyip paṟṟuk kōṭākak koṇṭu, ceyya eṉṉum colli nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. āyiṉ, ataṟku nikaḻkāla muṇarttum āṟṟaliṉmiyiṉ, mūvakit 
toṭarccik kālattiyum (Continuous Tenses), muṟiyē, 7 ām vēṟṟumit toḻiṟpeyarālum iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattālum muṟṟeccattālumē uṇarttivantiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
e.ṭu.
taṭṭuppuṭikkaṇ vantāṉ  (tol. col. 77, iḷam uri) - i.kā. 
toṭarcci
kaṇkava rōviyaṅ kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum  (maṇi. 3:13) - ni.kā. toṭarcci
nīrvārttuk kālkaḻuvā niṉṟu  (naḷa. 232) - .
āṭiṉir pāṭiṉir celiṉē  (puṟam. 109)  - e.kā. toṭarcci
ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam (Present Participle) ulakavaḻakkil irukkavum, atip payaṉpaṭuttātu iṅṅaṉam iṭarppaṭuvatu, kaṉiyirukkap pūmpiñcik kavarva toppatē.
paṇṭi yilakkiyattil illāta collellām piṟkālattavi yeṉakkoḷvatu peruntavaṟām. paṇṭi nūlkaḷ iṟṟi akaramutalikaḷalla. potumakkaḷ paḻañcoṟṟokuti talik kaḻakak kālattiṉiṉṟu caṟṟam māṟātu iruntuvantirukkiṉṟatu.
ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum col, tamiḻiṉ aṭippaṭic coṟ kaḷuḷ oṉṟāyum, nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷ pēccil āḻavērūṉṟiya tāyum, tamiḻnāṭeṅkaṇum iṉṟum ceytuviṭṭu, ceytukiṇṭu, ceytukiṉu eṉap palvēṟu kocci vaṭivil vaḻaṅkuvatāyum, tamiḻiṉiṉṟu nīkkamuṭiyātatāyum, uḷḷatu.
kāṭikkaṇṇi, kutirivāli eṉṉum ciṟu tavacaṅkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭup pāṇṭināṭṭil viḷintuvariṉum, avi iṉṟuḷḷa paṇṭiyilakkiyattil iṭampeṟavēyilli. iṅṅaṉamē ceytukoṇṭu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamum eṉka.
āṅkila nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattiṉ īṟākiya ing eṉpataṉ tolvaṭivākac collappeṟum inde eṉpatum, ceytiṇṭu eṉṉum koccivaṭivīṟṟi ottiruppatu peritum kavaṉikkat takkatām.
ceyya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭeccam nikaḻkālaccollaṉmi yālēyē, akaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccam eṉa itukāṟum kuṟikkap paṭṭatu. iṉi, etirkāla viṉiyeccam eṉṟē teḷivāyk kuṟikkappeṟum.
etirkāla viṉiyecca īṟu :
a, e.ṭu. ceyya, koṭukka, iṟappa.
ceyya eṉpatu ceyyal eṉṉum allīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyariṉ īṟukēṭām.
ceyyal (ceyal) vēṇṭum = ceyyavēṇṭum.
koṭukkal vēṇṭum = koṭukkavēṇṭum.
olvaḻi oṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum  (114)
īṟā kakaramuṉik keṭutal vēṇṭum  (115)
vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum  (156)
meynnilip potuccol kiḷattal vēṇṭum  (725)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya aṭikaḷilellām al allatu tal līṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar ceyya eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccap poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka. toḻiṟpeyarīṟukaḷ paṟpalavēṉum, allīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarē īṟukeṭṭu akaravīṟṟik koṇ ṭirukkum. ātalāl, ataṉiṉṟē v.fh.É.īW piṟantatākum. ceytal eṉpatu tallīṟṟatu; allīṟṟataṉṟu.
iṟakka, ciṟakka, viḻikka, kaḻikka mutaliyavaṟṟi, uyar naṭiyāḷar iṉṉōcikaruti iṟappa, ciṟappa, viḻippa, kaḻippa eṉat tirippar.

maḻiyattup pōkarute, veyilattup pōkarute, eṉpaṉa, maḻiyiṟ pōkātē, veyiliṟ pōkātē, eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum maliyāḷa (cēra) nāṭṭu vaḻakkām, ārk kāṉum koṭukkum pōḻ aruteṉṟu vilakkarutu. eṉṉum maliyāḷappaḻa moḻiyil, aruteṉṟu vilakkarutu eṉṉum toṭar, kūṭā teṉṟu taṭukkak kūṭātu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
kūṭātu eṉṉum coṟpōṉṟē, arutu eṉpatum mali yāḷattil taṉiviṉiyāka varum.
e.ṭu. īāḷkku vēṟē paṇi arutu = inta āḷukku vēṟu vēli kūṭātu.

mutaliya coṟkaḷō ṭoppumikoṇṭu īṟṟaṭi nīḷappeṟṟatu. ataṉāl, poruḷum teḷivuṟṟatu. collātu eṉṉum terinili viṉiccol vaṭivam muṟṟāyum eccamāyum iruppatupōṉṟē, allatu eṉṉuṅ kuṟippu viṉiccol vaṭivum muṟṟāyum eccamāyu mirukkamuṭiyum.

il eṉṉumcol, aṭippaṭiyil ciṟumi allatu kuṉṟaṟ poruḷatu. kuṉṟaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu iṉmikkaruttum tōṉṟum o.nō. ceyyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiyaviṉi = ceyyappaṭu poruḷ illāviṉi. Less = of smaller quantity, senselessness = absence of sense.
il eṉpatu oru ciṟumipporuḷ piṉṉoṭṭu. e.ṭu. toṭṭi - toṭṭil, muṟam - muṟṟil - muccil, ili eṉpatu tiṇmiyiṟ ciṟiya ilivaki- ilavu eṉpatu noyya pañcu. illi ciṟutuḷi. iṟi eṉpatu ciṟitu eṉṟum poruḷ paṭuvatu. iṭukiyatu iṭi. iṭṭiki ciṟu ceṅkal. iṭṭēṟi iru varappiṭippaṭṭa ciṟupāti. ulli, olli eṉpaṉa oṭukkaṅ kuṟippatāl ul eṉpatiṉiṉṟu il eṉṉuñ col tōṉṟi yirukkalām.

etirmaṟi viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarīṟukaḷ

	ceyyātavaṉ = ceyyāta avaṉ.

	e.ṭu. ceytilāṉ - ceytilōṉ.
	ceyyātāṉ - ceyyātōṉ.

1. iyalpīṟu 		e.ṭu.	allāṉ, illāṉ.
2. cuṭṭuppeyar	e.ṭu.	allātavaṉ - allātāṉ
					allātavaṉ = allāta avaṉ
3. ā ōṉāṉa īṟu	e.ṭu.	illāṉ - illōṉ.
iciṉ eṉṉum iṭinili
iciṉ eṉpatu ōr iṟantakāla iṭinili eṉṟu caṅkara namaccivāyarum (naṉ. 145, uri), ōr aciniliyeṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum (tol. col, 296), urittaṉar.
iciṉ eṉpatu viṉiyuṟuppāka varum iṭamellām, atu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccattōṭu cērntēyirukkiṉṟatu. iciṉ eṉpatikkoṇṭa viṉikaḷellām iṟantakāla muṟṟākavē yiruppiṉum, paṭarkki viṉimuṟṟukkaḷē perumpālum pālīṟu koṇṭaṉavākavum, ēṉi yīriṭa muṟṟukkaḷum pālīṟaṟṟaṉavākavumē yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṟantakāla viṭinilikaḷellām iṟantakāla viṉiyeccat tuḷḷēyē aṭaṅkiniṟṟaliṉ. ataṉoṭu cērntuḷḷa iciṉ eṉpatu oru tuṇiviṉiyākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. atu ī eṉpatē. atu utaviviṉi yīṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
āyiṉōr, pōyiṉōr, mēyiṉōr, tāyiṉōr eṉpaṉa pōṉṟu, īyiṉōr eṉpatum paṭarkkip palarpāl iṟanta kāla viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarām. yakara cakarap pōliyil atu īciṉōr eṉṟāki iciṉōr eṉṟu kuṟukum. piṉpu atu iṟanta kāla viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ tuṇiviṉiyākac cērntu.
ciṟanticiṉōr  (tol. 295)
uṇarnticiṉōr  (tol. 601)
aṟinticiṉōr  (tol. 643) 
mutaliya coṟkaḷip piṟappikkum.
tolkāppiyar ivviṉiyiṉ amippi muṟṟum aṟiyā tiruntatiṉāl, iciṉ eṉpati aciniliyākak koṇṭu, atiyum ciṉ eṉṟu tavaṟākap pirittu,
miyāika mōmati ikumciṉ eṉṉum
āvayiṉ āṟum muṉṉili aciccol.  (759)
avaṟṟuḷ
ikumum ciṉṉum ēṉi yiṭattoṭun
takunili yuṭiya eṉmaṉār pulavar  (760)
eṉṟu vaḻuppaṭa nūṟpā yāttu.
meyteri vaḷiyici aḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē  (102)
eṉat taṉmiyorumi yiṟantakāla viṉimuṟṟi eṇṇī ṟiṉṟiyum amittuviṭṭār. ataṉāl, piṟkālap pulavar.
vaḻipaṭal cūḻnticiṉ avaruṭi nāṭṭē  (kuṟun. 11)
kēṭṭiciṉ vāḻi tōḻi  (kuṟun. 30)
eṉat taṉmi muṉṉili viṉi muṟṟukkaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟi,
vēntaṉummē .......... puṟampeṟṟiciṉē
pāṭiṉiyummē .......... iḻipeṟṟiciṉē
pāṇmakaṉummē .......... pūppeṟṟiciṉē
eṉappaṭarkki viṉimuṟṟukkaḷiyum (puṟam. 11), eṇṇīṟum pālīṟum aṟṟaṉavāka amittuviṭṭaṉar. itu peritum mayakkatti viḷittuviṭṭatu. ataṉāl, tolkāppiyarkkup patiṉeṇnūṟṟāṇṭu pintiya pavaṇantiyārum,
.......... ciṉ .......... acimoḻi  (441)
eṉa mayaṅkiviṭṭaṉar.
ciṉ eṉpatu aciniliyāyiṉ; atu nīṅkiyapiṉ, nuvaṉṟiciṉē eṉṉum taṉmiyorumi viṉimuṟṟu nuvaṉṟiyē eṉṟum; puṟampeṟṟiciṉē eṉṉum paṭarkki āṇpāl viṉimuṟṟum aṟinticiṉōrē (tol. 643) eṉṉum paṭarkkip palarpāl viṉimuṟṟum, muṟiyē, puṟam peṟṟiyē, aṟintiyōrē eṉṟum; niṟkum. avi āciriyar kuṟitta collākāmi kaṇṭu koḷka.
taṉmiyorumi 		:	īyiṉēṉ-īciṉēṉ
muṉṉiliyorumi	:	īyiṉi-īciṉi-iciṉi
paṭarkkiyorumi		:	īyiṉaṉ-īciṉaṉ-iciṉaḷ
						īyiṉaḷ - īciṉaḷ - iciṉaḷ
4. uriccol
uriccol eṉpatu ilakkaṇavakiccol aṉṟu, atu poruḷkūṟappaṭṭa coṟkaḷum col vaṭivukaḷumākiya aruñcoṟṟokutiyē avaṟṟi,
uriccoṟ kiḷavi virikkuṅ kāli 
..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... 
peyariṉum viṉiyiṉum meytaṭu māṟi
..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... ..... 
payilā tavaṟṟip payiṉṟavi cārttit
tattam marapiṟ ceṉṟunili maruṅkiṉ
eccol lāyiṉum poruḷvēṟu kiḷattal
eṉṉum tolkāppiya uriccol iyalviḷakka nūṟpāvum (782), tolkāppiyar uriccoṟkaṭku akaramutalimuṟiyiṟ poruḷē kūṟiccelvatum, uriccoṟkut taṉiyilakkaṇa vakit taṉmiyiṉmiyum,
palvakip paṇpum pakarpeya rāki
orukuṇam palakuṇam taḻuvip peyarviṉi
oruvā ceyyuṭ kuriyaṉa vuriccol  (442)
iṉṉa tiṉṉuḻi yiṉṉaṇam iyalum
eṉṟici nūluṭ kuṇakuṇip peyarkaḷ
collām parattaliṟ piṅkala mutalām
nallōr uriccolil nayantaṉar koḷalē  (460)
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpākkaḷum, valiyuṟuttum.
(5) aivakic coṉṉili
1.	acinili (Isolating or Monosyllabic Stage)
		e.ṭu. il, āḷ.
2.	puṇarnili (Compounding Stage)
		e.ṭu. il-āḷ.
3. 	pakucoṉṉili allatu īṟupēṟṟunili (Inflexional Stage)
		e.ṭu. illāḷ
4. 	koḷuvunili (Agglutinative Stage)
		e.ṭu. ceyvippi.
5. 	tokinili (Synthetic Stage)
	e.ṭu. maka + kaḷ = makkaḷ, tam + āy = tāy, ātaṉ + tā = āntā – 
	ānti.
(6) coṟpaṭi vaḷarcci
nilam oṉṟaṉmēloṉṟāy aṭukkappaṭṭirukkum pala paṭi kaḷāy amintiruppatupōl, coṟkaḷum oṉṟaṉ mēloṉ ṟāy vaḷarntuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷum acikaḷumākiya pala paṭi kaḷik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
1. coṉṉīṭci
a-al-atu-antu-anta-antā
u-um-umpu-umpar-umparam
u-um-am-ampu-ampal-ampalam
u-ul-uḷ-uṭu-uṭal-uṭaṉ-uṭampu
u-i-e-ē-ēṇ-yāṇ-yāṇam-yāṇar
u-ul-uḷ-cuḷ-cuṇ-cuṇṇam-cuṇṇampu-cuṇṇāmpu
u-ul-mul-mal-maṉ-maṉṟu-mantu-manti
2. toḻiṟpeyar mutaṉiliyātal
naku-naki (to.pe.) - nakippu
kaḷ-kaḷi (to.pe.) - kaḷippu
vaḷ-vaḷi (to.pe.) - vaḷivu
kuḷ - koḷ - koḷum - (to. pe) - koḷumu - koḷumū - koṇmū.
3. iyaloliccol tiriyoliccoṟ poruḷ taral
paḷ-paṇṭu, paḷ-paḻa-paḻami
ar-araṅku. ar-aṟu-aṟi
4. iyaṟcol tiricoṟporuḷ taral
til-tiḷ-tiṇ-tiṇṇi. tiḷ-tir-tiraḷ-tiraḷi. tiraṇi
val-vaḷ-vaṇ-vaṇṇam-vaṇṇakam. vaḷ-var-vari-varaṇam.
vaḷittal = vaḷitteḻutal, eḻututal.
uruvap palpū vorukoṭi vaḷii  (neṭunal 113)
vari = vaḷikōṭu, eḻuttu
varital = eḻututal (piṅ.) 2. cittirameḻututal.
vallōṉ ṟiiya varivaṉap puṟṟa, vallippāvi  (puṟam. 33)
varittal = 1. eḻututal vaḷḷukir varitta cāntiṉ vaṉamuli (cīvaka. 2532).
2. cittirameḻututal.
tiraṇi eṉṉum coṟporuḷit tiṇṇi eṉṉum collē kuṟittalpōl, varaṇam eṉṉum coṟ poruḷi vaṇṇam eṉṉum collē kuṟikkum eṉa aṟika.
vaḷḷutal = vaḷital, vaḷitteḻututal.
5. viṉivaḷarcci
cey - ceykiṉṟu - ceykiṉṟāṉ - ceykiṉṟaṉaṉ.
6. viṉimutaṉili vaḷarcci
kal-kaṟpi-kaṟpippi-kaṟpippippi
naṭa-naṭattu-naṭattuvi-naṭattuvippi-naṭattuvippippi
7. peyarvaḷarcci
naṭattuvippikkiṉṟavaṉiṭattil (7 ām vē.)
(7) piṉṉamippu (Backformation)
oru tiricolliṉ mutali allatu īṟṟi nīkki maṟṟoru colli amittukkoḷvatu piṉṉamippām.
e.ṭu.
muḻuttal = tiraḷutal, paruttal. muḻu - muḻā = tiraṇṭa muracu. muḻā - muḻavu.
muḻā - miḻā = parutta māṉvaki (Stag). miḻā - mēḻam - mēḻakam - ēḻakam - ēṭakam - ēṭu - yāṭu - āṭu.
mēḻam = parutta cemmaṟiyāṭṭukkaṭā; mēḻakam = cemmaṟikkaṭā.
vempari mēḻaka mēṟṟi  (cīvaka. 521)
ēḻakam = cemmaṟikkaṭā, cemmaṟi, veḷḷāṭu.
yāṭu = āṭṭiṉpotu (tol : po. 567)
mēḻakam eṉṉum colliṉ tiripiṉiṉṟu āṭu eṉṉum colli amittukkoṇṭatu piṉṉamippām.
vēntaṉ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu vēntu eṉṉum vaṭivi amittatum piṉṉamippē. vēyntōṉ = muṭiyaṇintōṉ.
vēyntōṉ - vēntaṉ = muṭiyaṇinta cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyaruḷ oruvaṉ. vēntaṉ - vēntu.
(8) coṟpaṇpaṭuttam
1. moḻimutalākā mutaleḻutti moḻimutaleḻuttākkal.
e.ṭu.	lālāṭṭu-lōlāṭṭu, lālāṭṭu-rārāṭṭu - rōrāṭṭu.
eṉpaṉa paṇpaṭāta ulakavaḻakku. lālā eṉṟu āṭṭuvatu lālāṭṭu, rōrō eṉṟu āṭṭuvatu rōrāṭṭu, 
tālāṭṭu - tārāṭṭu, ōlāṭṭu - ōrāṭṭu eṉpaṉa paṇpaṭṭa tamiḻ vaḻakku.
la-ta, pōli, o.nō : calaṅki-cataṅki, kalampam - katampam.
loḷuloḷu eṉpati noḷunoḷu eṉpatum, loḷ loḷ eṉṟu kulikkum nāyi ñeḷḷi eṉpatum, aḵtē.
ṭakaramutal takaramutalāka eḻutappaṭum.
e.ṭu. 
ṭavaṇṭi - tavaṇṭi (oruvakippaṟi).
avari, tuvari, paṉi, mali mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ aikāravīṟu, akaravīṟṟiṉ paṇpaṭuttat tiripāyirukkalām.
2. coṟkaḷip poruṭkēṟpa vēṟupaṭuttal.
e.ṭu.
taliyaṉ, talivaṉ, taliccaṉ, taliyāri, talikkāraṉ
kaṭiya, kaṭikka; kaṭital, kaṭittal; kaṭintāṉ, kaṭittāṉ. 
kaṭital = nīkkutal, kaṭittal = pallāl veṭṭutal.
veḷku - veṭku, veḷku - veḵku.
veṭkutal = nāṇutal. veḵkutal = piṟarporuḷi virūmputal.
nīrniliyik kuṟikkum āṟu eṉṉum colli vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil valiyiraṭṭittalum, eṇṇik kuṟikkum āṟu eṉṉum colli aṅṅaṉam iraṭṭikkāmiyum, paṇpaṭut tattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatē.
tōṉṟaṟpuṇarcci vēṟupāṭu :
pūkkuḻal x pūṅkuḻal, paṉikkoṭi x paṉaṅkoṭi.
tiripuppuṇarcciyāl makaṉmi kuṟittal
kīraṉ + koṟṟaṉ = kīraṅkoṟṟaṉ (kīraṉ makaṉākiya koṟṟaṉ)
kaṇṇaṉ + cēntaṉār = kaṇṇañcēntaṉār (kaṇṇaṉ makaṉākiya cēntaṉār)
piṭṭaṉ + tattaṉ = piṭṭantattaṉ (piṭṭaṉ makaṉākiya tattaṉ)
ivaṟṟiṉ, nilimoḻiyīṟṟu ṉakaramey, varumoḻimutal valliṉa meykku iṉamelliṉamāyt tirital kāṇka. vaṭukañcāttaṉ eṉpatu vaṭukaccāttaṉ eṉṟu valippiṉ, vaṭukaṉ makaṉākiya cāttaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭātu, vaṭukaṉ (teluṅkaṉ) ākiya cāttaṉ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum.
(9) coṟṟūymi
tamiḻ tāṉē tōṉṟiya peruvaḷat tāymoḻiyātaliṉ, coṟṟūymi ataṉ ciṟanta paṇpukaḷuḷ oṉṟām. ataṉāl, paṇṭit tamiḻar atit kaṇṇuṅkaruttumāyp pēṇi, ayalnāṭṭiliruntu vanta poruḷkaṭkellām uṭaṉuṭaṉ tūya tamiḻccoṟpeyarkaḷiyē puṉintiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e.ṭu.
poruḷ tōṉṟiya iṭam	poruṭpeyar
arapi		kutiri, pērīntu
cīṉam		karumpu
amerikkā, pāracīkam	pukiyili
amerikkā		uruḷikkiḻaṅku miḷakāy
teṉ amerikkā		aṇṭimā, muntiri, (Cashew).
mēliyāciyā		(koṭi) muntiri
turukki		vāṉkōḻi
teṉṉamerikkā,		centāḻi
mēliyintiyat tīvukaḷ	(Pineapple)
maliyā	aṭikkāy, pākku pukivaṇṭi
iṅkilāntu	mitivaṇṭi (Cycle)
oṭṭakamum kutiripōl arapināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantatē, tolkāppiya marapiyalil oṭṭakam kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻnāṭu arapi nāṭṭoṭu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaṇikam ceytu vantatiṉāl, oṭṭakam tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantatoṭu ataṉpeyarum ilakkiyattil maṭṭumaṉṟi ilakkaṇattilum iṭam peṟalāyiṟṟu. ataṉ arapip peyar camal (Jamal) eṉpatām. ākavē, oṭṭakam eṉpatu akkāla marapuppaṭi tamiḻccollākavē yiruttalvēṇṭum. oru mātamvari paṭṭiṉiyirukkum tiṟam oṭṭakattiṉ ciṟappiyalpām. oṭṭappōṭutal = paṭṭiṉiyiruttal. ātalāl, attiṟam paṟṟi ataṟku appeyar iṭappaṭṭirukkalām. camaṟkirutattil ati uṣṭrakka eṉṟu tirittu, atiṉiṉṟu oṭṭakam eṉṉum col vantatākak kūṟuvar; ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakara mutaliyilum, iṅṅaṉamē kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷatu. erital viṉiyik kuṟikkum uṣ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu uṣṭrakka eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiyatākak kūṟuvar vaṭavar. itu poruntappoyttal eṉṉum uttiyē. āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukumuṉṉarē oṭṭakam tamiḻakattiṟkuṭ pukuntu viṭṭatu. mēlum, uṣ eṉṉum vaṭacollum uḷ (oḷ) eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ tiripē.
oṭṭakattiṟku neṭuṅkaḻuttaṉ - neṭuṅkaḻuttal, neṭuṅkōṇi eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
tamiḻar mīṇṭum taḻikkavēṇṭiṉ. tam muṉṉōrippōl tamiḻip pōṟṟalvēṇṭum. ākavē, mōṭṭār eṉpati iyaṅki eṉṟum, kāppi eṉpatik kuḷampi eṉṟum, tēnīr eṉpatik koḻuntu nīr eṉṟum, colvatē takkatām.
piṟamoḻiccollik kaṭaṉkoḷvatāl tamiḻ vaḷarum eṉpār, tamiḻaṟiyātavarum tamiḻppakivarum tamiḻik kāṭṭik koṭuppavarumē yāvar.
(10) colvaḷam
tamiḻ mikunta colvaḷamuṭiyateṉpati, nālvakiyilip peyarālum, aivakip pūnilip peyarālum, mukkaṉikaḷiṉ piñcu nilippeyarālum, vevvēṟu ciṟappup poruḷiṟ collutalik kuṟikkum aṟi, iyampu, ici, uri, eṉ, otu, kiḷa, kiḷattu, kūṟu, cāṟṟu, ceppu, col, navil, nutal, nuval, noṭi, pakar, paṟi, paṉṉu, paṉuvu, pukal, pulampu, pēcu, māṟu, miḻaṟṟu, moḻi, viḷattu, viḷampu, mutaliya coṟḷālum, eḻunilip peṇṇiḷamip peyarkaḷālum, piṟavaṟṟālum, aṟiyalām.
veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta kutiriyiṉattikkūṭa, eṇ vaki yākavum tamiḻar vakuttu. avaṟṟiṟkēṟpap peyariṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. mūvēntar kutirikaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉar kutiriyum vevvēṟiṉattic cērntavi. pāṇṭiyaṉ kutiri kaṉa vaṭṭam; cōḻaṉ kutiri kōram. ivi eṇvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkātavi.
koṭi vēṇṭal paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ :
ī eṉpatu iḻintōṉ		āḷum		col
tā eṉpatu ottōṉ		āḷum		col
koṭu eṉpatu uyarntōṉ	āḷum		col
tavacamaṇi paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ :
muṟṟa viḷintatu maṇi; ariviḷiccalāṉatu arivayiṟaṉ; uḷḷīṭaṟṟatu pokku allatu patar.
cottittēṅkāy paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ :
kōṭṭāṉ uṭkārntu kōṭṭāṉkāy allatu kūkikkāy; tēri amarntatu tērikkāy. oṟṟiyāḷ teṉṉi marattaṭi yil iḷanīr kuṭittatāl ēṟpaṭṭatu ollittēṅkāy; muṟkūṟiya iruvakiyum allittēṅkāy. oṟṟiyāḷ kuṭittāl olli paṭum eṉpatu colavaṭi. itu, pakkattilirup pavariyum kuṭippiyātu taṉiyāyk kuṭittal kūṭāteṉṉum tamiḻap paṇpāṭṭi uṇarttuvatākum.
teṇṭil (ōṇāṉ) vakipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ :
cillāṉ, teṇṭil, ōṇāṉ, karaṭṭi, kōmpi (paccōnti)
piṇampaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ
cettaṇimiyāṉatu cavam; iṟantu oru nāṭku mēṟpaṭṭuk kaṭṭuviṭṭatu piṇam; paṉṉāḷāki aḻukippōṉatu aḻaṉ.
yāṉi paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ
āmpal, umpal, uvā, ōṅkal, kaṭamā, kampamā, kari, kavaḷamā, kaḷiṟu, kaṟiyaṭi, kuñcaram, kumpi, kimmali, kimmā, cinturam, tumpi, nālvāy, pakaṭu, piṇimukam, pukarmukam, puḻikki - pūṭki, matamā, vaḻuvi, vāraṇam, vēḻam.
iṅṅaṉam vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇṭaṟkaritu. vaṭa moḻiyi liruppavi, perumpālum tamiḻiliruntu kaṭaṉ koṇṭaṉavum tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppumē.
īrāyiraṅkal teṟku nīṇṭirunta paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, mutaliru kaḻakattup pallāyiram taṉit tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ ilakkiyac coṟkaḷum, iṉṟiruntiruppiṉ, tamiḻc colvaḷam ettuṇip parantupaṭṭirukkum eṉpati uyt tuṇarntu koḷka.
(11) moḻiccemmi
tamiḻar aṟiyāmiyālum cōmpalālum tāḻvuṇarcciyālum tamiḻppaṟṟiṉmiyālum ēṟpaṭṭa koccivaḻakku, nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu tāṉ vantirukkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, ēṭeṭut teḻutum pōtum mēṭiyēṟip pēcumpōtum tiruntiya naṭiyiyē kiyāḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟu, toṉṉūlāciriyar ōr aḻiyā varampiṭṭu viṭṭaṉar. av varampē, puravalarum pulavarum illākkālattum, pakivarum koṇṭāṉmārum palkiya pōtum, pintamiḻi vēliyākak kāttu vantirukkiṉṟatu. ivvarampu ēṉi moḻikaṭkilli. ataṉāl avi makkaḷ vāykku vanta vakiyellām vaḻaṅki, avaṟṟiṉ coṟkaḷ pala oḻuṅkiṉṟiyum urutteriyātum pōyiṉa. tamiḻukku etu vaḻuniliyō atu piṟamoḻikaṭku vaḻāniliyāyiṟṟu.
ceruppu, tiruppu, neruppu, paruppu, eṉpaṉa muṟiyē, ceppu, tippu, nippu, pappu eṉat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉ vaḻu niliyām; teluṅkil vaḻaṅkiṉ vaḻāniliyām.
ikara aikāravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ 4 ām vēṟṟumiyēṟkum pōtu, kuvvurupu kiyyākat tirivatu tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām; teluṅku mutaliya piṟamoḻikaṭku vaḻā niliyam.
e.ṭu.
kiḷikki, malikki - tamiḻ (vaḻunili)
pulikki, akkaṭikki - teluṅku (vaḻānili)
ammi, kari, kuṭi, paṉi, maḻi mutaliya aikāra vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷi akaravīṟāka olippatum, ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatic ceyyēṇam eṉṟu colvatum, tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām, maliyāḷattiṟku vaḻāniliyām.
coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟil varum melliṉameykaḷ, muyaṟciyoṭu palukkappeṟātu mūkkoliyaḷavāy niṉṟuviṭiṉ, tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām, marātti, inti mutaliya moḻikaṭku vaḻāniliyām.
ḻakaratti lakaramākavum, ākāratti īkāra ēkāraṅkaṭkiṭip paṭṭa oliyākavum, olippatu tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām, āṅkilattiṟku vaḻāniliyām.
coṟkaḷ tam ciṟappup poruḷiḻantum vaṭivu citintum vaḻaṅkuvatu. tamiḻukku vaḻuniliyām; piṟamoḻikaṭ kellām vaḻā niliyām.
pāṇṭināṭṭut tamiḻic centamiḻ eṉṟum, piṟa nāṭṭut tamiḻik koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟum, paṇṭikkālattil pirittu vaḻaṅkiyatu, avaṟṟiṟkuc cemmiyeṉṉum paṇpu uṇmi yiṉmipaṟṟiyē, ikkālattuc cila koṇṭāṉmār talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyap patavi peṟṟu, mēṉāṭṭārip piṉpaṟṟi, potumakkaḷ pēcuvatē moḻiyeṉṟu, piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ vaḻu niliyit tamiḻiṟ pukutti, ataṉ cemmiyaraṇit takarkkap pārkkiṉṟaṉar. nakkīraṉmār avarik kaṭuttut tiruttutal vēṇṭum. ulaka moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, cemmi eṉṉum varampuḷḷatu tamiḻ oṉṟē.
(12) marapu vaḻakku
marapāvatu, iḷamippeyar, āṇpāṟpeyar, peṇpāṟ peyar, nilittiṇic ciṉippeyar, vilaṅkuk kāvalar peyar, aḵṟiṇiyuyirikaḷ kattalviṉikaḷ, mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi, uyarn tōr toṉṟutoṭṭu eccoṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷō ac coṟkaḷiyē vaḻaṅkutal.
potuvākap paṟavikaḷiṉ iḷamiyik kuñcu eṉṟum vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iḷamiyik kuṭṭi eṉṟum, colla vēṇṭum. ūruyirikaḷiṉ iḷamippeyar ivviraṇṭil oṉṟāyirukkum. naṇṭukkuñcu eṉṟum elikkuṭṭi eṉṟum vaḻakkumuṇṭu. āyiṉ, naṇṭukkuñci naṇṭukkuṭṭi yeṉṉum vaḻakkam illavēyilli. makkaḷ iḷamiyi makavu, kuḻavi, piḷḷi eṉṟu colvatē marapāyiṉum, kaṭicippiḷḷiyik kaṭik kuṭṭi eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇṭu.
piḷḷi eṉṉum iḷamippeyar ēṟattāḻa ellā vuyiriṉattilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. irukkum piḷḷi mūṉṟu, ōṭum piḷḷi mūṉṟu. paṟakkumpiḷḷi mūṉṟu eṉa oru colavaṭiyuṇṭu. āyiṉ, iv varampiṟantu piḷḷiccol vaḻaṅkuvatu tavaṟām.
kiḻavaṉ eṉṉum col peṇpāliṟ kiḻatti eṉṟāvatu pōl pulavaṉ eṉṉum col pulatti eṉṟākum. iṅṅaṉam amik kātu peṇpāṟpulavar eṉpatu vaḻuvām.
taṉcollit tavaṟāy vaḻaṅkuvatupōṉṟē, ayaṟcolli āḷvatum marapu vaḻuvām. vālipaṉ, vālipap peṇ eṉpavaṟ ṟiṟkup pakaramāka, iḷiñaṉ, iḷiñi eṉṉum teṉcoṟ kaḷiyē vaḻaṅkavēṇṭum.
veṅkāyappal, palāccuḷi, paṉinuṅku, vāḻippaḻac cati, kaṟṟāḻañ cōṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu marapām.
āṭṭiṭiyaṉ, paṉṟimēyppaṉ, kutirivātuvaṉ, yāṉip pākaṉ eṉṟē kūṟutal vēṇṭum.
kākam karikiṟatu, kuyil kūvukiṟatu, mayil akavukiṟatu, ānti kiḷikūṭṭukiṟatu, kūki kuḻaṟukiṟatu, allatu (iraṭṭukiṟatu), tavaḷi paṟiyaṭikkiṟatu, palli muṟkanteṟikkiṟatu, kutiri kaṉikkiṟatu, yāṉi piḷiṟukiṟatu, puli uṟumukiṟatu, arimā uraṟukiṟatu eṉpaṉa kattal viṉi marapākum.
cōṟu cāppiṭutal, kuḷampi (kāppi) kuṭittal, palakāram tiṉṉutal eṉpatē marapu. kuḷampi cāppiṭutal eṉpatu marapu vaḻuvām.
ippoli oru kōṭṭi kāṇum, immaruntu kātu valiyik kēṭkum, eṉ vaṇṭi paḻutupaṭṭu nūṟu urupāvik kēṭṭu viṭṭatu, putuvīṭu oru periya urupāvi viḻuṅki viṭṭatu - ivi pōṉṟaṉavum marapu vaḻukkē.
(13) coṟṟoṭar vakikaḷ
paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷ naṭiyiliruntami yālum ilakkaṇamellām ceyyuḷ moḻikkē eḻutap paṭṭamiyālum, urinaṭikkuc ciṟappāṉa ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷip paḻan tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eṭuttuk kūṟavilli.
coṟṟoṭarvakiyiṟ paṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟiya vellām, aṟuvakit tokinilit toṭarum eṇvakit tokānilit toṭarumē. avaṟṟuḷ, eḻuvāyttoṭarum viḷit toṭarum viṉimuṟṟut toṭarum toṭariyam allatu muṟṟuc coṟṟoṭarākum. āyiṉ, avi irucoṟṟoṭa rāṉa taṉittoṭariyamē.
urinaṭikku ilakkaṇam eḻutappaṭāviṭiṉum, coṟṟoṭara mippu, iṉṟupōṉṟē aṉṟum ulakavaḻakku moḻiyil iruntatu. ataṉāl, taṉit toṭariyam, (Simple Sentence), kūṭṭut toṭariyam (Compound Sentence), kalapput toṭariyam (Complex Sentence), kalavi allatu katampat toṭariyam (Mixed Sentence), eṉṉum toṭariya vaki nāṉkum, nēr kūṟṟu (Direct Speech) nēralkūṟṟu (Indirect Speech) eṉṉum kūṟṟuvaki yiraṇṭum, ilakkiyan tōṉṟumuṉṉarē tamiḻil amintiruttal vēṇṭum.
iii. poruḷ
tamiḻa ilakkaṇattiṉ mūṉṟām pakuti poruḷ eṉpatākum. colliṟkē poruḷuṇmiyālum, paṇṭiyilakkiya mel lām ceyyuḷ vaṭiviliruntamiyālum, ceyyuṭkut toṭariya maṉṟi eḻuttu aci cīr taḷi aṭi toṭi eṉpaṉavē uṟup pākiyālum, colliṟkaṭutta moḻiyuṟuppi allatu ilakkaṇap pakutiyip poruḷ eṉṟē koṇṭaṉar, mutaṉū lāciriyaṉum vaḻi nūlāriyarumāṉa muṉṉūlāciriyar.
ilakkiyamellām allatu eḻutappeṟṟaṉavellām ceyyu ḷāyiruntamiyāl, poruḷil yāppum aṭaṅkiṟṟu. vallōr  aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉavē ceyyuḷātalāl, yāppiv aṇiyum aṭaṅkiṟṟu.
poruḷi akam, puṟam eṉa irupālāy vakuttu avvirupāṟ ṟuḷḷum ellāpporuḷkaḷiyum tam nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāl viyakkattakkamuṟiyiv aṭakkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ipporu ḷilakkaṇamē, paṇṭippulavariyum pulamimikka iṟivaṉaṭi yāriyum iṉpakkaṭaluḷ āḻttiyatu. kumarikkaṇṭap potu makkaḷ moḻiyamippil tam nuṇṇuṇarvip pulappaṭutti yiruppatu pōṉṟē, pulamakkaḷum poruḷilakkaṇattil tam nuṇmatiyic ciṟappak kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. āyiṉ, iti ik kālappulavar kātaṟ ciṟappiyum pōrttiṟattiyumē viḷakkuva tākap piṟaḻa vuṇarntuḷḷaṉar.
veṇpā, āciriyappā, kalippā, vañcippā eṉṉum tali ciṟanta ceyyuḷ vakikaḷikkaṇṭu, yāppiṉum tamiḻi oppuyarvaṟṟatākkiyuḷḷaṉar paṇṭit tamiḻippulavar.
māttiri, eḻuttu, aci, cīr, aṭi, yāppu, marapu, tūkku, 
toṭi, nōkku, pā, aḷavu, tiṇi, kikōḷ, kūṟṟu, kēṭpōr, kaḷaṉ, kālam, payaṉ, meyppāṭu, eccam, muṉṉam, poruḷ, tuṟi, māṭṭu, vaṇṇam eṉṉum irupattāṟu ṟuppukkaḷik koṇṭu; pāṭṭu, uri, nūl, vāymoḻi, pici, aṅkatam, mutucol (paḻimoḻi) eṉṟum eḻunilatteḻunta nālvakic ceyyuṭ kaḷālāṉa; ammi, aḻaku, toṉmi, tōl, viruntu, iyipu. pulaṉ, iḻipu eṉṉum eṇvaki vaṉappukkaḷi (kāviyaṅ kaḷi) ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē, mutaṉmutalāka ulakil iyaṟṟiyavar kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻppulavar. avar ceyyuḷiṟ pēccuvaṉmiyum peṟṟirun taṉar. aṟivilum āṟṟalilum avariyottavar ikkālat toruvarumilli. innāḷiya tali ciṟanta pāvalarum, 
paṇṭi nallicip pulavari nōkka, pāṇarum pullicip pulavarumē (Bards and Poetasters), iṟṟit tamiḻppulavarō kumarikkaṇṭap potu makkaṭkum īṭākār.
ceyyuḷ ciṟanta kalamākavum ataṉ poruḷ ciṟanta amutākavum karutappeṟṟatiṉāl, vēṟemmoḻiyilumillāta poruḷilak kaṇam, tamiḻilakkaṇattiṉ koṭumuṭiyum muṭimaṇiyum mutir viṉivum uyirnāṭiyum tamiḻaṉiṉ taṉipperum perumiyum, ākak koḷḷappeṟṟatu.

antaṇaṉ aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ mutaṉ mutal tamiḻat tuṟaviyariyē kuṟittatu.  (ta.ti.u)
tamiḻappārppār
paṇṭāram, pulavaṉ, kurukkaḷ, tiru(kkaḷ), pūcāri, uvaccaṉ, ōtuvāṉ, pōṟṟi, nampi, arumikkāraṉ (puṭavikkāraṉ), vaḷḷuvaṉ mutaliya palvēṟu vakuppār āvar.  (ta.ti.u.)
tamiḻappūcāri
pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyarum mutaṉ mutal tamiḻap pūcāriyiyē kuṟittatu.  (ta.ti.u)
tamiḻam (1)

tamiḻam (2)

tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum
kumarināṭṭu makkaḷ tamiḻiṉ vevvēṟu niliyil vevvēṟu tici ceṉṟu paraviyuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻiṉ mukkaṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya mūviṭap peyarkaḷ oru kālattil kumarināṭṭu makkaḷoṭu āttirēliyarkkum cīṉarukkum irunta uṟavik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. kālṭuvel tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇam (ceṉṉip patippu) 361 ām pakkam pārkka.

nakaram ñakara makaramākavum, makaram vakaramākavum, tirital iyalpē, taṉmip paṉmip peyar āṅkilattil ‘we’ eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyil vayam eṉṟum tirintiruttal kāṇka.
mēṉ (men) eṉpatu cīṉattil vakuppik kuṟikkum tokutip peyar. atu maṉ eṉṉum teṉcollōṭu toṭarpuṭiya tāyirukkalām.
