turuvutal eṉṉum poruḷil, uruvu, kuruvu,
curuvu, turuvu, - puruvu, eṉṟum; iṅṅaṉamē piṟavāṟum,
ēḻaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruttal kāṇka.  iṅṅaṉam coṟkaḷ amintiruttali vēṟem moḻiyiluṅ kāṇa muṭiyātu. paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭāṉa paṇṭit tamiḻakam muḻuvatum muḻukip pōṉamiyālum, āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyamaṉittum aḻivuṇṭamiyālum, iruvaki vaḻak kiṉiṉṟum pala iṇippaṇṭuc coṟkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭa iyalavilli eṉa aṟika.
iṟṟi niliyatē paṇṭittamiḻum eṉṟu karutik koṇṭum, camaṟkirutatti uṇmiyil tēvamoḻi yeṉṟu nampik koṇṭum, tiraviṭamoḻiyāḷarellām tamiḻiṉ talimiyi oppukkoḷvatilli, āriya mayakkum itaṟkoru kāraṇam, poruḷilakkaṇamum eḻunilatteḻunta paṇṭic ceyyuḷilak kiyamum muttamiḻppuṇarppum tiraviṭa mūlaṅkāṇṭaṟkēṟṟa varalāṟṟuk kuṟippum tirukkuṟaḷum, tamiḻiṉ talimiyi nāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷām.  iṉi, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ cemmiyum tiraviṭac coṟkaḷiṉ koṭumiyum, tuṇic cāṉṟukaḷām.
tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcci nilikaḷ.
(1)	acinili - Isolating or Monosyllabie Stage
(2)	puṇarnili - Compounding Stage
(3)	pakucoṉṉili - Terminational State
(4)	koḷuvunili - Agglutinetive Stage
(5)	piriyānili - Inflexional Stage
(6)	tokunili - Synthetic Stage
(7)	paltokutinili - Polysynthetic Stage
(8)	pirinili - Analytical Stage
eṉa eṇvakip paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
āppirikkarum āttirēliyarum cīṉarum, acinilik kālattil kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu pirintupōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.
turēṉiyar allatu cittiyar eṉappaṭum makkaḷ, koḷuvu nilik kālattiṟ pirintu pōyirukkalām.
avarukkuc caṟṟē piṟpaṭṭa tamiḻaruḷ orucārār vaṭakkē ceṉṟu pirākirutarāka māṟiṉar. avarukkap piṟpaṭṭavar tiraviṭarākat tirintaṉar.
pirākirutarun tiraviṭaruṅ kalanta vakuppār orucārār vaṭa mēṟkākac ceṉṟu airōppāvil āriyarāka māṟiṉar.  avaruḷ oru piriviṉarē intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntu vēta āriyarāyiṉar.
āriyaṉ eṉṟa peyar tōṉṟiyatu intiya nilattilēyē.  airōppiyari āriyar eṉṟatu moḻiyiṉam paṟṟiyē.
āri = mēṉmi.  āriyaṉ = mēlōṉ. itteṉcolliyē āriyar taṅkaṭku iṭṭukkoṇṭaṉar. intiya varalāṟṟu nūlil, āriyar eṉpatu, pūcakarōṭu kūṭiya āriya āyar kūṭṭattiyē kuṟikkumēṉum, tamiḻāriyap pōrāṭṭattil āriyar eṉpatu, āriyap pūcāriyariyē eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
airōppā ceṉṟu āriyarāka māṟiya pirākiruta tiraviṭar, mutaṟkaṇ kāṇṭiṉēviyam (Scandinavia) ceṉṟu taṅkiyataṉāl, atiyaṭuttu tiyūttāṉiya moḻi tamiḻukkuc caṟṟu neruṅkiya tākavum, ataṟkut teṟkiluḷḷa ilattīṉam atiṉuñ caṟṟu vilakiyatākavum, ataṟkut teṉkiḻakkiluḷḷa kirēkkam mika vilakiṉatākavum, intiyā vanta āriyam mikamika vilakiṉatākavum, uḷḷaṉa.

vaṭamoḻiyiṉ ainnilikaḷil tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa.
aviyāvaṉa:-
(1) vaṭukanili (2) airōppiya nili. (3) pirākiruta nili. (4) vēta nili. (5) camaṟkiruta nili.
ivvin nilikaḷilum kalantuḷḷa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyil aintiliru pakutiyākum. tamiḻttuṇiyiṉṟi vaṭamoḻi iyaṅka iyalātu. vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟu kūṟi, teṉcoṟkaṭkellām poruntap poyttalākavum poruntāp poyttalākavum, valintum nalintum poruḷum poruṭkāraṇamuṅ kūṟum vaḻakkam, iṉip payaṉpaṭātu.  ati aṭiyōṭu viṭṭu viṭal vēṇṭum.
palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum tamiḻ talimi yākavum (Main) vaṭamoḻi kīḻttuṇiyākavum (Subsidiary) iruttal vēṇṭum.  potut tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭellām tamiḻilēyē naṭipeṟal vēṇṭum. pirāmaṇarē tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭi naṭatti vikkalām. pirāmaṇak kuṭiyiruppukaḷiṟ pirāmaṇarukkeṉṟu kaṭṭiya kōvilkaḷil camaṟkiruta vaḻipāṭu naṭattikkoḷḷalām.
mutalamiccar, kalviyamiccar, palkalikkaḻakat tuṇi vēntar, tamiḻttuṟit talivar, kalvittuṟi iyakkunarkaḷ, kalvinili yaṅkaḷil tamiḻāciriyaṉmār, varalāṟṟuttuṟi tol poruṭ ṭuṟi āciriyarum ārāycciyāḷarum talivarum ākiya aṉivar patavikaṭkum, nalla tamiḻarē amarttap peṟal vēṇṭum.  pariti māṟkaliñaṉ pōṉṟavarāyiṉ pirāmaṇarum amarttap peṟa lām.
āṅkilappaṭṭam peṟṟa piṟapāṭa āciriyarkkuriya campaḷamē, pulavar paṭṭam peṟṟa tamiḻāciriyarkkum aḷikkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
ārāyccippaṭṭaṅkaṭkuriya iṭunūlkaḷi (Thesis)¤ tamiḻilum eḻuti viṭukka, aracum palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum icivutaral vēṇṭum.
evvakiyilum tamiḻukkut tāḻviruttal kūṭātu.
iravil matiyoḷi ettuṇi viḷakkamāyiruppiṉum kati roḷiyiṉ maṟuniḻalākavē yiruppatu pōṉṟu, iṟṟic camaṟkiruta ilakkiyam ettuṇi virivupaṭṭiruppiṉum tamiḻ mūlattiṉ perukkamē eṉpati, uṇartal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻiṉ taṉiyiyalpukaḷ
tamiḻ, varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta mutupaḻan toṉmoḻiyāta lālum, atu tōṉṟiya paḻam pāṇṭināṭu teṉmāvāriyil muḻukiyum, an nilatteḻunta mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum tamiḻp pakivarāl aḻiyuṇṭum pōṉataṉālum, ataṉ taṉiyiyal pukaḷ neṭuṅkālat tiṭamāṉa naṭunili ārāycciyiṉṟit talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyarālum aṟiyavoṇṇātaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa.  avviyalpu kaḷāvaṉa:-
1.  toṉmi
ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ 
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ
eṉpatu, taṇṭiyalaṅkāra uriyil mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷa paḻam pā.
tamiḻi ulakat takaviruḷi yakaṟṟum cuṭarākac coṉṉatiṉ karuttu.  atu ulaka mutaṟṟāy uyartaṉic cemmoḻi yeṉpatē.  ataṉālēyē, ap pāvip pāṭiyavar, tamiḻi oppuyarvaṟṟa moḻiyeṉṟuṅ kūṟi muṭittār.
eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatākac collappaṭum puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyiṉ āciriyarākiya aiyaṉāritaṉār, kuṟiñciyum mulliyuṅ kalanta pāli nilattu maṟavar kuṭiyiṉ paḻimi yik kuṟikkumiṭattu.
poyyakala nāḷum pukaḻviḷitta leṉviyappām
viyakam pōrtta vayaṅkolinīr - kiyakalak
kaṟṟōṉṟi maṇṭōṉṟāk kālattē vāḷōṭu
muṟṟōṉṟi mūtta kuṭi  (pu.ve. 35)
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu,  kuṟiñci mullivāṇar mikap paḻamiyāṉa tamiḻ vakuppār. avar kuṭiyiṉ toṉmi kūṟavē.  tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyum uṭaṉ kūṟiyavāṟām.
iṉi, muttamiḻt tuṟipōki muṟṟat tuṟantu mūvēntariyum muttamiḻ nāṭṭiyum oppap pukaḻnta cēramuṉivar iḷaṅ kōvaṭikaḷ, ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutiyil tāmiyaṟṟiya cilappatikārattuḷ.
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyu mimayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi  (11:19-22)
eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppatum: aṭiyārkkunallār, toṭiyōḷ puvamum eṉṉum cilappatikāra vēṉiṟkātit toṭarukku,
akkālattu, avar nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭavelli yākiya paḵṟuḷiyeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkum kumariyeṉṉu māṟṟiṟku miṭiyē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ nīrmali vāṉeṉa malinta ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻmaturi nāṭum, ēḻ muṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻ piṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻkuṉṟa nāṭum, ēḻkuṇakāri nāṭum, ēḻkuṟumpaṉi nāṭum eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum, kumari kollam mutaliya paṉmali nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum taṭanīrkkumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉkāṟum, kaṭal koṇṭoḻitalāṟ kumariyākiya puvameṉṟāreṉṟuṇarka eṉṟu viḷakkavuri varintiruppatum: teṉmāvāriyil mūḻkippōṉa paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭiyum ataṉ parampiyu muṇarttum.
paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa toliviṉ aḷavum, palvēṟu nilappakutikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa muntu nūlkaḷuḷ allatu urikaḷuḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.  aṭiyārkkunallār avaṟṟik kaṭṭik kūṟiyirukka muṭiyātu.
iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷuriyiṟ kāṇum mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷa, teṉmaturit talikkaḻakamum kapāṭa (katava?) pura iṭikkaḻakamum, muḻukippōṉa paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭuṭpaṭṭaṉavē.
oru kālattil, teṉṉintiyā, muḻukippōṉa teṉṉila vāyilāka āttirēliyāvuṭaṉum, teṉṉāppirikkāvuṭaṉum iṇikkap paṭṭirunta taṉālum, tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyātalālum, māntaṉ piṟantakam maṭakācukkarp (Madagascar) pakkam eṉṟu (Haeckel) ekkēl pērāciriyarum, taṅkaṉiyikkāp (Tanganyika) pakkam eṉṟu ilīkki (Leakey) eṉṉum māntaṉūl ārāycci yāḷarum, kūṟiyiruppavi, paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭi oru pakuti yākak koṇṭa kumarikkaṇṭam eṉṉum oru perun teṉṉilap parappu, oru kālattirunta mikkup 
puṟaccāṉṟukaḷām.
tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷātalāl, tamiḻ varalāṟṟiṟkuk kumarināṭṭuk koḷki iṉṟiyamiyāta aṭippaṭic ceytiyākum.
mūvēṟu kaṭalkōḷkaḷāṟ paṭippaṭiyāka muḻukippōṉa kumari nāṭṭiṉ teṉkōṭiyiliruntatu kumarimali yeṉṟum, vaṭa kōṭiyiliruntatu kumariyāṟu eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
2.  meṉmi
māntaṉ piṟantakamum tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum paḻam pāṇṭināṭum ākiya kumarināṭu, nākarika nāṭāyiṉum, ulaka mutal nāṭātalāl, atil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tamiḻariṉ vāyil, uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumāka muppateḻuttukaḷē tōṉṟiṉa.  avaṟṟuḷḷum, ḻa,ḷa,ṟa,ṉa eṉṉum nāṉkum pintit tōṉṟiyaviyākum.
tamiḻccoṉ mutalil taṉimeyyum cila uyirmeyyum, col liṭiyil, cila meykaṭkup piṉ cila vēṟṟumeyyum, col liṟutiyil valliṉa meyyum, varuvatēyilli.  ik kaṭṭup pāṭum vilakkum vēṇṭumeṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭavi yalla. māntaṉ vāyoli vaḷarcci nōkkiṉ, akkālat tamiḻar ciṟu piḷḷi niliyiliruntataṉāl, avar vāyil eḷiya muṟiyil olikkak kūṭiya taṉiyolikaḷum kūṭṭolikaḷumē piṟantaṉa. ik kālattum, nelli māvaṭṭa nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷ, cākṣi eṉṉum vaṭacollic cākki eṉṟum, jāti eṉṉum vaṭacollic cāti eṉṟum olippatik kāṇalām. 
iṅṅaṉam eḷiya volikaḷik koṇṭiruntum, tamiḻiṉ ōciyiṉimikku eḷḷaḷavuṅ kuṟivilli.
taṇṭali mayilka ḷāṭat tāmari viḷakkan tāṅkak
koṇṭalkaḷ muḻavi ṉēṅkak kuvaḷikaṇ viḻittu nōkkat
teṇṭiri yeḻiṉi kāṭṭat tēmpiḻi makara yāḻiṉ
vaṇṭuka ḷiṉitu pāṭa marutamvīṟ ṟirukku mātō  (nāṭṭup. 41)
eṉṉuṅ kamparāmāyaṇac ceyyuḷip pāṭik kāṇka.
oru moḻikku vēṇṭiyavi coṟkaḷē yaṉṟit taṉiyeḻuttu kaḷalla.  kumarināṭṭup potumakkaḷ, muppatolikaḷik koṇṭē, akkālattil maṭṭumaṉṟi iṉivaruṅ kālattum māntaṉ maṉattil tōṉṟakkūṭiya ellāk karuttukkaḷiyum kuṟikkattakka, vērccoṟkaḷip piṟappittiruppatāl, tamiḻukkup piṟamoḻi yolikaḷē tēviyilli.
urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum olikkum mūvakic ceyaṟki vallolikaḷum, mūccoliyum, tamiḻiliṉmiyāl atu ciṟu piḷḷikaḷum nōyāḷiyarum kaḻiperu mūppiṉarum eḷitāy olikkat takkatāyiruppatuṭaṉ, ēṉi moḻikaḷi liyalāta nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tiruppukaḻ vaṇṇaṅkaḷum: valliṉap pāṭṭu, iṭiyiṉap pāṭṭu, aṭimaṭakku, iru poru ḷiraṭṭuṟal, palporu ḷiraṭṭuṟal, naṭuveḻuttaṇi, muppāki, patiṉpaṅki, patiṟṟup patiṉpaṅki mutaliya collaṇikaḷum: kōmūttiri, cuḻikuḷam, tāmarik kaṭṭu, cakkarakkaṭṭu, tērkkaṭṭu, irunākap piṇiyal, eṇṇākap piṇiyal mutaliya miṟip pākkaḷum (cittirak kavikaḷum): pāṭaṟkēṟṟatāyu mirukkiṉṟatu.
3.  tāymi
makkaṭ perukkam, vaṇikam, koḷḷikkum pōrukkum paḻikkum tappal, putunāṭu kāṇal, kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya pala karaṇiyaṅkaḷāl, tamiḻar vaṭakku nōkkic ceṉṟu, nāḷaṭivil tamiḻ tirāviṭamākat tirintatāl tāmum tirāviṭarāyiṉar.  piṉṉart tirāviṭaruḷ oru cārār, mēṟkūṟiya karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē vaṭamēṟkākac ceṉṟu, airōppāvi yaṭintu āriyarāka māṟiṉar.
ātalāl, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum āriyattiṟku mūlamumāyiṟṟu.
tamiḻ eṅṅaṉan tiraviṭamākat tirintateṉpataṟku, oru teluṅku viṉiccolliṉ kūṟukaḷē pōtumāṉavi.

eṭuppolikaḷ (voiced sounds) āriyattiṟ pōṉṟē tiraviṭa moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻt tōṟṟatti aṟiyātavarum oppukkoḷḷātavarum, tamiḻilum 
muṟkālattil paya, taya, ṉaya, ṉaya,ye eṉṉum eṭuppolikaḷ vaḻaṅkip piṉṉark ka, ca, ṭa, ta, pa eṉṉum eṭuppila (unvoiced) volikaḷāka māṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉpar. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷpavar ayalnāṭṭārum tamiḻp pakivarum āvar.  piñcu muṟṟik kāyāṉa piṉ mīḷap piñcākātatupōl, eṭuppilā voli polintu eṭuppoli yāṉapiṉ, mīḷa eṭuppilā voliyāka māṟāteṉa aṟika.  teṉ ṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷi mēṉāṭ ṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷākat talikīḻāyk koṇṭataṉālēyē, iṅṅaṉam talikīḻāṉa muṭipiṟku vara nērntatu. itu iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa teṉpati, kuḻanti vāyolikaḷiyum vaḷarntōṉ vāyolikaḷiyum oppunōkkik kāṇka.
tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūlam eṉpati, palvēṟu tamiḻ vērccoṟ kaḷiṉiṉṟum tirintu kuṭumpaṅ kuṭumpamākavum kulaṅkulamākavum vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷuṭ ciṟcila.  āriyattiṟ kuṭumpat toṭarpuṅ kulattoṭarpumiṉṟit taṉittaṉiyāy vaḻaṅkuvataṉāl aṟiyalām.
e-ṭu: kul - kulavu. kulavutal = vaḷital.
L. clino = to bend. Gk. klino = to slope.
kul - kuli - kuḷi - kuḷiki = māttiri yuruṇṭi.
kuḷi - kuḻi - kuḻiyam = 1. vaḷitaṭi, 2. naṟumaṇa vuruṇṭi.
kul - kuḷ - kuḷu - kuḻu - kuḻavi = uruṇṭu nīṇṭa arikal (ammikkuḻavi).
kuḷiki - va. (Skt.) kuḷikā.
L. globus. E. globe = spherical body kulavu - kuravu, kuravutal = vaḷital.

Gk. choros = (orig.)a dance in a ring. L. and E. chorus.

AS. cran, E. crane, D. craan, Ger. krahn, kraniah, Ice. trani,Dan. trane.
Amor, karan, W. garan, Gk. geranos. L. grus. OF. grue.

OE., OS., OHG. hring, ON. hringr, E. ring = circle, circular ornament, (mōtiram).

AS. hoc, hooc, D. hock, Ice. haki, Ger. haken, OHG. hako, L.G. hakc, E. hook.

kōḷ - kōṇ - kōṇam. Gk. gonia, E. penta gon.
kōṇ - kōṭu - kōṭṭam = 1. vaḷivu. 2. matil cūḻnta kōvil.
matil cūḻnta ciṟiccāli. Skt. kostha (kōṣṭa).
kōṭṭam - kōṭṭi = 1. nilāvic cūḻum vaṭṭakkōṭu. 2. matil cūḻnta araṇmaṉi allatu nakar, 2. (Skt.) kōṭṭa. L. castrum.
ivaṟṟi oru pāṉic cil parukkip patam pōlak koḷka. iṅṅaṉam ērāḷamāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷōṭu oliyum poruḷum ottiruppatu, koṭivaḻit toṭarpiṉālaṉṟit taṟceyalāka irukka muṭiyātu.
4.  tūymi
ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ, tūymiyi vēṇṭuvatum, atik kāttuk koḷḷa vallatum tamiḻ oṉṟē.
5.  cemmi
tamiḻar pēriṉamāyp perukip perunilattiṟ paraviyapiṉ, coṟ kaḷiṉ vaṭivu potumakkaḷ vāyil iṭantōṟum vēṟupaṭu vatiyum, avvēṟu pāṭṭiṟku elliyiṉmiyiyum, kaṇṭa ilakkaṇa nūlār, tiruttamāṉa vaṭiviyē aḷavippaṭutti ataṟkuc centamiḻ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar.
irukkutu, irukku, ikkutu, ikku, kītu eṉa vevvēṟiṭattil vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻaṅkiṉālum, ēṭeṭutteḻutum pōtum mēṭiyēṟip pēcum pōtum, irukkiṉṟatu allatu irukkiṟatu eṉṉum vaṭiviyē āḷavēṇṭumeṉpatu, tollāciriyar kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭa cemmiyeṉṉum varampām.
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē. uyartiṇi yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē eṉṟu makkaḷ iyal variyaṟi ceyyappaṭṭirup patāl ataṟkēṟpa ellā viṉikaḷiyum paṇpaṭṭa muṟi yil iyaṟṟuvatē vilaṅkiṉum uyarnta nilimiyik kāṭṭuva tākum.
āyiṉum, ic cemmiyit tamiḻaḷavu ēṉi moḻikaḷ pēṇa iyalātu. tamiḻil vaḻuniliyāyiruppaṉa pala ēṉimoḻikaḷil vaḻāniliyām. ātalāl, teṉmoḻikaḷ tamiḻum tirāviṭa mum eṉa iraṇṭākap pirittaṟippaṭum. tūymiyuñ cemmi yum uṇmiyiṉmikaḷē itaṟkuk karaṇiyam.
6.  mummi
ilakkiya niliyil, iciyum nāṭakamum iyaloṭu cērkkap paṭuvatāl, tamiḻ muttamiḻ eṉappaṭum.
7.  iyaṉmi
ēṉi yilakkiyap perumoḻikaḷilum, ilakkaṇam eḻuttum collum yāppum aṇiyum eṉa nāṟkūṟē koṇṭatu.  tamiḻilō ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum aṟiviyal muṟiyiṟ pākupaṭuttum poruḷilakkaṇamum uṇṭu.
cilar, kātalum pōrum ākiya iraṇṭiyē poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟumeṉpar. avar aṟiyār. kātalallāta ellāp poruḷ kaḷum puṟapporuḷāka vākit tiṇiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
8.  viyaṉmi
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ ulakeṅkum paraviyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: ammi appaṉ mutaliya peṟṟōr peyarkaḷ.
9.  vaḷami
teṟkil īrāyiraṅ kal tolivu nīṇṭirunta paḻam pāṇṭināṭu nīruḷ mūḻkiviṭṭataṉāṟ pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭa taṉāṟ pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭu mīḷā niliyaṭintaṉa. 
āyiṉum, ikkālattum, ili, tāḷ, tōki, ōli eṉṉum nālvaki ilip peyarkaḷum: arumpu, pōtu, malar, vī, cemmal eṉṉum ainnilip pūppeyarkaḷum: kaccal (vāḻi), vaṭu (mā), mūcu (palā), kurumpi (teṉṉi, paṉi) eṉṉum palvēṟu piñcukaḷiṉ ciṟappup peyarkaḷum, uḷḷaṉa.
yāṉiyik kuṟikka irupattintiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa peyarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
tottunōy (Infectious disease), oṭṭuvāroṭṭi (Contagious disease), tikkutal (to stammer), koṉṉutal (to stutter) eṉa nuṭpa vēṟupāṭu kuṟikkum oru poruṭ palacoṟkaḷum nirampavuḷḷaṉa. 
ivviyalpi yaṟiyātār vaṭacoṟ kaṭaṉkōḷ tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉpar.  vaṭacoṟ kalappāl, teṉcoṟkaḷ vaḻakkiṟantum vaḻakkuk kuṉṟiyum poruḷ māṟiyum vērp poruḷ maṟintum iṟantupaṭṭumē pōyiṉa. ataṉāl tīmiyēyaṉṟi naṉmiyilli.
e-ṭu:
teṉcol	vaṭacol
vaḻakkiṟattal:
cūḷ, kaḻuvāy	āṇi, pirāyaccittam
vaḻakkuk kuṉṟal:
āṇṭu, makiḻcci	varuṣam, cantōṣam
poruḷmāṟal:
uyirmey, tōḷ	pirāṇi, pujam
vērpporuḷ maṟital
akaṅkari, pattiriṉi 	ahaṅkāra, patnī
mēlum vaṭamoḻiyeṉṉum camaṟkirutamē āyirakkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷit tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇ ṭuḷḷatu.  ammoḻic coṟkaḷuḷ, ainti lirupakuti tamiḻ: ainti lorupakuti mēliyāriyam: ainti lorupakuti vaṭanāṭṭup pirākirutam: ainti loru pakuti putitākap puṉiyappaṭṭavi.  vaṭa moḻittuṇiyiṉṟit tamiḻ vaḻaṅka muṭiyum: āyiṉ tamiḻt tuṇiyiṉṟi vaṭamoḻi vaḻaṅkamuṭiyātu.
10.  iḷami
tamiḻ ulaka mutaṟṟāy moḻiyāyiruntu patiṉeṇ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip piṟappittum, ēṟattāḻa aimpāṉ āriya moḻikaḷit tōṟṟuvittum, iṉṟum iḷamiyāyiruppatu, ataṉ aḻiyāt taṉmiyik kāṭṭum.  eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻi yampiyici koṇṭāṉ eṉṟār kampar. āriyampō lulakavaḻak kaḻintoḻintu citiyāvuṉ, cīriḷamit tiṟamviyantu ceyaṉmaṟantu vāḻttutumē eṉṟār cuntarampiḷḷi. kāṇka: tamiḻiṉ taṉipperuntaṉmikaḷ kaḻakap poṉviḻāmalar 1970.  (urimi vēṭki poṅkal malar 1974)
tamiḻiṉ toṉmi
nilanūliyal ūḻikaḷ (Geological Eras)
1. toṭakkaniliyuyir ūḻi (Archezoic Era)
2. muntuniliyuyir ūḻi (Proterozoic Era)
3. paṇṭiniliyuyir ūḻi (Paleozoie Era-Primary Period)
4. iṭiniliyuyir ūḻi (Mezozoic Era-Secondary Period)
5. aṇmi niliyuyir ūḻi (Cainozoic Era)
aṇmi niliyuyir ūḻip pirivukaḷ
1.  mūṉṟām maṇṭalam (Tertiary Period):-
(1)	viṭiyaṇmi (Eocene)
(2)	cillaṇmi (Miocene)
(3)	pallaṇmi (Pliocene)
2.  nāṉkām maṇṭalam (Quarternary Period):-
(1)	kaḻipallaṇmi (Pleistocene)
(2)	aṇṇaṇimi (Recent)
nāṉkām maṇṭalap pirivum kālamum
1.  kaḻipallaṇmi
1. 	mutaṟ paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala muṟpaṭarcci (1st Glacial Advance) - ki.mu. 450,000 - 475,000.
2.	mutaṟ paṉikkaṭṭip paṭala iṭikkālam (1st Inter-Glacial Epoch) - ki.mu. 400,000 - 450,000.
3.	iraṇṭām paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala muṟpaṭarcci (2nd Glacial Advance) ki.mu. 375,000-400,000
4.	iraṇṭām paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala iṭikkālam (2nd Inter Glacial Epoch) - ki.mu.175,000-375,000.
5.	mūṉṟām paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala muṟpaṭarcci (3rd Glacial Advance) - kimu. 150,000 - 175,000.
6.	mūṉṟām paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala iṭikkālam (3rd Inter Glacial Epoch) - ki.mu. 50,000 - 150,000.
7.	nālām paṉikkaṭṭip paṭala muṟpaṭarcci (4th Glacial Advance) - kimu. 25,000 - 50,000.
2. aṇṇaṇimi
paṉikkaṭṭip paṭalap piṉṉik kālam (Post Glacial Advance) - tōrā. 25,000 āṇṭukaḷ.
māntak kuraṅkiṉ vaḷarccik kālam.
1.	neṟṟuṭippāṉ kurakku māntaṉ (Nut-cracker man or Sinjanthropos Boisi) kālam - tōrā. ki.mu. 600,000.
2. 	nimirnta kurakku māntaṉ (Pithecanthropos erectus) kālam - tōrā. ki.mu. 500,000.
māntaṉ tōṟṟa vaḷarccik kālam
1.	māntaṉ tōṟṟam - tōrā. ki.mu. 100,000.
2. 	tamiḻaṉ (nākarika māntaṉ) tōṟṟam - tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000.
3. 	muḻu nākarikat tamiḻaṉ tōṟṟam - tōrā. ki.mu. 10,000
mēlināṭukaḷuḷ mutaṉmutalāka nākarikamaṭintatu ekipatu.  ataṉ nākarikat tōṟṟam ki.mu. 6000 eṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ekipatiya nākarikattiṟku muntiya kumarināṭṭut tamiḻ nākarikat tōṟṟam ki.mu. 10,000 eṉṟu kūṟuvatil, oru ciṟitum uyarvu naviṟciyilli. paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟaṅ karit teṉmaturit talikkaḻakat tōṟṟak kālamum atuvē.
māntaṉūṟ pirivukaḷ
1.	uṭaliyal māntaṉūl (Physical Anthropology)
2.	kumukavil māntanūl (Social Anthropology)
3.	paṇpāṭṭiyal māntaṉūl (Cultural Anthropology)
tamiḻiṉ toṉmi
ki.mu. 1000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ epirēyattil eḻutappaṭṭa yūta aracar varalāṟṟilum nāṭpottakattilum (Chronicles) uḷḷa tuki (tōki) eṉṉuñ colli, tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyik kuṟikkum mutalilakkiyac cāṉṟāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār kālṭuvelār.
āyiṉ, ki.mu. 1200 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa āriya vētattil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ uḷḷamiyē, tamiḻiṉ toṉmikku atiṉum ciṟanta ilakkiyac cāṉṟām.
(1) cāyam
e.ṭu.	cāyam (sāyam) = cāyuṅkālam.
		cāy - cāyum (etirkālap peyareccam).
		cāyuṅkālam = poḻutu cāyum vēḷi.
poḻutu cāykiṟatu, poḻutu cāya vantāṉ, eṉpatu iṉṟum vaḻakkam.
cāyuṅkālam eṉpatu, cāyaṅkālam, cāyṅkālam eṉa ulaka vaḻakkil tiriyum.
iṅṅaṉamē, cāyuntaram eṉṉum collum cāyantaram, cāyntaram eṉat tiriyum. cāytal eṉṉum col taṉittum poḻutu cāytalik kuṟikkum. ceykiyilē eṉṉum 7-ām vēṟṟumi yurupēṟṟu ēkāra vīṟu peṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar oru nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattuṭaṉ cērntu, avviṉic collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum ceyal nikaḻum niliyi uṇarttuvatu marapu.
e.ṭu.	varacceykiyilē (varumpōtu), pōkac ceykiyilē 
	(pōkum pōtu)
ic ceykiyilē eṉṉum col, cilē, cē eṉṟu kocci vaṭivuṅkoḷḷum.
e.ṭu.	varaccilē, pōkaccilē
		varaccē, pōkaccē
ik kocci vaṭivu koṇṭa cāyaccē eṉṉum col, cāykiṟa vēḷi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu pōl cāykiṟaccē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa vaṭivu koṇṭa piṉ, cāyaraṭci eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.
cāyaṅkālam eṉṉum vaṭiviluḷḷa cāyam eṉṉum nili moḻiyē, cāyuṅkālam (eṟpāṭu) eṉṉum poruḷil muṉpu vaṭa tirāviṭattil vaḻaṅkip piṉpu āriya vētattiṟ pukuntu ataṉpiṉ camaṟkirutattil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ivvuṇmiyi maṟuttum maṟittum talikīḻāy māṟṟiyum sāyam eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟē cāyaṅkālam eṉṉum teṉcol vantateṉṟu tuṇintu ēmāṟṟuvar.  ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakara mutaliyilum, iti vaṭa colleṉṟu kuṟittatuṭaṉ ivvēmāṟṟuk koḷkiyi valiyuṟuttavē, cāyuṅkālam, cāyuntaram eṉṉum moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam tōṉṟum vaṭivukaḷi vēṇṭu meṉṟē kāṭṭātu viṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
sāyam eṉṉum colliṟku vaṭavar poruntap poyttalākak kāṭṭum vēr muṭittaṟ poruḷi uṇarttum sō eṉṉum col.  atuvum cāy eṉpataṉiṉṟu tirinta cā eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ tiripē.
(2)  tā.
iccol (ilavacamākak) koṭuttal eṉṉum poruḷil, vaṭamoḻiyil tā (da) eṉṟum, ilattīṉil tō (do) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉ tamiḻp poruḷ ottōṉukkuk koṭuttal eṉpatu.
ītā koṭu eṉak kiḷakkum mūṉṟum
iraviṉ kiḷavi ākiṭaṉ uṭiya.
avaṟṟuḷ,
īyeṉ kiḷavi iḻintōṉ kūṟṟē.
tā eṉ kiḷavi oppōṉ kūṟṟē.
koṭu eṉa kiḷavi uyarntōṉ kūṟṟē.
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam (927, 28, 29, 30).
putup perukku nīrik kuṟikkum veḷḷam eṉṉum col taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiyiḻantu nīr eṉṉum potup poruḷil maliyāḷattilum, viṭi collutalik kuṟikkum ceppu eṉṉuñ col taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiyiḻantu collutal eṉṉum potup poruḷil teluṅkilum, vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē; ottōṉukkuk koṭuttalik kuṟikkum tā eṉṉum collun taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiyiḻantu koṭuttal eṉṉum potup poruḷil āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉṟaṟika. toṉṟu toṭṭu iruvaki vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅki varutalum, aṭippaṭiyic cērnta eḷiya collāyiruttal, ciṟappup poruḷ koṇṭu ēṉiyiru tūya teṉ coṟkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭimiyum, tā eṉpatu tamiḻc collē eṉpataṟkut takka cāṉṟukaḷām.  taravu, taruki, taraku, tattam, tāṉam ākiya coṟkaḷellām tā eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tirintaviyē. iṟutiyiraṇṭum vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkuvataṉālēyē vaṭacoṟ pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, tā eṉṉum viṉiccol paṭarkkiyiṭattil varāteṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭiruppatum, atu tamiḻccollē yeṉpati uṇarttum.
tarucol varucol āyiru kiḷaviyum
taṉmi muṉṉili āyī riṭatta.  (tol. 512)
(3)  muttu
iccol uruṇṭiyāṉatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.  itaṉoṭu toṭarpuṭiya coṟkaḷ mutti, muṇṭu, muṇṭi, muṭṭu, muṭṭi, muṭṭi, muṭṭāṉ mutaliyaṉa.
muṇṭu = uruṇṭa kaṭṭi. muṇṭi - muṭṭi.
muṇṭi viḷipaḻam (patiṟṟup 60.6)
muṇṭam = uruṇṭa kaṭṭi, ki kāl taliyillā uṭampu.
mutti = cōṟṟuruṇṭi.  mutti - motti.
muṭṭu = naṭattiṟkuriya uruṇṭa paṟi.
muṭṭi = kommaṭṭi.
muṭṭāṉ = tirunīṟṟiṟkuriya cāṇavuruṇṭi, mañcaṭkiḻaṅku.
mitukkam paḻattik kuṟumuttam paḻam eṉpatu karūr vaḻakku.  uruṇṭu ciṟattirukkum vitikaḷiyellām mutteṉpatu potuvāṉa ulaka vaḻakku.
ulakil muttu viḷiyum palaviṭaṅkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu (maṉṉārkuṭā)k kaṭal.
muttu periyatāyiruppiṉ muttam eṉappaṭum.  am eṉpatu oru perumip poruḷ īṟu (Augmentative suffix):
x.neh.: mati = nilā. matiyam = muḻunilā.
nili = tērnili pōṟ ciṟiya iṭam.  niliyam = pukivaṇṭi niliyam pōṟ periya iṭam.
viḷakku = vīṭṭu viḷakkup pōṟ ciṟiyatu.  viḷakkam = kalaṅkari viḷakkampōṟ periyatu.
muttam eṉṉum teṉcolli vaṭamoḻiyāḷar mukta eṉṟu tirittu, cippiyiṉiṉṟu viṭutali (mukta) peṟṟateṉṟu vērp poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kūṟi vaṭa collākak kāṭṭa muyalvatu, kuṟumputtaṉamāṉa kuṟikkōṭ colliyal (Tendentious Etymology) ākum.
muttu eṉpatu, tamiḻil ciṟitāy uruṇṭu tiraṇṭirukkum palvēṟu poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkum.
muttu: 1. kiḷiñcilil viḷiyum maṇivaki.
muṟimēṟi mutta muṟuval  (kuṟal.1113)	
2.	uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa viti.
e.ṭu. āmaṇakku viti. muttirukkuṅ kompacikkum
(taṉippā. 1.3.2).
3.	uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa āṭṭakkāy.
4.	periyammik koppuḷam.
5.	kaṇṇīrttuḷi parumuttuṟiyum (cīvaka.1318)
6. 	paṉi muttu nīrttuḷi. nīrc cāntaṭinta mū uy  (paripā.10. 13).
uruṇṭu tiraṇṭirukkum eṇṇeyuḷḷa vitikaḷ mutteṉṟē peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu.	āmaṇakkumuttu ( muttukkoṭṭi, koṭṭi muttu), 
	kurukku muttu, vēppamuttu.
muttuccampā, muttuccōḷam eṉpaṉa uruṇṭu tiraṇṭu muttuppōli ruppaṉa.  uruṇṭu tiraṇṭuḷḷa kōrikkiḻaṅku muttakkācu eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu.
muttukkuḷippu varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāta toṉṟu toṭṭup pāṇṭi nāṭṭu maṉṉārkuṭāk kaṭalil naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. muttūrkkūṟṟam eṉṟu or āḷnilappirivum paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭiliruntu kiṟittuviṟku muṉ pāṇṭināṭṭu muttu mēṉāṭukaṭku ērāḷamāy ēṟṟumatiyāṉatum, urōm nakarap perumāṭṭiyar aḷavaṟṟa poṉṉi ataṟkuc cela viṭṭatum, varalāṟaṟinta uṇmiyākum.  pāṇṭiya kavāṭam eṉṉum parumuttavaki cāṇakkiyariṉ artta cāttiram eṉṉum poruḷnūliṟ ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  toṇ 
(nava) maṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa muttu veṇṇiṟamuḷḷa teṉpatu, cīrkeḻu veṇmuttam (pālikkali,9) eṉṉum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkālum, vāytiṟantāl muttutirntu viṭumā? eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkālum aṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi, vēta āriyar nāvalantēyattiṟkuḷ (intiyāviṟkuḷ) kāl vippataṟku īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muntiya pāpilōṉiya moḻiyilum, āriyam eṉṉum pērē tōṉṟuvataṟku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muntiya ekiptu moḻiyilum, maṟukka 
muṭiyāta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ aṭippaṭiyāyuḷḷaṉa.  ākavē mēliyulakil (tōrā. ki.mu. 5,000) mutaṉmutalāka nākarika maṭinta ekiptu nāṭṭu moḻiyil, oru col, irucol alla,  pala coṟkaḷ, aviyum aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ, tamiḻā yiruntatē tamiḻiṉ toṉmikkut taliciṟanta ilakkiyac cāṉṟākum.
ekipatiyat tolvaralāṟ ṟārāycciyāḷarāṉa villiyarcu tūvarṭṭu (Villiers Stuart) eṉpavar, airōppiyariṉ muṉṉōruṭa perumpālār āciyāviṉiṉṟu pāpel mantepu (Babel Mandeb) eṉṉum nīriṇippu (Strits) vaḻiyāka āppirikkak kariyēṟi ettiyōppiyāvil cilakālan taṅkiyapiṉ ekipatu nāṭṭu nīlāṟṟu veḷinilattiṟkup paraviṉareṉṟum, piṉpu aṅkiruntu naṇṇilak kaṭal (Mediterranean Sea) kaṭantu airōppāviṟ kuṭiyēṟi āriyarāy māṟiṉāreṉṟum, ekipatiyar paṉiyōli pōl eḻutu karuviyākap payaṉpaṭuttiya pāppiracu (Papyrus) eṉṉum tāḷ allatu tōkiyuḷḷa nāṇal mutalil apiciṉiyāvilēyē iyaṟkiyāy viḷintateṉṟum, ekipatiyariṉ tēviyal (Sacred) uyirikaḷum 3 -ām 4 -ām 5 -ām āḷkuṭi (Dynasty) aracariṉ kallaṟic cuvarkaḷil variyap peṟṟa vilaṅkukaḷum apiciṉiyāviṟkum teṉṉāppirikkā 
viṟkumē yuriyaviyeṉṟum ekipatiyak kuṟunila aracukaḷi yellām mutaṉmutalāka oṉṟāka iṇittu ōraracāṭci niṟuviya meṉecu (Menes)ntªjD« teṟkattiyāṉēyeṉṟum, ekipatiya moḻi airōppiya moḻikaṭkellām mūlamāṉa acinili (Monosyllabic) moḻiyeṉṟum, atu camaṟkirutat tiṟku 3,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭateṉṟum tam ōr ekipatiya araciyiṉ paḷḷipaṭip paṭamāṭam (The Funeral Tent of An Egyptian Queen) eṉṉum varalāṟṟārāycci nūliṉ iṟutiyil, moḻinūl (Philology) eṉṉum talippil, takka cāṉṟu kāṭṭit tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāyk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
itiṉiṉṟu, kaṭal kōḷukkut tappiyum vēṟṟu nāṭu paraviyum ceṉṟa mutupaṇṭik kumari māntaruḷ vaṭamēṟkākac ceṉṟa oru peru vakuppār, cōmālināṭu (Somaliland) apiciṉiyā (Abyssinia) eṉṉum ettiyōppiyā, ekipatu ākiya nāṭukaḷil muṟiyē paravi, piṉpu airōppāviṟkuc ceṉṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.  ituvē, āttirēliyāviṟkum teṉṉāppirik kāviṟkum iṭippaṭṭirunta kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭamēṟkāc celvārkku ēṟṟa iyaṟkiyāṉa vaḻi eṉpati ñālappaṭam (Atlas)  nōkkik kaṇṭukoḷka.

tamiḻiṉa oṟṟumi

ceṉṟeṟi ciralum paṉṟiyum paṇiyum  (cilap.15:207-14) 
nūṟṟuvarik kolli, taḷḷiveṭṭi, kaḷiṟṟuppoṟi, viḻuṅkum pāmpu, kaḻukuppoṟi, pulippoṟi, kuṭappāmpu, cakaṭappoṟi, takarppoṟi, arinūṟpoṟi mutaliyaṉavumāṉa, muṉṉōr ceyta matiṟpoṟikaḷiyuñ ceyyat teriyātu, 
māṭṭu vaṇṭiyum kutiri vaṇṭiyum nara vaṇṭiyum toṭar vaṇṭik kūṇṭumē ceyyum niliyiluḷḷaṉar.
iṅkilāntiluḷḷa makkaḷ peyarellām peyaraḷavil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉavē yaṉṟi, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟpōl cemparlēṉ mutaliyār, carccil ceṭṭiyār, attilīk kavuṇṭar, īṭaṉ nāṭār, vilcaṉpiḷḷi eṉṟu paruḷaṟṟavālkaḷuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkuvatilli .(iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi nāṭukaḷilum).
aṉṟiyum, aṅku makkaḷ vakuppi viṉaviṉ, toḻilaṭip paṭiyil uḻavar vaṇikar āciriyar kaṇakkar eṉṟu collu kiṉṟaṉarēyaṉṟi, iṅkuppōl āciriyaṉik kammāḷaṉ (āccāri) eṉṟum, kaṇakkaṉi vaṇikaṉ (ceṭṭiyār) eṉṟum, kaṭṭaṭavēli ceypavaṉip paṭittalivaṉ (mutaliyār) eṉṟum, itayaṟkāraṉippaṭakaṉ (pāṇaṉ) eṉṟum, orēn vali bcaypavarippalvēṟu btāḻiṟkulattārākavum, ilvāḻvāṉittuṟavi (aiyar)eṉṟum, pakuttaṟiviṉṟi cbcālvatilli. iṉi, māṇavaṉiṉ aṟiyāmiyip pōkki avaṉukku uṇmiyāṉa ceytikaḷi aṟivikkak kaṭamippaṭṭuḷḷa āciriyaṉ,  māntaṉūlum (Anthropology) uṭalnūlum (Physiology) kaṟṟavaṉā yiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku māṟāka, piṟappaṭippaṭiyil avaṉi ōr iṉamākavum (Species) taṉṉi maṟṟōriṉamākavum kūṟuvatum, iṅṅaṉamē kalviyatikāriyum kalvittuṟi iyakkunarum kalviyamiccarum palkalikkaḻakat tuṇik kaṇkāṇakaruṅ kūṟuvatum, āciriyaṉ māṇavaṉip piṟappil tāḻntavaṉeṉṟum āciriyaṉip piṟappil tāḻntavaṉeṉṟum koḷvatum, ettuṇik kēṭāṉa ceyal! ittakik kalviyāl oru nāṭu muṉṉēṟa muṭiyumō?
piṟavik kulap piriviṉiyāl viḷiyum peruṅ kēṭukaḷ
(1)	ulakuḷḷa aḷavum oṟṟumi yiṉmi.
(2)	iṉa muṉṉēṟṟa miṉmi.
(3)	tamiḻ moḻi yilakkiya vaḷarcci yiṉmi.
(4)	tamiḻap paṇpāṭ ṭaḻivu.
(5)	aṭimittaṉamum tāḻvum toṭarki.
(6)	mativiḷakkamum uṭal valimiyumuḷḷa makappēṟiṉmi.
(7)	ayalārkkum takutiyillārkkum patavip pēṟu.
(8)	pakuttaṟivip payaṉ paṭuttāmi.
oruvar ettuṇi yuyariṉum, tammi nāṭār eṉṟu collik koḷḷiṉ, kula ēṇiyil avarukkuriya paṭiyil tāṉ avari vippar.  ataṉāl avar veḷḷāḷaroṭu uṟavāṭa muṭiyātu.  aṅṅaṉamē, veḷḷāḷarum ettuṇi uyarvā yiruppiṉum, avar pirāmaṇarukkut tāḻntavarākavē karutappaṭuvar.  ātalāl, āriyak kula ēṇiyit tūḷtūḷākkic cuṭṭerittal vēṇṭum. oru kulattāriṭi oru naṟpaḻakka miruppiṉ, atip piṟarum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum; tīppaḻakka miruppiṉ ati viṭṭoḻikka vēṇṭum. nākarikam, tuppuravu, oḻukkam, kalvi, celvam, atikāram, matinuṭpam mutaliyavaṟṟālēyē uṇmiyāṉa uyarvuṇṭākum.  nāṉ kārkātta veḷḷāḷaṉ eṉṟum, nāṉ paṭittalik kavuṇṭaṉ eṉṟum, colvatāl maṭṭum uyarvuṇṭākātu.  turukkiyit tirutti muṉṉēṟṟiya kamāl pācā pōl, oruvar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkut tēvi.
tamiḻukkut tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ ceyyavēṇṭiyavi
1.	ceṉṉi mānilattiṟkut tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟu peyariṭṭu moḻi yiyaṟ kalinākarikat taṉṉāṭci (Linguistic and Cultural Autonomy) amittal.
2.	taṉittamiḻp paṟṟum tamiḻilakkiyavaṟivum uḷḷa āciriya riyē kalviyamiccarāka amarttutal.
3.	taṉittamiḻppaṟṟum tamiḻaṟivum uḷḷavarāy ōyvupeṟṟuḷḷa, kalvittuṟiyiyakkunar, peruṅkallūri mutalvar, talimip pērāciriyar ākiyōriyē, tamiḻnāṭṭup palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ tuṇikkaṇkāṇakarāka (Vice-Chancellors) amarttutal.
4.	iruvaki vaḻakkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷiyum aṟintavarum, vaṇṇaṉi moḻiyiyaliyaṉṟi varaṉmuṟi moḻi nūliyē kaṭippiṭippavarum, vaṭamoḻiyintit tākkuta liṉiṉṟu tamiḻikkāppavarum, uṇmiyi yurikkum tiṇmiyuḷḷavarumāṉa tamiḻp pērāciriyariyē, ceṉṉi aṇṇāmali maturiyākiya muppalkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷilum tamiḻt tuṟit talivarāka amarttutal.
5.	tamiḻukku māṟāka vēli ceyyum tamiḻāciriyar, talimi yāciriyar, mutalvar, tuṇikkaṇkāṇakar ākiyōrip pataviyiṉiṉṟu nīkkutal.
6.	intiyāl tamiḻ keṭuvatu tiṇṇamātalāl, tamiḻ āṅkilam ākiya irumoḻit tiṭṭattiyē, kalvi, āḷviṉi (Administration), vaḻakkut tīrppu ākiya muttuṟiyilum ellā maṭṭattilum kiyāḷutal.
7.	tamiḻppaṟṟaṟṟa pirāmaṇat tamiḻppaṇṭitar koṇṭāṉ mārit tuṇikkoṇṭu tokutta ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakarāti eṉṉum kalavimoḻi akara mutaliyi uṭaṉē tiruttutal.
8.	tamiḻnāṭṭu uṇmivaralāṟṟi eḻutuvittup pāṭamāk kalum, tamiḻppakivarāl eḻutappaṭṭa poy varalāṟṟip puṟakkaṇittalum.
.9. 	centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutaliyi muḻu niṟivāka uruvākkutal.
10.	cemmiyāṉa āṅkilat tamiḻakaramutali yoṉṟu tokup pittalum, kaliyaṟiviyal kammiyakkuṟiyīṭukaḷit tūya tamiḻil moḻipeyarttalum.
11.	pērāyakkaṭciyāḷar tokutta kalikkaḷañciya naṭiyiyum, atiluḷḷa tamiḻippaṟṟiya tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷiyum, tiruttutal.
12.	acciṟku varāta tamiḻccoṟkaḷiyum tamiḻ ēṭṭup pottakaṅ kaḷiyum takka aṟiñarikkoṇṭu tokup pittal.
13.	kalveṭṭut tokuppum veḷiyīṭum takka tamiḻaṟiñarik koṇṭu ceyvittal.
14.	pāṭappottakaṅkaḷit tūya tamiḻil eḻutuvittalum, tūyatamiḻ nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭum kaḻakaṅkaḷi ūkkutalum.
15.	tūya tamiḻiṟ pēcum māṇavarkkum tūya tamiḻiṟ ciṟanta nūliyaṟṟum aṟiñarkkum paricaḷittal.
.16.	āṭpeyar, iṭappeyar, poruṭpeyar, paṭṭappeyar, ciṟappuppeyar ākiya ellāppeyariyum tūya tamiḻākkal.
17.	ilakkaṇam, ici, nāṭakam, kaṇiyam, maruttuvam mutaliya paḻantamiḻ aṟiviyalkaḷiyum kalikaḷiyum muṉpōl tūymippaṭuttutal.
18.	araciṉar aṟivippukkaḷum viḷamparaṅkaḷum eḻuttup piḻiyum ilakkaṇap piḻiyumiṉṟi veḷivarac ceytal.
19.	tamiḻiyum patiṉeṇ tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiyum oruṅkē kaṟpikkum oru kallūri niṟuvutal.
20.tamiḻppaṟṟuḷḷavariyē, caṭṭacapi pārāḷumaṉṟa vēṭ pāḷaraḷākat terinteṭukkavum tērnteṭukkavum ceytal.
21.	vaṇṇaṉi aṭippaṭiyiṉṟi varalāṟṟaṭippaṭiyilēyē moḻi nūli vaḷarttal.
22.	kōyilvaḻipāṭum, iruvakic caṭaṅkukaḷum tamiḻil naṭipeṟacceytal.
23.	tamiḻukkāka uyiriḻantōr kuṭumpattiṟku vāḻkki naṭip poruḷ aḷittal.
24.	tamiḻppaṟṟāṟ pataviyiḻantōrip piṉṉōkkiya valimi yoṭu (Retrospective effect) mīṇṭum pataviyil iruttutal.
25.	varuvāyaṟṟa peruntamiḻp pulavarkku utavic campaḷam aḷittal.
26.	tamiḻuṇarcciyip parappip potumakkaṭkut tamiḻaṟivu pukaṭṭum taṉittamiḻ itaḻkaṭkup poruḷutavi ceytal.
27.	mēṉāṭṭārit tuṇikkoṇṭu, teṉmāvāriyil āḻa mūḻkip paḻamporuḷeṭut tārāya ēṟpāṭu ceytal.
28.	alukkiṟkum aḷapeṭikkum pōtiya iṭiveḷi viṭṭu, immiyum picakātu tiyākarāciyar kīrttaṉikaḷ pōṉṟē iṉpamāyp pāṭakkūṭiya uyariya meṭṭup pāṭal kaḷi aṟivum oḻukkamum taḻuviya potup poruḷkaḷ paṟṟi, iyaṟṟittarum icivāṇarkkum pāvalarkkum ciṟanta paricaḷittal.
29.	ūrkāvalar paṭittuṟiyiṉar uṭaṟpayiṟciyum meyk kāṭṭum (Parade) tamiḻ ēvaṟ coṟkaḷikkoṇṭu naṭappittal.
30.	kūṭṭaṅkaḷilum mānāṭukaḷilum iṟutiyil ulaka vāḻttup pāṭṭu tamiḻiṟ pāṭal.
31.	maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, (P.T.) cīnivāciyaṅkār, (V.R) irāmac cantiratīṭcitar ākiya mūvarkkum ceṉṉiyil paṭimi niṟuvutalum, ammūvar muḻuvuruvappaṭaṅkaḷiyum mutaṉmi yāṉa araciyal aluvalakaṅkaḷilum potuk kūṭaṅkaḷilum māṭṭivittalum.
32.	ceyyuṭkē ciṟappākavuriya ilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷi nīkkiviṭṭu, urinaṭi yilakkaṇatti maṭṭum virivākavum viḷakka mākavum eḻutip potuttamiḻ māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka vittal.
33.	ikarattiṉ neṭilik kuṟittaṟku, iṭikkālattil āriyarāṟ pukuttappaṭṭa, vāyiṟkālpōṉṟa kiranta vari vaṭivi nīkki viṭṭu, iṟutiyil cuḻikoṇṭa paḻiya ikara vaṭiviyē kiyāḷutal.
34.	iṅkilāntil 1875ām āṇṭiṟ ceytavāṟu, tamiḻnāṭṭil ilavacak kaṭṭāyat tuvakkakkalviyic caṭṭa vāyilāyp pukuttutal.
35.	aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻaka moḻiyiyal tuṟit toṭakka viḻāttalivar par. caṭṭarciyiṉ acciṭṭa (tamiḻukku māṟāṉa) talimiyuriyi, akkaḻakattiṉiṉṟu akaṟṟutal.
36.	par. caṭṭarciyum par. kattirēyum intiya ñāla nūliyal varalāṟu - mutaṉmaṭalattil (Gazetteer of India - Vol.I) tamiḻip paṟṟi varintuḷḷa tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷit tiruttutal.
37.	tiruviyāṟṟu aracar kallūriyil, camaṟkiruta mutalvariṉ kīḻirukkum tamiḻttuṟiyip pirittut tamiḻ mutalvariṉ kīḻ amittal.
tamiḻukku veḷināṭṭiṟ ceyyavēṇṭiyavi
1.  uṇmiyāṉa tamiḻ varalāṟṟi āṅkilattil veḷiyiṭṭu ulaka muḻutum parapputal.
2. veḷināṭukaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, nūlkaḷ, kalikaḷ, paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ, vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi ārāyumāṟu, takka ārāycciyāḷarik kāṉā, ekiptu, piraṉīcu malināṭu mekcikkō (Mexico), kāmpōtiyā, ciyām, malēyā, cālittīvu mutaliya nāṭukaṭku aṉupputal.
3.	veḷināṭukaḷuṭaṉ pōṉṟē, intiya naṭuvaṇaracōṭum piṟa intiya mānilaṅkaḷōṭum, eḻuttuppōkkuvarattu āṅkilattilēyē naṭattutal.
4.  	ovvoru perunāṭṭilum oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar patavi ēṟpaṭacceytal.
5.  	tamiḻmoḻi nākarikap paṇpāṭṭi eṭutturikkum oru viṭimukar kuḻuvi (Delegation), ulakañ cuṟṟivaracceytal.
6.	tamiḻar peruntokiyiṉarāyuḷḷa ilaṅki pōṉṟa veḷi nāṭukaḷil, intiyat tūtāṇmikkuḻuvil, tamiḻp paṟṟum tamiḻaṟivumuḷḷa tūyatamiḻar oruvarum irukkac ceytal..
7.	ākkacuppōrṭu (Oxford) āṅkila akaramutaliyilum pirit tāṉiyak kalikkaḷañciyattilum, (Encyclopedia Biritanica) tamiḻip paṟṟiya tavaṟāṉa kuṟippukkaḷiyum uṭaṉē tiruttacceytal.
tamiḻē ulakamutal tāymoḻi
cāṉṟukaḷ:-
1.	tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiyami.
2.	paḻantamiḻ tiraviṭamoḻikaṭkut tāyāyiruttal.
3.	āriya moḻikaḷākak karutappaṭum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi tamiḻāyiruttal.
4.	mēliyāriya moḻikaḷilum aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ pala tamiḻāyiruttal
5.	irumutu kuravarik kuṟikkum ammi appaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻc coṟkaḷ tirintum tiriyātum perumpāṟ perumoḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkutal.
6.	ellā moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷilum oṉṟiraṇṭēṉum tamiḻc colliruttal.
7.	āriyamoḻikaḷiluḷḷa cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷellām tamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyiruttal.
8.	tamiḻilakkaṇa amitikaḷ pala moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟutal.
9.	pala moḻikaṭku allatu moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaṭkuc ciṟappākac collappeṟum ilakkaṇa amitikaḷiṉ mūlanili, tamiḻil iruttal.
tayir - tati (dadhi) - i.vē.
ti - tayir. o.nō: ai - ayir (nuṇmi), mi - mayir (kariyatu), vi - vayir (kūrmi).  pi - payir (paciyatu).
tittal = kuttutal, muṭkuttutal.
kāṉavēl muṭṭikkuṅ kāṭu  (poyyāmoḻippulavar).
pāliṟku uṟimōriṭutali uṟikuttutal, piri kuttutal eṉpatu vaḻakku.
tati - tahī (i.)  (va.va: 172).
taraṅkam - taraṅka = ali.
tuḷaṅkutal = 1. acital.
tuḷaṅkimil nallēṟṟiṉam  (culit.106).
2. nilikalaṅkutal.
kaṭimaran tuḷaṅkiya kāvum  (puṟam.23.).
tuḷaṅku - taḷaṅku - tayaṅku. tayaṅkutal = acital.
tayaṅkiya kaḷiṟṟiṉmēl  (kalit. 31:10).
taḷaṅku - taraṅku = acintiyaṅkum ali.
taraṅkāṭun taṭanīr  (tēvā.461 : 1).
taraṅku - taraṅkam =1. ali.
nīrttaraṅka neṭuṅ kaṅki (periyapu. taṭuttāṭ. 165).
2. iciyali.
oṇṭaraṅka vicipāṭu maḷiyaracē  (tēvā. 87: 1).
o.nō: araṅku - araṅkam.  alital = acital.
ali = aliyum nīrttiri.
ampōtaraṅkam = karinōkkivarum nīralipōl varavarac ciṟuttuvarum kalippāvuṟuppu.
ampōtaraṅkam aṟupatiṟṟaṭittē  (to. 1408).
tolkāppiyam vaḻinūlātalāl, āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa iṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē ampōtaraṅka vottāḻicik kali vaḻakkiliruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭavar taram + ka (paya) eṉṟu pakuttu, kuṟukkē celvatu eṉṟu mūlap poruḷurippar.  taram eṉpataṉ mūlam trū. itu turuvu eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟirivu.  ka eṉpatu ē eṉpataṉ kāṉmuḷi. 
ē - yā - jā - kā - ka.  (va.va. 172, 173.)
tarukkam - tarkka.
tarukkutal = 1. cerukkutal.
taṉṉi viyantu tarukkalum  (tirikaṭu. 38).
2. mikuttuk kūṟutal.
taṉṉoṭu mavaḷoṭun tarukkiya puṇarttu  (tol.996).
3.  pōrūkkaṅ koḷḷutal.
vempōrt tarukkiṉār mintar  (cīvaka. 1679).
4. tākkutal, iṭittal.
taṉmaruppāl veṇpiṇṭi cērat tarukki (patiṉo.tiruvīṅkōy.40).
5. takarttal.
marakkala miyaṅkavēṇṭi ... tarukkiyaviṭattu  (kamparā.mīṭcip 171). (va.va:)
tarukku = 1. cerukku. 2. etirkkum valimi.
etirceyun tarukkilāmiyiṉ  (kamparā. tāṭaki. 40).
3. coṟpōr.  tarukkiṉāṟ camaṇceytu  (tiv. periyati.2.1:7).
tarukku - tarukkam =1. coṟpōr.
poruvaru tarukkañ ceyya  (kantapu. tatīciyut. 157).
2.  tarukka nūl.
tamiḻt tarukka nūl eḻu poruṭ pākupāṭṭi aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭatu.  tarkka paripāṣi eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷa akattiyat tarukka nūṟpākkaḷik kāṇka.  tamiḻt tarukka nūliyē vaṭavar vicēṭikam eṉa vakuttaṉar.  (va.va: 174 -175).
talikkaṭṭu
kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum maṭṭum uḷḷa kuṭumpam; kuṭumpam kaṇavaṉum maṉivi makkaḷum cērnta kūṭṭam; kuṭumpu pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cērnta kūṭṭam; il allatu kuṭi pala tali muṟiyākat toṭarntu varum peruṅ kuṭumpu: marapu toṭarntu varum kuṭi vaḻi; kulam pala kuṭikaḷ cērnta tokuti (e.ṭu) paḷḷiyar, kavaṇṭar, akampaṭiyar, kuṭiyāṉavar mutaliya pala kuṭikaḷ cērnta tokuti vēḷāṇkulam; iṉam orēvakiyāṉa moḻipēcum pala kulattokuti (Nalion) varaṇam niṟaṅ kāraṇamāka ēṟpaṭṭa māperum kulam (Race).  (col: 47)
talikkaḻakam
muttamiḻum oruṅkē kaṟṟa pulavar nūṟṟukkaṇakkiṉar tōṉṟiya taṉāl, paḻiya ilakkiyatti ārāyavum putiya ilakkiyatti iyaṟṟavum, pāṇṭiyaṉ paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟaṅkari mēlirunta maturi yeṉṉum taṉ talinakaril oru kaḻakam niṟuviṉāṉ. ataṉ uṟuppiṉar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉ patiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭu nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṉpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉṟum, avarāṟ pāṭap paṭṭaṉa mutunāriyum mutukurukum kaḷariyāviriyum pala paripāṭalum piṟavumeṉṟum, akkaḻakatti naṭatti vanta pāṇṭiyar kāyciṉavaḻuti mutal kaṭuṅkōṉ īṟāka eṇpat toṉ patiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruṭ pāvaraṅkēṟiṉār eḻuvareṉṟum, akkaḻakam irunta kālanīṭci nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṉpatāṇ ṭeṉṟum, iṟiya ṉārakapporuḷ uriyiṟ collap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
akkaḻaka vuṟuppiṉar peyarkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. akattiyar iṭikkaḻakak kuliviṟkuppiṉ vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta āriyar, mutaliru kaḻaka vuṟuppiṉarum tūya tamiḻarāvar.  civaṉ peyarum murukaṉ peyaruṅ koṇṭa iruvar talikkaḻaka vuṟuppiṉarā yiruntirukkalām.  murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar ki.mu. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭup pārata kālattavar.  nitiyiṉ kiḻavaṉ eṉpatu, vaṭaticit talivaṉik kuṟikkum tolkatik kaṭṭuppeyar.
talik kaḻakam ki.mu. 10,000 pōl tōṉṟiṟṟu. ak kālattu makkaḷ ikkālattāriṉum mika nīṇṭu vāḻntirupparātalāl, carācari āḷukku 50 āṇṭu vittukkoḷḷiṉ, 89 pāṇṭi yarukkum 4450 āṇṭākum. mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kālanīṭci 4440 āṇṭu. itu muṟṟum poruttamāṉatum nikaḻntirukkak kūṭiyatumākum.
talikkaḻakattiṟku aḷavi nūlāyirunta māpiṇṭa nūṟ peyarum maṟintu viṭṭatu. akattiyam oru māpiṇṭamāyiṉum, iṭikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatātaliṉ, talikkaḻaka nūlā yiruntirukka muṭiyātu.  eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum mukkūṟ ṟilakkaṇamuṅ koṇṭa iyaṟṟamiḻ nūl piṇṭam; iyalici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻilakkaṇamuṅ koṇṭa nūl māpiṇṭam. ovvoru tamiḻum ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum irupāṟpaṭṭatu. muttamiḻkkum ilakkaṇam orē nūlāyirukkum; āyiṉ, ilakkiyam vevvēṟu vaṉappuk kaḷākavum paṉuvalkaḷākavum irukkum. iyaṟṟamiḻikkaṇap poruṭkūṟṟil yāppum uvami yeṉṉum aṇiyum aṭaṅkum.
talikkaḻakam varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmiyatātalāl, atip paṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉiyiṟ cila viḷattaṅkaḷ ki.pi. 6-ām allatu 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil variyappaṭṭavuriyil, tavaṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatu iyaṟkiyē.
talikkaḻakam toṭaṅkiṉapiṉ, ulakiyaṟ kalikaḷum aṟiviyal kaḷum vaḷarntu vantatupōṉṟē, mataviyal ārāycciyum āḻntu vaḷarntatu.  civa neṟikkum tirumāl neṟikkum mēṟpaṭṭa, kaṭavuḷ neṟiyeṉṉum potu neṟiyum kaṇ ṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. potu makkaḷ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkattiyum, pulamakkaḷ peruntēva matattiyum, tuṟaviyar kaṭavuḷ neṟiyiyum, kaṭippiṭittaṉar.
āyiṉ, kāḷi pōrveṟṟit teyvamākavum ammi nōyt teyvamākavuṅ karutappaṭṭataṉāl, nāḷaṭivil aintiṇip potut teyvamāṉāḷ.  tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭumākiya teṉṉilattiṉ teṉkōṭiyilirunta perumalit toṭarukkum, vaṭakōṭiyilirunta pērāṟṟiṟkum, kumari yeṉṉum kāḷiyiṉ peyarē iṭappeṟṟatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ matikkulattāṉātalāl, avaṉ mutal talinakar avaṉ kula mutalvaṉ peyari yoṭṭi maturi yeṉappaṭṭatu.  mati - matiri - maturi. o.nō: kuti - kutiri.  kutittal tāṇṭutal.
viki maturi piṟkālatta tātalāl, maruta muṉṟuṟi eṉṉum toṭariṉiṉṟu maturip peyar vanta teṉpatu poruntātu.  paḵṟuḷi maturip peyarē viki maturikkum piṟkālattil iṭappaṭṭatu.
talikkaḻakak kāla nākarikamum paṇpāṭum
āriyar teṉṉāṭu varukikku muṟpaṭṭa mutaliru kaḻakat taṉit tamiḻ nūlkaḷaṉittum aḻiyuṇṭu pōyiṉum, moḻiyamip piṉiṉṟum mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṉiṉṟum kaṭikkaḻakap pāṭal kaḷilum paṉuvalkaḷilumuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷiṉiṉṟum, talikkaḻakak kāla nākarikap paṇpāṭṭi oru vāṟuṇaralām.
moḻi
aḵṟiṇiyiṉiṉṟu makkaḷip pirittuk kāṭṭuvatum, oru nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ nākarikap paṇpāṭṭu aḷavukōlum, moḻiyē.  moḻiyiṉālēyē māntaṉ naraṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.  naralutal = 1. olittal.  āṭukaḻi naralum cēṭcimi  (puṟam. 120). 2. kattutal. veṇkuruku naralavīcum nuṇpaḵṟu valiya (akam. 14) 3. pēcutal.
naral - naraṉ = māntaṉ. vaṟitē niliyāta vimmaṇṇulakiṉ naraṉāka vakuttaṉi  (tēvā. 934:2).
naraṉ - naram = māntappiṟavi.  narattilum piṟattirāta (tiv. tiruccan. 29).
vāṉaram (vālnaram) = vāliyuṭiya māntaṉ pōṉṟa vilaṅku. vāṉara mukaṉ  (cīvaka. 1168).
naraṉ - va. nara. vāṉaram  (va. vānara.)
nara eṉṉuñ colliṟku vaṭamoḻiyil vērilli.  vāṉara eṉṉuñcolli nara ēva eṉṟu pirittu, naraṉip pōṉṟatu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
moḻikku aṭuttapaṭiyāka, māntaṉiṉ ciṟappāṟṟal muṉṉutal.  muṉṉu tal karututal.  muṉṉuvāṉ (vāṉīṟṟu ni. kā. vi. e.) - muṉṉāṉ (ma.) AS munan (to think).
muṉ - muṉṉam =1. karuttu.  muṉṉa mukatti ṉuṇarntu (puṟam. 3). 2. maṉam. (tivā.).
muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam = uḷḷam.
maṉam - va. maṉa. ṭu. mens. ME mynd, E mind.
muṉ - maṉ = karutum āṟṟaluḷḷa māntaṉ.
maṉpatu = makkaṭ kūṭṭam, maṉpatu maṟukkat tuṉpaṅ kaḷivōṉ (paripā. 15:52).
maṉpatu - maṉpati = makkaṭkūṭṭam.
maṉpati kākkuniṉ purimi  (puṟam. 210).
maṉ - va. manu. OS, OHG man, E man.
moḻiyiluṅ karuttilum aṉṟum iṉṟum eṉṟum ciṟantavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
ovvoru nāṭṭilum, moḻiyi vaḷarppavar potu makkaḷ; ilakkiyatti vaḷarppavar pulamakkaḷ. moḻikaḷ iyaṉ moḻiyum tirimoḻiyum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. tāṉē tōṉṟiyatu iyaṉmoḻi; oṉṟiṉiṉṟu tirintatu tirimoḻi.  āku peyarkaḷ irumaṭiyum mummaṭiyun tiriyum.  tiraviṭam tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta tirimoḻi; āriyam tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu tirinta irumaṭit tirimoḻi.
kumarināṭu tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam maṭṭumaṉṟi māntaṉ piṟantakamu mātalāl, tamiḻē immiyum ēṉimoḻi kalavāta muḻut tūya iyaṉmoḻiyākum.
irutiṇi aimpāl īreṇ mūviṭaṅkaḷākiya kiḷaviyilak kaṇamum, nuṇpirivukaḷiyuṭiya eṇ vēṟṟumip peyarilak kaṇamum, nannāṉku vakippaṭṭa mukkāla viṉiyilakkaṇamum; vaḷami, cemmi, marapu, takuti, tūymi mutaliya coṟṟiṟaṅ kaḷum; curukkam, teḷivu, matippuṟavu, iṭakkaraṭakkal, maṅkalam mutaliya coṟṟoṭaramitikaḷum koṇṭu; patiṉeṇ tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkut tāyum patiṉāṟāriyap pirivukaṭku mūlamumā yamintatu tamiḻ.
irutiṇi
ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum, uyirum meyyum uyirmeyyum (uyiriyum) eṉa mūvakiyāka vakuttālum, taṉakkum piṟarkkum nallatun tīyatumākiya iraṇṭiyum pakuttaṟiyum paṇpiyē ciṟappākak koṇṭu, aḵtuḷḷati uyarnta vakuppeṉṟum aḵtillatit tāḻnta vakuppeṉṟum tuṇintu, ataṟkēṟpa, uyarnta vakuppik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭkē āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālīṟum, tāḻnta vakuppik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭ kellām orumi paṉmi yeṉṉum īreṇṇīṟumē koṭuttu, moḻiyi vaḷarttavar tamiḻap potumakkaḷē. makkaḷum tēvarum uyarnta vakuppu; maṟṟavi tāḻnta vakuppu.
orumi :	murukaṉ vantāṉ, vaḷḷi ceṉṟāḷ.  uyartiṇi.
			kāḷi uḻukiṟatu, āvu kaṟakkiṉṟatu, kal kuttukiṟatu, 
				atu paṟakkiṉṟatu. - aḵṟiṇi.
			paṉmiyīṟum iruvakuppiṟkum vevvēṟām.
paṉmi : 	aracar vāḻntaṉar (vāḻntār),
			āciriyaṉmār pēciṉār. - uyartiṇi
			kāḷikaḷ uḻukiṉṟaṉa, māṭukaḷ mēykiṉṟaṉa, 
				avi iṉikkiṉṟaṉa, ilikaḷ acikiṉṟaṉa.  - 
				aḵṟiṇi.
makkaḷ, kurukkaḷ, avarkaḷ vantārkaḷ, eṉpaṉa vaḻuvati tiyākak koḷḷappeṟum.
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampal eṉṉum muṟiyil, potumakkaḷ vakutta vakuppukaṭkē ilakkaṇa nūlār uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi yeṉap peyar koṭuttaṉar.  iṉṟum, kallā māntar ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷi aṟiyā viṭiṉum, irutiṇi muṟippaṭiyē pēcuvar. uyiriyum pāliyumēyaṉṟip pakut taṟivi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu vēṟemmoḻiyārum poruḷkaḷip pakuttilar. teluṅkil makat, amakat eṉṟatu tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟip piṟkālattilēyē.
vaḷami
nālvakiyaṭi: vāḻiyili, neltāḷ, karumputtōki, paṉiyōli.
palvakip piñcu : mā vaṭu, palā mūcu, vāḻik kaccal, teṉṉaṅ kurumpi, pākku nuḻāy.
kāyppu māṟiyapiṉ tōṉṟum piñcu nuru (noru).
yāṉippeyarkaḷ: āmpal, umpal, uvā, eṟumpi, ōṅkal, kari, kaṟiyaṭi, kimmali, kimmā, tūṅkal, tumpi, tōl, nālvāy, pakaṭu, puḻikki - pūṭki, perumā, poṅkaṭi, matamā, maṟamali, mātiram, moy, vaḻuvi, vāraṇam, vēḻam mutaliyaṉa.
mukattiṟ cempuḷḷi yuḷḷatu cinturam allatu pukarmukam. murukaṉ ūrti piṇimukam.  āṇyāṉi kaḷiṟu; peṇyāṉi piṭi. ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta kutiriyiṉattiyum, pattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vakiyākap pirittaṉar.
tamiḻ moḻi, taṉakkuriya vīṭu eṉṉuñ colli maṭṭumaṉṟi, il eṉṉun teluṅkuc colliyum, maṉiyeṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac colliyum, camaṟkirutattiṟkum piṉṉiya (Finnish) moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa kuṭi eṉṉuñ colliyum, taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷateṉṟu, kālṭuvelār tamiḻc col vaḷattip pārāṭṭik kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
cemmi
eḻuttukkaḷiyum coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum, oliyum poruḷum tirikkātum citikkātum ilakkaṇa neṟiyiṟ pēcuvatu cemmi.  cemmi tavaṟiyatu koṭumi.  cemmiyāṉa tamiḻ centamiḻ. koṭumiyāṉa tamiḻ koṭun tamiḻ. tamiḻi eṉṟuñ centamiḻākavē vaḻaṅkavēṇṭumeṉpatu, toṉṉūlāciriyar iṭṭa niliyāṉa varampu.  avvarampi ṉālēyē, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka āriyar ettuṇiyō kēṭu ceytiruppiṉum, tamiḻ iṉṉum aḻiyātu iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
ikkālattup pērāciriyar cilar ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum kocci vaḻakkiṟkum vēṟupāṭaṟiyātu, vaccirukkōm (vittiruk kiṟōm) eṉpati ulakavaḻakkākak koḷvar.
nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum
kalanta mayakkam ulaka mātaliṉ
irutiṇi aimpāl iyalneṟi vaḻā amit
tirivil colloṭu taḻāal vēṇṭum.  (tol.1589).
vaḻakkeṉap paṭuvatu uyarntōr mēṟṟē 
nikaḻcci avarkaṭ ṭākalāṉa.  ( 1592).
eṉpavaṟṟik kaṇṭum avar uṇarvatilli.  potumakkaḷuḷ oru cārārāṉa kīḻmakkaḷ pēcciyun taḻuvavēṇṭumeṉiṉ, avar oḻukkattiyum piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭiyatākum. itu uyarntōk kuṭampāṭaṉṟu. ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟkum ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum ciṟitu vēṟupāṭiruppiṉum, iraṇṭum centamiḻ vaḻakkēyeṉṟum, cemmi tamiḻiṉ uyirnāṭip paṇpeṉṟum, tamiḻukku vaḻu niliyāṉavi tiraviṭa moḻikaṭku vaḻāniliyāku meṉṟum, ataṉālēyē avi taṉimoḻikaḷāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
marapu
epporuḷ eccoliṉ evvā ṟuyarntōr
ceppiṉar appaṭic cepputal marapē.  (naṉ. 388).
iḷami, āṇpāl, peṇpāl, vilaṅkuk kāvalar, uṟuppukkaḷ, viṉikaḷ, kaḻiporuḷ mutaliya palvakip poruḷumpaṟṟi, paṇṭi mēṉmakkaḷ eccoṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉarō accoṟ kaḷiyē vaḻaṅkutal marapām.
ūṇviṉikaḷ
uṟital,
uṟiñcutal	-	nīrpporuḷiyum nekiḻccip poruḷiyum 
					kāṟṟāl vāykkuḷ iḻuttal.
kuṭittal 	- 	nīrpporuḷi iyalpāka uṭkoḷḷutal.
parukutal 	- 	nīrkkalattiṟ paṟpaṭak kuṭittal.
aruntutal 	-	ciṟitu ciṟitākak kuṭittal.
maṇṭutal 	-	maṇṭiyuṭpaṭak kuṭittal
māntutal 	- 	pēraḷavākak kuṭittal
capputal 	-	oṉṟi mellātu nāviṟkum aṇṇattiṟkum iṭiyiliṭṭu nerukki ,ataṉcāṟṟi mellamella uṟiñcutal; allatu apporuḷic ciṟitu ciṟitākak  karittal.
cuvittal 	- 	oṉṟi meṉṟu ataṉ cuviyi nukartal.
cavittal 	- 	melliya poruḷi mellutal, kuḻanti tāyp 
		pālic capputal
cūmputal,
cūpputal  	-	tittippuk kuccum mūḷiyelumpum viralum pōṉṟa 
		vaṟṟi vāyiliṭṭuc capputal
tiṉṉutal 	- 	paḻamum palakāramum pōṉṟa ciṟṟuṇṭiyi meṉṟu 
		uṭkoḷḷutal.
uṇṇutal 	- 	kavaḷaṅ kavaḷamākac cōṟṟi uṭkoḷḷal.
cāppiṭutal 	- 	kuḻampum cāṟum mōrum (allatu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu) 
		kalanta cōṟṟik kavaḷaṅ kavaḷamākak kaṟivakikaḷuṭaṉ (allatu aviyiṉṟi) uṭkoḷḷutal.
maṭuttal,
vāymaṭuttal -	kampaṅkañciyum kēḻvarakuk kūḻum pōṉṟavaṟṟik 
		kaṭṭikaṭṭiyākak kūṭṭil toṭṭuṇṇutal, kavaḷaṅkavaḷamākap piṟar ūṭṭutal.

		uṭkoḷḷutal.

moyttal tiraḷutal. moy - moyi - moci.
mocital moyttal.
kaṭuntē ṟuṟukiḷi mocintaṉa tuñcum  (patiṟṟup.71:6).
ārtal - vayiṟu nirampa vuṇṇutal.
viḻuṅkutal - mellātum cuvi pārātum orē taṭaviyil virintu vāyvaḻi vayiṟṟukkuḷ iṭutal.
uṭkoḷḷutal - evvakiyilēṉum vayiṟu cērppittal.  itu ellā ūṇviṉikaṭkum potuvām.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattiṟ pulavar palar marapum (Idiom) takutiyum (Pro-priety) pōṟṟāmiyāl, palacoṟkaḷ tam ciṟappup poruḷi iḻantuviṭṭaṉa.
iṉṟu, kuḷampi (kāppi) cāppiṭutal, curuṭṭuk kuṭittal, kocuvali, tēṭkaṭi, eṉpaṉa marapuvaḻuvām.  ivi, kuḷampi kuṭittal, curuṭṭup piṭittal allatu pukittal, ulaṅku vali allatu nuḷampuvali, tēṭkoṭṭu eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. campaḷatti (salary) ūtiyam (profit, gin) eṉpatum vaḻuvām.  ilakkaṇappulaki nirampātavar nūlāciriyarum pottaka ākiriyarum āvataṉālum marapu keṭukiṉṟatu.
tūymi
cemmi pōṉṟē tūymiyum tamiḻiṉ uyirnāṭip paṇpām.
veḷināṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta poruḷkaṭ kellām, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu nōkki uṭaṉuṭaṉ tamiḻppeyarkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: aṭikkāy (pākku), aṇṭimā (muntiri, Cashew) uruḷik kiḻaṅku, oṭṭakam, karumpu, kaḻuti, kuccuk kiḻaṅku (āḻvaḷḷik kiḻaṅka, ēḻilikkiḻaṅka, kompukkiḻaṅku, cavarikkaṭṭi, maravaḷḷikkiḻaṅku), kutiri, centāḻi, (Pine-apple), pukiyili, pukivaṇṭi, pērīntu, mitivaṇṭi, miḷakāy, muntiri (koṭi muntiri, grape), vāṉkōḻi.
kāḷ kāḷ eṉṟu kattuvatu kaḻuti.
tamiḻiṉ iyalpi aṟiyātār cilar,
kuraṅkiṉ ēṟṟiṉik kaṭuvaṉ eṉṟalum
marampayil kūkiyik kōṭṭāṉ eṉṟalum
cevvāyk kiḷiyit tatti eṉṟalum
vevy verukiṉip pūci eṉṟalum
kutiriyuḷ āṇiṉic cēval eṉṟalum
iruḷniṟap paṉṟiyi ēṉam eṉṟalum
erumiyuḷ āṇiṉik kaṇṭi eṉṟalum
muṭiya vanta avvaḻak kuṇmiyiṉ
kaṭiya lākā kaṭaṉaṟinta tōrkkē.  (tol. 1568).
eṉpatip piṟaḻavuṇarntu, tamiḻil varituṟiyiṉṟip piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkat tolkāppiyam iṭantatantu viṭṭatākak kūṟuvar.  innūṟḷapāviluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷē yeṉpatiyum, avaṟṟuṭ cila ciṟitē poruḷ tirintavi yeṉpatiyum, avar aṟintilar.
kaṭuvaṉ eṉpatu kaṇavaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripu. kaṇavaṉ eṉṉuñcol aḵṟiṇiyilum vaḻaṅkum.
vaṅkāk kaṭanta ceṅkāṟ pēṭi
eḻāluṟa vīḻnteṉak kaṇavaṟkā ṇātu  (kuṟun. 151).
eṉpati nōkkuka.  kōṭṭāṉ marakkompil allatu pontil vāḻvatu.  tatti ilikaḷilum ōlikaḷilum totti niṟpatu.  tottu - totti - tatti. totti - tōtta (inti).  pūci eṉpatu vīṭṭup pūṉiyiṉ peyar.  cēval eṉpatu paṟavi yāṇiṉ peyar. cē eṉpatu vilaṅkāṇiṉ peyar. e-ṭu: cēṅkaṉṟu = āṇkaṉṟu. cē - cēvu - cēval. ēṉam eṉpatu kariyatu eṉṉum poruḷuḷḷatu.  ēṉal =karuntiṉi. ēṉi - yāṉi. kaṇṭi kaṭiyatu (kaṭumiyāṉatu).
āyiṉum, tolkāppiyarkku irupatu nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiya naṉṉūlār, tamiḻiṉ tūymiyik kāvātu, ellā vaṭacoṟ kaḷiyum tamiḻil vaḻaṅka iṭantantatu pōṉṟē,
paḻiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum
vaḻuvala kāla vakiyi ṉāṉē. (462).
eṉṟu vaḻuppaṭak kūṟiviṭṭār.  itu taḻuvat takkataṉṟu.
curukkam
curuṅkac collal oru paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟākap paṇṭit tamiḻarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
payaṉilcol pārāṭṭu vāṉi makaṉeṉal
makkaṭ pataṭi yeṉal.  (kuṟal. 196). 
palacollak kāmuṟuvar maṉṟa mācaṟṟa 
cilacollal tēṟṟā tavar  ( 649)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
oruvaṉ kūlakkaṭikkucceṉṟu, kaṭikkāraṉiṭam pācippayaṟu (paccippayaṟu) uḷḷatā eṉṟu viṉaviṉ, kaṭikkāraṉ aḵtillāviṭattu, uḻuntallatilliyeṉṟu ta;ṉaṉiṭamuḷḷa vēṟoru payaṟṟi allatu carakkippaṟṟic colla vēṇṭumeṉṟum, viṉaviya poruḷ maṭṭumē yirukkumāyiṉ, ati ippayaṟalla tilliyeṉṟu cuṭṭikkūṟa vēṇṭu meṉṟum, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉ mēṟkoṇṭu pala viṉākkaḷum viṭikaḷum vīṇāka eḻāvāṟu taṭukkumeṉṟum, paṇṭi yilakkaṇa nūlār vaṇikarkkuc coṉṉatu, maṟṟellāt toḻilāḷarkkum poruntum potu vāypāṭākum.
epporu ḷāyiṉum alla tilleṉiṉ
apporu ḷallāp piṟituporuḷ kūṟal.
apporuḷ kūṟiṉ cuṭṭik kūṟal.
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam  (col. 35.36).
teḷivu
curuṅkac collal ciṟanta paṇpāyiṉum, viḷakkamiṉṟic colvatu payaṉpaṭātu kuṟṟamākumātaliṉ, māppūttatu eṉṟu palaporuḷorucolliṉ ciṟappu viṉi kuṟiyātu, mā vīḻntatu eṉṟu potuviṉi kuṟippatu kūṭāteṉṟum, ati māmaram vīḻntatu, vilaṅkumā vīḻntatu eṉṟu teḷivu paṭuttik kūṟavēṇṭumeṉṟum; oru poruḷiṉ iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa iyaliyum ceyaliyuṅ kūṟumpōtu, ataṟkuk karaṇiyattiyum kuṟippākavō veḷippaṭiyākavō kuṟittal vēṇṭumeṉṟum; toṉṉūlāciriyar neṟiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
oṉṟuviṉi maruṅkiṉ oṉṟit tōṉṟum
viṉivēṟu paṭāap palaporu ḷorucol
niṉiyuṅ kālik kiḷantāṅ kiyalum.
kuṟittōṉ kūṟṟan terittumoḻi kiḷavi.
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.  (col.54, 55).
ilakkiyam
ici, nāṭakam, kaṇitam, kaṇiyam, ēraṇam, mantiram, maṟi, paṭṭāṅku, maṭi, maruttuvam, aṟam, araciyal, mal, pōr, nilam, nīr mutaliya palvēṟu nūlkaḷ paṟṟiya ciṟappilakkiyamum; aṟuvakip pāvāṉa taṉinilic ceyyuḷum, eṇvaki vaṉappāṉa toṭarnilic ceyyuḷum, ākiya potuvilakki yamum; mūlamum uriyum ceyyuḷākavē yiruntaṉa.  iṅṅaṉam vēṟemmoḻiyilum iruntatilli, irukkappōvatu milli. 
ilakkiyam muḻutum ceyyuḷākavē iruntataṉāl, ilakkiya vaḻakku ceyyuḷvaḻakkeṉappaṭṭatu. ellāppulavarum pāvalarāyiruntataṉāl, pāvalar eṉappaṭātu pulavar eṉṟē peyar peṟṟaṉar. avar ikkālattup pāvalarpōl, ēṭum eḻutukōlum eṭuttu ōriṭattamarntu eṇṇiyeṇṇi aṭittuntiruttiyum ceyyuḷiyaṟṟātu, urinaṭiyiṟ pēcuvatu pōl, eṅkum eṉṟum epporuḷum paṟṟik kaṭuttup pāṭiyavarāvar. āciriyarum aṟivuṟuttuvōrum kaṇiyarum ākiya kalvit toḻilāḷar maṭṭumaṉṟi; uḻavar, vaṇikar, maruttuvar, kollar mutaliya palvakip piṟatoḻilāḷarum, kuṟiñci nilattuk kuṟavarum, pālinilattuk kaḷḷar maṟavarum, mullinilattu āyarum, neytal nilattup paravarum ākiya varuḷḷum cilar pāvalarāyiruntaṉar.  ataṉālēyē, talik kaḻakattup pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar uḷḷiṭṭu, nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉar.
ilakkaṇam
tamiḻil ilakkaṇa mutaṉūl iyaṟṟiyavar. muṟṟattuṟantu muḻumuṉi varāṉa oru meypporuḷaṟiñar.
viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉūl ākum.  (tol.1594)
muṉital veṟuttal.  muṉi - muṉivu - muṉivaṉ. muṉitar veṟuttal. muṉi - muṉivu - muṉivaṉ = ulaki veṟuttup paṟṟit tuṟantavaṉ.
eḻuttu
mutaṉūlāciriyar oru ciṟanta meyp poruḷaṟiñarāyirunta taṉālēyē, uyirum meyyum uyirmeyyum (uyiriyum) pōṉṟu eḻuttolikaḷ mūvakippaṭṭiruttalikkaṇṭu, mupporuṭ peyarkaḷiyē eḻuttolikaṭkum uvamiyāku peyarāka iṭṭirukkiṉṟār.  tāṉāka iyaṅkum uyirip pōṉṟu tāṉāka volikkum uyireḻuttum; uyiriṉ cērkki yiṉṟittāṉāka volikkāta meyyeḻuttum; uyiroṭu cērnta vuṭampu ataṉāl iyakkappaṭṭu ataṉoṭu oṉṟi ataṉiṉum muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟum uyirmey pōṉṟu, uyireḻuttoṭu cērnta meyyeḻuttu olikkappaṭṭu ataṉoṭu oṉṟi ataṉiṉum muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟum uyirmey yeḻuttum; iruttalik kāṇka.  uyirmey uyiriyuṭiya mey.  pirāṇi eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻakkiṉāl uyirmey eṉṉuñ colliṉ poruḷ maṟintatu.
oliyiṉ nilimiyiyē uruvattiluṅ kāṭṭuvataṟku, uyir meykku meyyuruvamum uyirkkuṟiyuñ cērnta taṉivaṭivamit tār.  ivvamippu mutaṉmutal tamiḻilēyē ēṟpaṭṭatu.  uyireḻuttukkaḷum meyyeḻuttukkaḷum cērnta tokutikkuk kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, avaṟṟōṭu uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷum cērnta tokutikku neṭuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum peyar.  ivi uṟavukkuṟiyīṭukaḷātalāl (Relative Terms), oruṅkē tōṉṟiṉa viyākum.
mēlināṭukaḷ uṭpaṭap piṟanāṭṭu vaṇṇamālikaḷel lām (Alphabets)FW§fz¡nf.  cappāṉilum ettiyōppiyā vilum uḷḷa aciyeḻuttukkaḷum (Syllabaries), meyyiṉmi yāl neṭuṅkaṇakkākā.
mēṉāṭṭuk kuṟuṅkaṇakkellām oḻuṅkiṉṟi uyirum meyyuṅ kalantiruppatāl, tamiḻk kuṟuṅkaṇakkuppōl piṟappi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa muṟiyik (Order) kāṭṭuvatilli. colliṉ mūviṭattilum enta eḻuttum varalāmātalāl, mutaṉili iṭinili iṟutinili eṉṉum iṭavarampum mēli moḻiyeḻuttukkaṭkilli. coṟkaḷ nīṇṭakālamāka mēṉmēlun tirintu tirintu urumāṟi, turup piṭitta iruppukkaruvikaḷpōl uṟuppup pirikkamuṭiyātirup patāl, pakucollamippum coṟpuṇarcciyum ākiya eḻuttiṉ puṟattilakkaṇaṅkaḷum perumpālum mēlimoḻikaṭ killi.  ākavē, pattu vakiyakamum iruvakip puṟamum ākiya paṉṉīreḻut tilakkaṇaṅkaḷum nirampiya moḻi tamiḻ oṉṟē.
eḻuttilak kaṇamē yīrā ṟavitām
eṇpeyar muṟipiṟap puruvam aḷavē
mutalī ṟiṭinili pōli yakamām
kiḷaviyum puṇarcciyuṅ kiḷappiṉ puṟamām.
māttiri eṉpatum teṉcollē. kumarināṭṭār potuvāka eḵkuc ceviyarāyiruntamiyiṉ, kaṇṇimi yaḷavāṉa oru māttiriyiṉ ariyaḷavōṭu kālaḷaviyum eḻuttoli kaḷiṟ kaṇṭaṟintaṉar.
tamiḻil eḻuttut tōṉṟiya kālam ki.mu. 10,000. vēta āriyar nāvalan tēyattiṟkuṭ pukunta kālam ki.mu. 1500. avarukku eḻuttilli. avar vētam nīṇṭa kālamāka eḻutāk kiḷaviyākavē yiruntatu.  teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ, tamiḻeḻuttip piṉpaṟṟik kiranta eḻutti amittuk koṇṭaṉar. ataṉpiṉ, kiranta eḻutti aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭu, vaṭanāṭṭil, ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkum 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil, tēvanākari tōṉṟiṟṟu. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiyum eḻuttilak kaṇattiyum taḻuvi, eḻuttumuṟiyum uyirmeyt taṉivaṭivum coṟ puṇarcciyum, mutaṟkaṇ camaṟkirutattilum piṉṉar ēṉi yintiya moḻikaḷilum, ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
ki.mu.3-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttu tamiḻakattup pukuntatu.  ataṉiṉṟē vaṭṭeḻuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. āriyar tam tamiḻoḻipput tiṭṭatti aṭip paṭiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkiyatāl, mūvēntarum āriya aṭimi yarāyp pōṉa piṟkālattil, tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kalveṭṭil vaṭṭeḻuttup pukuntirukkiṉṟatu. tamiḻukkum vaṭṭeḻut tiṟkum yātoru toṭarpumilli.  iṉṟuḷḷa tamiḻeḻuttut toṉṟu toṭṭu varuvatē.
tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi.  (98)
eṉṟu 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭup pavaṇanti muṉivar kūṟatal kāṇka.  ekara okaramey puḷḷi nīṅkiyatum, ēkāra ōkāramey iraṭṭiccuḻik kompu peṟṟatum, ēkāravuyir kīḻiḻuppuk koṇṭatumē, piṟkālattu vēṟupāṭām.
col
coṟkaḷi mutaṉili, īṟu, puṇarcci, cāriyi, iṭinili, tiripu eṉṉum āṟuṟuppākap pakuttum; iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol eṉa mūvakiyāka vakuttum; moḻinūṟku aṭikōliyatu tamiḻē.
poruḷ
tamiḻilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷ naṭiyiliruntamiyāl, collilakkaṇatti yaṭuttuc coṟṟoṭarilakkaṇattik kūṟāviṭiṉum, ceyyuḷilakkaṇattik kūṟi ataṉpiṉ poruḷi lakkaṇattik kūṟiyirukkalām. āyiṉ, mutaṉūlāciriyar meypporuḷaṟiñarā yiruntataṉāl, moḻinaṭi etuvāyiṉum poruḷē ataṉ uḷḷīṭeṉṟum, taṉiccoṟkum poruḷuṇ ṭeṉṟum, kaṇṭu, ataṉiyē colliṟkaṭuttuk kūṟiyatōṭu, ataṟkē ciṟappuk koṭuttuc ceyyuḷi ataṉuḷḷaṭakki, mūṉṟām atikārattip poruḷatikārameṉap peyariṭṭu iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūliṉ muṭimaṇiyākkiṉār. tiruk kōviliṉ mukamaṇṭapamum iṭimaṇṭapamum uṇṇā ḻikiyum pōla, eḻuttum collum poruḷum muṟiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ciṟantaṉa vākum.
piṟkālattup pāṇṭiya ṉoruvaṉ, eḻuttatikāramum col latikāramum vallārimaṭṭum talippaṭṭup poruḷatikāram vallārit talippaṭātapōtu, puṭipaṭak kavaṉṟu, eṉṉi? eḻuttuñ collum ārāyvatu poruḷatikārattiṉ poruṭ ṭaṉṟē! poruḷatikāram peṟēmeṉiṉ ivi peṟṟum peṟṟilēm. eṉṟu varuntiyatāka,iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri kūṟiyiruppatu iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.  itaṉāl, tamiḻaṉ perumiyiyum tamiḻiṉ perumiyiyum oruṅkē uṇaralām.  ēṉimoḻiyilakkaṇa nūlārellām eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi eṉṉum nāloṭu niṉṟuviṭa, tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlār maṭṭum yāppiṉ poruḷukkum ilakkaṇam vakuttatu, kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻariṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa akakkaraṇa vaḷarcciyik kāṭṭum. poruḷilak kaṇam pōlap paṇṭit tamiḻaṉiṉ pulami nuṇukkattik kāṭṭum cāṉṟu vēṟetuvumilli.
ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum akampuṟam eṉa iraṇṭākap pakuttu, avvoṉṟiyum evvēḻu tiṇiyāka vakuttiruk kiṉṟār mutaṉūlār.
kūlikkāraṉmutal kōmakaṉ vari enniliyarāyiṉum et toḻilarāyiṉum, makkaḷiyellām āṇṭu naṭattum kuṇam iraṇṭu.  avi kātalum maṟamum. makkaḷellārum āṭavarum peṇṭirumākap paṭikkappaṭṭiruppatāl, kātal vāḻkki māperumpālarkku iṉṟiyamiyātatākum.  poruḷillārkku ivvulaka milliyātalāl, oru toḻili mēṟkoḷvatu ellārkkum iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ettoḻililum pōṭṭi toṉṟu toṭṭuc ciṟṟaḷavākavō pēraḷavākavō iruntē vantirukkiṉṟatu.  etirikaḷi veṉṟu vāḻkki yit tiṟampaṭa naṭatta maṟamum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
iṉi, orē peṇṇip palar maṇakka virumpupōtu, kātalilēyē maṟamuṅ kalakkiṉṟatu.
ivviru kuṇaṅkaḷuḷḷum allatu kuṇavāḻkkiyuḷḷum, uḷḷattiṟku mika neruṅkiyatu kātalē.  ātalāl ati akam eṉṟār.  akamallātatu puṟamātaliṉ maṟattip puṟam eṉṟār.  iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭiyum vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟiṉum, avi akappakiyum puṟappakiyum pōlap pirintu nillātu, akaṅkiyum puṟaṅkiyumpōla oṉṟiyē niṟkum.
tamiḻaṉukkut tamiḻ akam; tiraviṭam akappuṟam; āriyam puṟam; cēmiyam puṟappuṟam.  ivvakik kūṟṟiṉiṉṟu, akam puṟam eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi oruvāṟuṇaralām.
aracaṉ ellārkkun talimiyākavum potuvākavum ellāriyun taṉṉuḷaṭakkiyum niṟpatāl, aracaṉukkuc coṉṉatu aṉivarkkuñ coṉṉatāku meṉṟum, kātal vāḻkkiyilum maṟavāḻkkiyilum taliciṟanta nukarcciyum paṭṭaṟivum aracaṉukkē yuṇṭeṉṟuṅ kaṇṭu, ōr iḷavaraca ṉiyē kātalaṉākavum ōr iḷavaraciyiyē kātaliyākavuṅ koṇṭu, akapporuḷilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉat tamiḻukkē ciṟanta marapākum.  āyiṉum, itu nāṭaka vaḻakkamum ulakiyal vaḻakkamum kalantatātalāl, ciṟupāṉmi potumakkaḷ kātal vāḻkkiyum āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappeṟum.
akapporuḷ eṉṉum kātal allatu maṇavāḻkki, kikkiḷi (orutalikkāmam), aintiṇi (irutalikkāmam), peruntiṇi (poruntākkāmam) eṉa mūvakiyāka vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  avaṟṟuḷ, ellāvakiyilum nallateṉa oppukkoḷḷap paṭṭa irutalikkāmam puṇartal, pirital. iruttal, iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉṉum aintu uripporuḷākap pirikkappaṭṭum; avi muṟiyē kuṟiñci pāli mulli neytal marutam ākiya nilaṅkaṭku urimiyākkappaṭṭum; uḷḷaṉa. ippirippum nilavurimip paṭuttamum, mutaṉūlā ciriyariṉ neṭuṅkālak kūrṅkavaṉippiyum nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattiyum teṟṟeṉak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
talimik kuṭimakaṉ
makkaḷ vāḻkkikku ettuṇiyō pala poruḷkaḷ vēṇṭiyiruppiṉum, avi yellāvaṟṟuḷḷum iṉṟiyami yātatu uṇavu oṉṟē.  ataṉāl, uṇavi viḷivikkum uḻavaṉ ellāruḷḷum uyarntavaṉākiṉṟāṉ. itupaṟṟiyē, tiruvaḷḷuvar tam oppuyarvaṟṟa ulakap potu nūlil, kuṭi ceyalvakiyiṉ piṉ uḻavatikāratti vittār.
uṇavaḷippavaṉ
uḻuvār ulakattārk kāṇiyaḵ, tāṟṟā(tu)
eḻuvāri yellām poṟuttu.  (kuṟaḷ.1032)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷāl, uḻavar ulakamākiya tērukku akcāṇiyāvar eṉṟurittār teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār. accāṇi yillāta tēr muccāṇum ōṭātu. ceyaṟki viḷiviṉṟi, makkaḷiṉam perutta ulakam oru nāḷum naṭipeṟātu.  uṇavu viḷittali ataṉ arumi paṟṟi aḷittal eṉṟatu uyarpu naviṟci.
viruntōmpi vēḷāṇmi ceypavaṉ
viruntu, putumi, putitāy vanta ayalārkku aṉpuṭaṉ uṇavaḷippatē viruntōmpal. vēḷāṇmiyāvatu piṟari virumpic ceyyum palvaki yutavi.  ivi yiraṇṭum tāmākavē uṇavi viḷippavarum talimuṟi talimuṟiyāyp piṟarip pēṇip paṇpaṭṭu varupavarumāṉa uḻavar kuṭiyiṉarkkē iyalum.
purappōr koṟṟattāṉ

taḻuvutoṭarum taḻāt toṭarum
vēṟṟumi yimmuta lāṟā malvaḻi
toḻilpaṇ puvami yummi yaṉmoḻi
eḻuvāy viḷiyī reccamuṟ ṟiṭiyuri
taḻuvu toṭaraṭuk keṉavī rēḻē   (naṉ.152)
itaṉ uriyil, vi.mu. caṭakōparāmānujācāriyārum, nr.kiUZzkhrhÇahU«,
ivi taḻuvutoṭar eṉavē, av viruvaḻiyilum, taḻāt toṭa rum cila uḷa eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. nīrkkuṭam eṉpatu nīriyuṭiya kuṭam eṉa viritalāl, nīr eṉpatu uṭiya eṉpatit taḻuvik kuṭam eṉpatip poruḷāl taḻuvāmal toṭarntataṉāl, itu taḻāttoṭarākiya vēṟṟumit toki.............marattic cāttaṉ veṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatil, maratti eṉpatu veṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatit taḻuvic cāttaṉ eṉpatip poruḷāl taḻuvāmal toṭarntataṉāl, itu taḻāttoṭarākiya vēṟṟumi viri.  curiyāḻa ammi mitappa eṉpatu curimitappa ammi āḻa eṉak kūṭṭappaṭutalāl, curi eṉpatu āḻa eṉpatiyum ammi eṉpatu mitappa eṉpatiyum poruḷāl taḻuvāmal toṭarn taṉa.  ivi taḻāttoṭa rākiya alvaḻippuṇarcci.  taḻāt toṭarāvatu nilimoḻi yāṉatu varumoḻiyōṭu poruṭ poruttamuṟat taḻuvāta toṭar. poruṭ porutta muṟat taḻuviya toṭar taḻuvu toṭar eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
taḻuvutoṭar, taḻāttoṭar eṉpaṉa ilakkaṇap poruttamuṟat taḻuvukiṉṟa toṭar. ïy¡fz¥ bghU¤jKw¤ jGthj bjhl® v‹nw bghUŸgL k‹¿., poruṭ poruttamuṟat taḻuvukiṉṟa toṭar, poruṭ poruttamuṟat taḻuvāta toṭar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭā.
cūttirattuṭ kūṟappaṭṭa patiṉāṉku toṭarkaḷuḷ, mutaṟ patiṉmūṉṟum taḻuvutoṭarum, kaṭiyoṉṟum taḻāttoṭaru mākum eṉpatik kuṟittaṟkē, aṭukkut toṭarkku muṉ taḻuvutoṭar eṉpatik kūṟip pirittaṉar. ēṉip patiṉmūṉṟum taḻuvutoṭar eṉavē, iṟutiyoṉṟum ataṟketirākiya taḻāttoṭareṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. meymmayakkuc cūttirattil oṉṟi uṭaṉili eṉṟatiṉāl iṉṉoṉṟu vēṟṟunili yeṉṟaṟiyappaṭṭāṟpōla. itu cūttiramātaliṉ coṟcuruṅ kaṟku iṅṅaṉam yākkappaṭṭatu.
vēṟṟumittoki mutaliya āṟu tokikaḷum, eḻuvāyt toṭar mutaliya eṭṭut toṭarkaḷum, nilimoḻiyum varu moyiyum ilakkaṇattiṟ campantappaṭṭiruppatum, aṭukkut toṭar oṉṟumaṭṭum campantappaṭātu nilimoḻi varu moḻikaḷ taṉittaṉi niṟpatum eṭuttukkāṭṭu vāyilāyk kaṇṭukoḷka.
e-ṭu.	cōṟuṇṭāṉ - vēṟṟumit toki - taḻuvutoṭar
		irāmaṉ vantāṉ - eḻuvāyt toṭar - taḻuvutoṭar
		pāmpu pāmpu - taḻāttoṭar
nīrkkuṭam eṉpatu nīriyuṭiya kuṭam eṉṟu viriyumpōtu, nīri eṉpatu uṭiya eṉpatiyum, uṭiya eṉpatu kuṭam eṉpatiyum taḻuvukiṉṟami kāṇka. nīrkkuṭam eṉṟu tokiyāy niṟkum pōtum nīr eṉpatu (tokkuniṟkum) uṭiya eṉṉuñ colliyē avāy niliyāl taḻuvum.  allākkāl, vantēṉ eṉṉum toki vākkiyattil nāṉ eṉṉum eḻuvāy avāyniliyāl varuvikkappaṭāmi kāṇka. cāttaṉ maratti veṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatē iyalpāṉa tamiḻuri muṟiyātaliṉ, marattic cāttaṉ eṉpatu oru payaṉ nōkkiya muṟi māṟṟākum.  cāttaṉ maratti eṉpatil cāttaṉ eṉpatu eḻuvāyum, maratti eṉpatu eḻuvāyiṉ toḻili yaṭikiṉṟa ceyappaṭuporuḷāyum eṅṅaṉam iyiyumō, aṅṅaṉamē marattic cāttaṉ eṉpatilum iyiyum.
vanta cāttaṉ makaṉ eṉṉun toṭaril vanta eṉpatu makaṉit taḻuvumāyiṉ, aḵtu iṭippiṟavaral eṉap potuviyalil vēṟilakkaṇamākak kūṟappaṭutaliṉ īṇṭik kēlātākum.  ēṟpiṉ, potuviyaliṉ kūṟum iṭippiṟavaral kūṟiyatu kūṟalāyk kuṟṟan taṅkum.
curi yāḻa ammi mitappa eṉpatu ceyyuṭkuriya moḻi māṟṟup poruḷkōḷātaliṉ, atuvum īṇṭik kēlātu.
āṅkilattilum taḻuvutoṭar taḻāttoṭarkaṭku ilakkaṇap porutta muṇmi yiṉmikaḷiyē ilakkaṇamākak koḷvar.
Compound words (toṭar moḻikaḷ) are subdivided into two classes:
I.  Unrelated (taḻāttoṭar) or those in which the simple words are not connected together by any grammatical relation. (These have been also called juxta-positional).
II. Related, (jGî bjhl®) or those in which there is some grammatical relation between the component words.(These have been also called syntactical.)   (Nesfield: Book IV)
patiṉāṉku toṭarkaḷilum taḻuvutoṭarum taḻāttoṭarum uṇṭeṉṟu naṉṉūlār koṇṭiruppiṉ, ati oru taṉic cūttirattil viḷaṅka vittiruppārēyaṉṟi, ittuṇi uyt tuṇarviṟkum mayakkattiṟkum iṭan tantirār eṉka. (cen tamiḻc celvi maṭaṅkal 1936)
taḻiyuṭi
taḻikoṇṭu cēṟal, cantaṉattaḻi takāteṉṟu maṟuttal, taḻi etirtal, taḻi ēṟpittal, taḻi viruppurittal mutaliya akapporuḷ tuṟikaḷ makkaḷ kuṟiñci niliyil taḻiyuṭi aṇintiruntatik kāṭṭum.
taḻiyuṭiyiṉ piṉ maravuriyāṭi aṇiyappaṭṭatu.  maravurikkuc cīri eṉṟu peyar.  appeyarē cīli, cēli eṉat tirintu iṉṟu paruttiyāṭiyik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  (col. 19)
taḷam
taḷam - tala
taḷ - taḷam = aṭi, aṭipparappu.
itaṟku vaṭavar kāṭṭum tru eṉṉum mūlam poruntāmi kāṇka. tru = citaṟu.
citaṟu eṉṉum teṉcollē, Strew eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkum tru eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkum mūlamākat terikiṉṟatu. (va.va: 174).
taṟṟuṭuttal
āṭavar ariyil āṭi yaṇiyum muṟi.
(1)	cuṟṟik kaṭṭutal,
(2)	tāṟu pāyccik kaṭṭutal,
eṉa iruvaki.  ivviraṇṭum tamiḻaruṭiyaṉavē.  cilar tāṟu pāyccik kaṭṭutal āriya vaḻakkam eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar.  atu tavaṟu.
tāṟu pāyccik kaṭṭutalukku mun nilippāṭu vēṇṭum.  avi, 
(1) mellāṭi,
(2) palvēṟu mey yiyakkam,
(3) viṉivirivu,
eṉpaṉa. pañciṉālum paṭṭiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭa mel lāṭi, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻar aṇintu vantaṉar.  uḻavu, kūttu, pōr mutaliya toḻilkaḷ palvaki uṭam paciviyum virinta celaviyum kaṭuvuḻippiyum vēṇṭuvaṉa.  ātalāl, avaṟṟic cevviyāyc ceytaṟkut tāṟupāyccik kaṭṭutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. iruntaviṭatti liruntukoṇṭu necavāḷi taṉ vēṭṭiyic cuṟṟik kaṭṭik koṇṭirukkalām.  āyiṉ, virintiyaṅkum vēṭṭik kāraṉum, pōr ceyyum maṟavaṉum, naṭañ ceyyuṅ kūttaṉum, tāṟupāyccik kaṭṭāmal tam toḻilic ceyyamuṭiyātu.  kāl caṭṭiyum callaṭamum (Drawers, arikkāṟ caṭṭi) paṇṭik kālattililli.
tāṟupāyccik kaṭṭutal,
(1) 	moṭṭittāṟu,
(2) 	vaṭṭattāṟu,
(3) 	vāl tāṟu,
eṉa mūvaki.  moṭṭit tāṟāvatu, cuṟṟilum vēṭṭiyit tirittu, muḻaṅkāṟkuk kīḻ mikutiyāy allatu oru ciṟitum vēṭṭi toṅkātapaṭi muṉpuṟa muntiyi allatu aṭip pākatti irukāṟkumiṭiyē pāyccip piṉṉāl iṟukac cerukutal. vaṭṭattāṟāvatu: oru puṟa muntiyik kaṇukkāl vari irukālum maṟiyat toṅkaviṭṭu, maṟṟoru puṟamuntiyi irukāṟkumiṭiyē pāyccip piṉṉāl iṟukac cerukutal. vāltāṟāvatu: vaṭṭattāṟṟoṭu piṉṉāl vāl viṭṭuk kaṭṭutalām.  itu oṟṟi vāltāṟu: iraṭṭi vāltāṟu eṉa iruvakippaṭum.  muṉpakkattiliruntu oru kīḻ muntiyi maṭṭum piṉṉāṟ koṇṭupōy iṭakkāṟ pakkamēṉum valakkāṟ pakkamēṉum naṭuvilēṉum vālviṭṭuc cerukuvatu oṟṟi vāltāṟu eṉpatām.  iru kīḻ muntiyiyum muṉpōṟ koṇṭu pōy valakkāṟ pakkam oṉṟum iṭakkāṟ pakkamoṉṟumāka iruvāl viṭṭuc cerukuvatu iraṭṭi vāltāṟu eṉpatām.  moṭṭit tāṟṟukku nāṉku muḻamum, vaṭṭat tāṟṟukku aintu muḻamum, vāl tāṟṟukku āṟu muḻamum, āṭi vēṇṭum. ivi, muṟiyē, oṉṟiṉoṉṟu matippāṉavi.  ātalāl, kallāp potumakkaḷum kallāp perumakkaḷum kaṟṟa perumakkaḷum ivaṟṟi, muṟiyē, kiyāḷvar.  itu toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkam.
kallāp potumakkaḷ perumpālum uḻippāḷikaḷātaliṉ,  matippu vēṇṭāmiyōṭu uḻippu vacatiyum moṭṭit tāṟṟiyē avarkkuriyatākkum.  ūkkamāy viṉiceyyum uḻippāḷikaḷ, ciṟappāka uḻavar, moṭṭittāṟ ṟuṭiyum mikiyeṉak karuti nīrccīli (kōvaṇam) yoṉṟē yaṇintu viṉiceyvar.
evvuḻippu viṉiyiyum ūkkamāyc ceyya muṉiyiṉ, ariyāṭiyi iṟukak kaṭṭikkoḷvatu māntar iyalpu.  ataṉāl,
kuṭiceyval eṉṉum oruvaṟkut teyvam
maṭitaṟṟut tāṉmun tuṟum.  (kuṟaḷ. 1023)
eṉṟu, teyvattiṉ mēlum taṟṟuṭuttali ēṟṟik kūṟiṉār tiruvaḷḷuvar.  oru viṉiyi muṉintu ceyvataṟku ariyil āṭiyi iṟukkikkaṭṭiṉāl maṭṭum pōtātu: kīḻp pāycciyuṅ kaṭṭa vēṇṭum.
taṟṟuṭuttaleṉiṉum tāṟu pāyccik kaṭṭuta leṉiṉum oṉṟē.  taṟutal - iṟukak kaṭṭutal allatu uṭuttutal. taṟṟuṭuppatu tāṟu. (mutaṉili nīṇṭa toḻilāku peyar). taṟu-tāṟu. x.neh.: iṟu - īṟu, uṟu - ūṟu.
taṟukkaṇittal:
(1)	uṇavupporuḷ iṟukutal,
(2)	cati allatu puṇ kāyttuppōtal,
(3)	paḻaṅ kaṉṟippōtal.
vaḻi naṭikkukkūṭa, cuṟṟik kaṭṭuvatiṉum tāṟupāyccik kaṭṭutalē vacatiyāṉatu: allākkāl, kāl taṭṭum, veṭṭum: tuṇi pitirum, kiḻiyum.

maliyāḷa (paḻañcēra) nāṭṭut tuṟimukaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa kōḻikkōṭṭiliruntu ēṟṟumatiyāṉa tuṇi āṅkilattil kālikkō eṉappaṭṭatu. kōḻikkōṭu iṉṟu kaḷḷik kōṭṭi eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. im marūu. (Calicut eṉṉum) āṅkilap peyar vaṭivit taḻuviyatu:
paṇṭi mēṉāṭṭārāṭiyic cuṟṟik kaṭṭiṉarēyaṉṟit taṟṟuṭuttiṉa tilli. ki.mu. mūvāyiram āṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ intiyāviṟ pukunta vēta āriyar, āṭumāṭu mēykkum mulli nākarikattiyē aṭintiruntataṉāl, avarum mellāṭi yittaṟṟuṭuttiyatu milli: tamiḻarpōṟ kaṭumiyāyp palatuṟiyil uḻittatu milli.

oruvar taṉṉalattiṉāl koḷki taḷarvatum aḵtiṉmiyāl uṟittu niṟpatum, oḻukkam kulam matam kaṭci mutaliya pala tuṟi kaḷilum kaṇkūṭākak kāṇkiṟōm.  entak koḷkiyiyum uṟutiyākak kaṭippiṭikkavēṇṭumāyiṉ, taṉṉala miruttal kūṭātu. koḷkiyeṉṟatu iṅkup potunalak koḷkiyi.
āciyiṉmiyi yuṇarttum virākam eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripāku peyarāṉa (tattitāntamāṉa) virākkiyam eṉṉum col viṭāppiṭiyi yuṇarttutal kāṇka. āciyiruppiṉ viṭāppiṭi yirāteṉpatum, aḵtillā viṭiṉ viṭāppiṭi yirukkumeṉpatum, ic collāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. (col. 107).

tāṇṭakam
26 eḻuttiṟku mēṟpaṭāta 4 aṭikaḷik koṇṭa pāviṉam maṇṭilam eṉṟum, attokikku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷi yuṭiya 4 aṭikaḷik koṇṭa pāviṉam tāṇṭakam eṉṟum peyarpeṟum.  maṇṭila (virutta) eḻuttaḷavit tāṇṭic celvatu tāṇṭakam. tirunāvukkaracar tāṇṭakaṅkaḷuḷ māperumpālaṉa maṇṭila aḷavē koṇṭiruppatāl,
mūviraṇ ṭēṉum irunāḷ kēṉum
cīrvaki nāṭṭic ceyyuḷiṉ āṭavar
kaṭavuḷarp pukaḻvaṉa tāṇṭakam avaṟṟaḷ
aṟucīr kuṟiyatu, neṭiyateṇ cīrām.
eṉṉum paṉṉiru pāṭṭiyal nūṟpāvāl (195) amikkappaṭum.  (ta.i.va. 33:74).

tāmari
tāmari - tāmarasa
tum = cem. tum - tumpu - tuppu = civappu, pavaḻam, arakku.
tumpu - tumparam = civappu, civappāṉa attippaḻam.
tum - (tumar) - tuvar = civappu, pavaḻam, kāvi, tuvari, tuvarppu (kācukkaṭṭi). tuvarttal = civattal, tuvarppuc cuviyātal.
tuvar - tuvari = cempayaṟu, ceppukkōṭṭi nakar.
tuvar - tukir = pavaḻam
tumar - tamar - tāmaram = cempu. tāmaram - tāmari = cemmuḷari, muḷari.
tāmarasa eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭivit tāma+rasa eṉṟu pakuttup pakal muḷari eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar.  āyiṉ, tāma eṉṉuñ coṟku mūlamākak koḷḷum tama eṉṉuñ coṟkō iruḷ allatu iravu eṉṟutāṉ poruḷ.  iṉi, ikkuḻaṟupaṭiyip perukkaṟkut tam eṉpati aṭi mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar.  ataṟkut tiṇaṟutal allatu tikkumuk kāṭutal eṉpatu poruḷ iṅṅaṉam vaṭavar tiṇaṟit tiṇṭāṭuvatellām, vēṇṭumeṉṟē meymmiyip puṟakkaṇittup poymmiyi mēṟkoḷvataṉ viḷivē.
tāmaram - tāmpara 
tumparam -utumpara, uṭumpara (a.vē.).  (va.va:176).
tāmari: tum - tumar - tuvar = civappu, tuvar - tuvari = cempayaṟu. tumar - tamar (tāmari) - tāmari = cenniṟa nīrppūvaki.  iccol iṉṟu taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiḻantu, avvakiyiṉ iruniṟa malarkkum potuppeyarāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.  tāmari - tāmarasa(va.)   (ti.ma:743).
tāymoḻik karaṇam
tēva moḻiyeṉa oṉṟiṉmiyālum, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku muntiyatum āriya moḻikaṭku mūlamum āṉatiṉālum, āriyak karaṇam tamiḻaṉukku māṉakkēṭṭi viḷippataṉālum, pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu varumuṉ ellāt tamiḻar maṇaṅkaḷum tamiḻilēyē naṭipeṟṟu vantatiṉālum, ovvoru tamiḻaṉum tamiḻilēyē karaṇam ceyvittal vēṇṭum. pirāmaṇaṉ tamiḻaṉpaṉākit tamiḻiṟ karaṇam ceyya iciyiṉ ati ēṟṟuk koḷḷalām.  tamiḻiṟ karaṇam ceyviyātavaṉ tamiḻaṉākāṉ.
ovvoru tūya tamiḻaṉum, taṉ vīṭṭuk karaṇatti maṭṭu maṉṟip piṟar vīṭṭuk karaṇattiyum tamiḻiṟ ceyvikka muyaṟci ceytal vēṇṭum.  vaṭamoḻiyil naṭakkum karaṇattiṟkuc cellutal kūṭātu.  (ta.ti.57).
tāyam viḷiyāṭṭu
itu palavakippaṭum.  avaṟṟuḷ peruvaḻakkāṉatu patiṉintu nāyum puliyum eṉpatām.  itu tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē āṭappaṭṭateṉpatu,
 valeṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē  (tol. 373)
nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli
āvayiṉ ukaraṅ keṭutalu murittē
ukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi akaram niliyum  (. 374)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāl (cūttiraṅkaḷāl) aṟiyalām.
paramapatam, ciluvittāyam eṉa vaḻaṅkum kuṟukkuk kaṭṭat tāyam, ākiya iraṇṭum, perumpāṉmiyāka vaḻaṅkum peṇṭir tāya viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷām.
tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam tamiḻaratē
oru kātalaṉ taṉ kātalikku, allatu oru maṇamakaṉ taṉ maṇamakaḷukku, allatu kaṇavaṉ taṉ maṉivikku, kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvataṉ vāyilāy, avaḷit taṉ vāḻkkittuṇivi eṉṟu piṟarkkuk kāṭṭuvatu, tamiḻakattut toṉṟutoṭṭa vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
kaṭṭutal eṉṉum col maṇattal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu, tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam paṟṟiyē. mutaṟkālattil, makkaḷ kuṭumpappiriviṉṟik kulaṅkulamāy allatu tokuti tokutiyāy vāḻntuvanta niliyil, oru kulattup peṇṭir, perumpālum kulattalivaṉ maṉiviyar nīṅkalāka, akkulattu āṭavar aṉivarkkum potu maṉivi yarākavē iruntu vantaṉar. piṉpu nākarikan tōṉṟit taṉimaṉi viyar ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, oruvaṉ taṉ maṉiviyi allatu maṉivi yari vēṟākap pirittu vittaṟku tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu.  īkiyariya iḻiyaṇi makaḷir eṉpatu  (puṟam 127), tamiḻappeṇṭir tāliyaṇiyum vaḻakkattik kuṟikkum caṅkanūṟ cāṉṟām.
muntukālattil, ciṟappākak kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻntu vantapōtu, iliyiyum pūviyum āṭiyaṇiyāka aṇivatu tamiḻar vaḻakkam.  nālvakiyiliyuḷḷum ōliyeṉpatu uṟutiyāṉatātaliṉ, paṉaṅkuruttāl kātaṇi kaḻuttaṇi mutaliya palvēṟu aṇikaḷi avar ceytu koṇṭaṉar.  cēraṉ paṉam pūmāliyi aṭiyāḷa māliyākak koṇṭirunta tiṉālum, ōliyik kuṟikkum tōṭu eṉṉum col iṉṟum kātaṇippeyarāy vaḻaṅkutalālum, kātil ōliyaṇivatāl ōlippaḷḷi yeṉṟa peyar peṟṟa oru paḷḷi vakuppār  iṉṟiruttalālum, tāḻttappaṭṭa cila kulattup peṇṭir ōliyiyē kātil aṇivatālum, paḻaṅkālattil ōliyaṇi kaḷik kātilum kaḻuttilum tamiḻap peṇṭir aṇintaṉar eṉpatil, eḷḷaḷavum viyappiṟkiṭamiṉṟām.  kātiṟpōṉṟē kaḻuttilum paṉaṅkuruttaṇi aṇiyappaṭṭami.

ceyattaku kōvi
eṉṉum cilappatikārat toṭaril (6:101-2), kōvi eṉpataṟkup piṉṟāli yeṉṟu arumpatavurikārarum aṭiyārkku nallārum urittiruppatāl, akkālattu muṉṟāli piṉṟāliyeṉa iruvakiyaṇi yiruntamiyum peṟappaṭum.  muṉṟāli mārpil toṅkuvatu; piṉṟāli mutukil toṅkuvatu.  ivaṟṟāl, tāli eṉa oru taṉiyaṇi muṟkālattiruntamiyum, piṉpu eḻunta ataṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷ avaṟṟiṟkēṟpa vevvēṟu aṭiyaṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭamiyum, peṟappaṭum.
tāli eṉa oru kuṟippiṭṭa maṅkalaaṇi piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya piṉ, atu ovvoru kulattiṟkum oṉṟāka vevveṟu vaṭivilum aḷavilum ceyyappaṭṭu atataṟkēṟpap peyarum peṟṟatu.  āmittāli, poṭṭuttāli, ciṟutāli, peruntāli eṉpaṉa cila peyarkaḷ.
tālipōṉṟē tālikkoṭiyum maṅkalamākak karutappaṭṭu, maṅkalanāṇ eṉappeṟṟatu.  atu mutaṟkaṇ mañcaḷ tōytta nūṟkayiṟāyiruntatiṉāl, cila kulattār iṉṉum ataṉ paḻaniliyi marapākap pōṟṟikkāppār. cila kulattār atippoṟkoṭiyākac ceytukoḷvar. iṉi, maṅkala nāṇiyē tāliyākak koṇṭārum uṇṭu.
tāliyiyum, taṉiyāka aṇivatum vēṟu cila urukkaḷuṭaṉ cērttu aṇivatumāka, iruvēṟu marapukaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa. tāliyiyē aṇiyāka mutu paṇṭi niliyil, iṉṟum pala anākarikat tiraviṭa marapiṉar uḷar.  koṅku nāṭṭu vēḷāḷap peṇṭir kaṉatta tāliyi aṇivatāl, ciṟappu nāṭkaḷil ati aṇivatum piṟanāṭkaḷiṟ kaḻaṟṟi vittiruppatum avar vaḻakkam.
tālikaṭṭuvatu āriyar vaḻakkamaṉṟeṉpatu, piṉvarum (P.T.) cīnivāca aiyaṅkār kūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
tirumaṇac caṭaṅkukaḷil, talimiyāṉatākat tāliyaṇivatum, mañcaḷ tōytta nāṇik kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭuvatum, pōṉṟa cila aṉāriya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, teṟkattup pirāmaṇappeṇṭirkku arumiyāka uḷḷaṉa - tamiḻar varalāṟu (History of the Tamils) pak.57.
itu (tāliyaṇivatu) kirukiya cūttiraṅkaḷilēyē collap paṭāta oru tūya tamiḻa vaḻakkam. kiyippiṭikkum pāṇik kirakaṇattiyum ēḻaṭiyiṭum capta patīyiyumē, tirumaṇac caṭaṅkiṉ  uyirnāṭip pakutikāḷakak kirukiya cūttiraṅkaḷ koḷkiṉṟaṉa. mēṟpaṭi, aṭikkuṟippu.

kaḻuttilaṇiyum tāliyiṉum kiyilaṇiyum mōtiramē kaṇṇi yamum nākarikamum vāyntateṉiṉ, kottuk kottāyp poṟṟōṭariyum, motta mottamāyp paṉmaṇimāliyum kaḻuttilaṇiyumpōtu, tālikkēṉ iṭamilliyeṉa etir viṉavi viṭukka.
kuṟippu:- tamiḻarellārum ōriṉamāyiṉum, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅ kaḷil vēṟupaṭṭa palvēṟu tamiḻak kulaṅkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu varuvatālum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē cila tamiḻakkulaṅkaḷ cila āriya vaḻakkaṅkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu viṭṭataṉālum, itupōtuḷḷa ōriru cāṉṟukaḷimaṭṭuṅ koṇṭu, kaḻakakkālattil tāli oru kulattārkkum maṅkala vaṇiyākavilli eṉṟu muḻuvuṟutiyāyk kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu.  āyiṉ, atu mutaṟkālattil aḻaku allatu kāppup paṟṟiya potuvaṇiyākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉpatum, atu tamiḻar aṇiyē eṉpatum teḷḷat teḷivām.

ticiccol evi?
tolkāppiyar tam kālat tamiḻc ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷi iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol, vaṭacolleṉa nāl vakippaṭuttiṉār.  avaṟṟuḷ, iyaṟcollum, tiri collum centamiḻ nāṭṭuc collum, ticic col koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭuc collum ākum. vaṭa col eṉṟatu āriyac col liyum vaṭa nāṭṭuc colliyum ākum.  vaṭanāṭṭuc col lāvatu, pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭa tiraviṭac col. aḵtu āriyattiṟku muntiyatu.
akkālattut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya allatu pukuttappaṭṭa ayaṉ moḻic col āriyac col oṉṟē. ataṉāl atit tici paṟṟi vaṭacol eṉṟu pirittuk kūṟiṉar. avvāṟē iklattu ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyum, āṅkilac col, ilattīṉac col, kirēkkac col, cīṉaccol eṉa avvam moḻip peyarālēyē kuṟittal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟit ticic colluḷ aṭakkutal kūṭātu.
mēṟkuṟitta nālvakic collum paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ varumāṟu:

ṟaṉittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē.  (tol. ecca.1)
